### [1] [The facts on quantum self-dual Einstein gravity and self-dual Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26242)
*Noah Miller*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于自对偶爱因斯坦引力（self-dual Einstein gravity, SDG）和自对偶杨-米尔斯理论（self-dual Yang-Mills, SDYM）的教学性综述论文。SDG是一种四维量子引力理论，具有单圈精确性、在紫外（UV）和红外（IR）有限、微分同胚不变但非么正等特点，其唯一非平凡振幅是单圈全加（one-loop-all-plus）振幅，且与纯爱因斯坦引力的振幅相等。论文系统梳理了从完整作用量到自对偶作用量的推导、相关计算技术以及天体全息（celestial holography）等领域的关键结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对偶引力是一种具有特殊性质的量子引力模型：单圈精确且UV和IR有限，其非平凡散射振幅与纯爱因斯坦引力的结果一致，这使其成为研究量子引力结构的有力理论工具。尽管已有大量文献，但在推导细节、计算步骤等方面仍存在若干空白，文章旨在填补这些文献缺口，并指出未来研究的方向。

Method: 论文采用教学式综述方法，系统梳理并推导了自对偶作用量从全爱因斯坦引力/杨-米尔斯作用量通过光锥规范（lightcone gauge）的约化过程，涵盖鬼场消除（decoupling of ghosts）、Berends-Giele流、树级一负振幅（tree-level-one-minus amplitudes）的消零、天体$Lw_{1+\infty}$对称性、微扰子（perturbiners）、双离壳流（double-off-shell currents）计算、维度移位（dimension shifting）、幺正割（unitarity cuts）、BDPR引力公式、单圈色序以及协变形式化与规范固定等内容。

Result: 文章表明：SDG是一种单圈精确、UV和IR有限的非么正量子引力理论，其唯一非平凡振幅（单圈全加振幅）与纯爱因斯坦引力的对应振幅相等；SDG和SDYM之间在计算框架上存在深层联系；此外，论文还回顾了天体全息程序中的若干精选结果，并对单圈一负（one-loop-one-minus）引力振幅的全n表达式作了简要评述。

Conclusion: 自对偶理论（SDG与SDYM）为研究量子引力和规范理论的结构提供了独特的理论视角，其计算框架和对称性分析对未来研究具有重要参考价值。文章填补了部分文献空白，同时指出若干可供深入探索的未来研究方向，包括单圈一负振幅的完整表达式等相关问题。

Abstract: Self-dual Einstein gravity is a one-loop exact, UV and IR finite, diffeomorphism invariant, non-unitary theory of quantum gravity in four dimensions. The only non-trivial amplitudes are the one-loop-all-plus amplitudes, and they are equal to those of pure Einstein gravity.
  In these notes we review SDG and SDYM pedagogically. We walk the reader through key calculations, fill in some gaps in the literature, and point out directions for future research. Topics include: derivation of the self-dual actions from the full Einstein gravity/YM actions in lightcone gauge, decoupling of ghosts, Berends-Giele currents, vanishing of tree-level-one-minus amplitudes, celestial $Lw_{1+\infty}$ symmetry, perturbiners, double-off-shell current calculation of one-loop-all-plus SDYM and SDG amplitudes, dimension shifting, unitarity cuts, the BDPR gravity formula, one-loop color ordering, and the covariant formulations of the self-dual theories as well as their their gauge fixing. We also review some selected results from the celestial holography program, and briefly comment on an all-n expression for the one-loop-one-minus gravity amplitudes.

</details>


### [2] [Renormalization in Liouville gravity and stochastic inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26244)
*Jordan Cotler,Victor Ivo,Juan Maldacena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究由具有对数相关性（logarithmic correlations）的随机高斯场（random Gaussian field）ζ(x) 所刻画的 d 维随机几何体系的体积元 e^{dζ} 重整化问题，并建立了暴胀（inflation）理论与刘维尔理论（Liouville theory）之间的联系，指出体积元重整化失效的临界点对应永恒暴胀（eternal inflation）的相变转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机源于暴胀理论与刘维尔理论，旨在建立两类看似不同的物理框架之间的深层次联系。作者希望从固定物理截断出发、通过粗粒化操作直接推导 KPZ 标度关系，并揭示随机高斯场刻画下随机几何的重整化行为与永恒暴胀转变之间的对应关系。

Method: 作者假设随机 d 维几何的整体尺度因子为 ds² = e^{2ζ}dx²，其中 ζ(x) 为具有对数相关性的随机高斯场。通过从固定物理截断出发，利用平坦度量 dx² 定义的固定基准截断对算子进行粗粒化（coarse-graining），对体积元 e^{dζ} 进行重整化分析，从而直接给出 KPZ 标度关系的物理论证。

Result: 本文给出了 KPZ 标度关系的直接物理论证，并指出同样的重整化问题在随机暴胀（stochastic inflation）中亦出现，表现为暴胀子场的布朗运动模型。研究发现，当涨落振幅超过临界值时，刘维尔体积元的重整化失效，这一现象对应于向永恒暴胀的转变；在 d = 2 维情形下，此临界值恰好匹配刘维尔引力中熟悉的 c_m = 1 屏障。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了暴胀理论与刘维尔理论在随机几何体积元重整化上的统一性，证明了体积元重整化的失效与永恒暴胀转变以及刘维尔引力中的 c_m = 1 屏障存在等价关系。此外，作者指出该结果与随机表面的数学概率方法之间存在关联，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Motivated by inflation and Liouville theory, we consider random $d$-dimensional geometries characterized by an overall scale factor $ds^2 = e^{2 ζ} dx^2 $ given in terms of a random Gaussian field $ζ(x)$ with logarithmic correlations. We discuss aspects of the renormalization of the volume element $e^{d ζ}$, connecting well-known Liouville theory formulas (KPZ) and inflationary ones. By starting from a fixed physical cutoff and coarse-graining operators to a fixed fiducial cutoff, defined via the flat metric $dx^2$, we provide a direct physical derivation of the KPZ scaling relation. We point out that the same renormalization problem arises in stochastic inflation, where it is modeled by Brownian motion of the inflaton field. We show that the breakdown of the renormalization of the Liouville volume when the fluctuation amplitude exceeds a critical value corresponds to the transition to eternal inflation. In $d = 2$, this matches the familiar $c_m = 1$ barrier in Liouville gravity. We also discuss connections to mathematical probabilistic approaches to random surfaces.

</details>


### [3] [Positivity in energy correlators and the event distribution formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26246)
*Alexandre Belin,Nathan Borak,Johan Henriksson,Romain Piron,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了能量关联子（energy correlators）最一般的无穷层次正性问题解由事件分布公式（event distribution formula）给出，即能量关联子可表示为天球上概率测度空间中测度的矩（moments）。针对两点和三点能量关联子推导了非平凡的双侧界，且由无穷层次一致性得到的界严格强于仅对两、三点关联子施加正性得到的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量关联子是广泛适用于多种理论（从规范理论、共形场论到弦论）和时空维度的普适观测量，其受正性约束和能量守恒约束的严格数学结构此前未被系统阐明。本文旨在解决能量关联子无穷层次正性问题，并将所得约束应用于碰撞物理和共形场论。

Method: 作者论证能量关联子受三类基本性质约束：能量通量的逐点正性（pointwise positivity）、幺正理论中态范数所赋予的全局正性（global positivity）以及能量守恒所组织的无穷一致层次结构。在此基础上提出事件分布公式，将能量关联子表示为天球上概率测度之测度的矩，并针对两点和三点关联子详细推导其对多极展开系数的双侧界。

Result: 作者证明了无穷层次一致性约束给出的界严格强于仅依靠两、三点关联子正性得到的界；对于论文中研究的低自旋多极系数（low-spin multipole coefficients），所得界是最优的，其极值可由有限粒子事件的有限混合实现。该正性约束的后果在碰撞物理和共形场论中得到了进一步展示。

Conclusion: 能量关联子无穷层次的正性问题被完整求解，事件分布公式构成其最一般解；正性约束为能量关联子的多极展开提供了可检验、且最优的双侧界，并在高能碰撞物理和共形场论中具有直接可观测的物理后果。

Abstract: Energy correlators are universal observables, well defined across a wide range of theories and spacetime dimensions, from gauge theory and conformal field theory to string theory. Energy correlators are constrained by three fundamental properties: pointwise positivity of the energy flux, global positivity originating from their interpretation as state norms in a unitary theory, and energy conservation organizing multi-point energy correlators into an infinite consistent hierarchy. We argue that the most general solution to the infinite hierarchy positivity problem of energy correlators is given by the event distribution formula, which expresses energy correlators as moments of the measure on the space of probability measures on the celestial sphere. We work out in detail implications of positivity for two- and three-point energy correlators and show that it implies nontrivial two-sided bounds on their multipole expansion coefficients. The bounds obtained by requiring consistency of the infinite hierarchy of energy correlators are strictly stronger than those obtained by imposing positivity of the two- and three-point correlators alone. For the low-spin multipole coefficients studied in the paper, the derived bounds are optimal in the sense that their extrema are realized by finite mixtures of finite-particle events. We further demonstrate consequences of positivity in energy correlators in collider physics and conformal field theories.

</details>


### [4] [Why Cooper pairs live in AdS2: a spectral analysis of the Yukawa-SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26251)
*Veronika Carolin Stangier,Jörg Schmalian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Yukawa–Sachdev–Ye–Kitaev模型中建立了Cooper配对几何表述的谱学基础，通过解析求解高斯涨落核的本征值，证明超导不稳定性与普适低能涨落完全位于连续散射谱扇区，并成功将双局域Cooper对场投影映射到AdS₂中的标量物质场，为早期全息表述中的投影假设提供了微观论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期全息表述中存在对双局域配对理论的隐式谱投影，但缺乏微观层面的严格论证。本文旨在通过谱学分析为Yukawa–SYK模型中Cooper配对的几何（de Sitter/AdS）表述建立严格基础，阐明局域体场如何从双局域配对理论中涌现。

Method: 从大N双局域有效作用量出发，推导偶频自旋单态Cooper通道中的高斯涨落核；遵循Maldacena–Stanford构造，对任意共形权重h解析确定核本征值k(h)，并将配对涨落分解为de Sitter空间拉普拉斯算子的连续谱与离散谱扇区；随后在最低连续模附近展开核，得到限制在连续谱子空间上的Klein–Gordon作用量，并利用该子空间上良定义的逆Radon变换将投影后的Cooper对场映射至AdS₂。

Result: 超导不稳定性及其附近的普适低能涨落完全位于连续散射扇区，离散模保持非临界性质。最低连续模附近的展开给出dS₂上的Klein–Gordon作用量，该作用量限制在连续谱子空间上——恰是逆Radon变换至AdS₂良定义的子空间。由此投影的双局域Cooper对场可直接映射为AdS₂中的标量物质场，无需对体理论做进一步谱限制。

Conclusion: 本文结果为早期全息表述中隐含的谱投影提供了缺失的微观论证，澄清了局域体场如何从双局域配对理论中涌现，从而夯实了Cooper配对几何化表述的谱学基础。

Abstract: We establish the spectral foundation of the geometric formulation of Cooper pairing in the Yukawa--Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model. Starting from the large-$N$ bilocal effective action, we derive the Gaussian fluctuation kernel in the even-frequency spin-singlet Cooper channel. Following the construction of Maldacena and Stanford, we determine the kernel eigenvalue $k\left(h\right)$ analytically for arbitrary conformal weight $h$ and resolve the pairing fluctuations into the continuous and discrete sectors of the associated de-Sitter space Laplacian. We show that the superconducting instability and the universal low-energy fluctuations near it reside entirely in the continuous scattering sector, while the discrete modes remain non-critical. Expanding the kernel about the lowest continuum mode yields a Klein--Gordon action on ${\rm dS}_2$, restricted to the continuous spectral subspace. This is precisely the subspace on which the inverse Radon transform to ${\rm AdS}_2$ is well defined. The projected bilocal Cooper-pair field can therefore be mapped onto a scalar matter field propagating in ${\rm AdS}_2$, without requiring any further spectral restriction on the bulk theory. Our results provide the missing microscopic justification for the projection implicit in earlier holographic formulations and clarify how a local bulk field emerges from the bilocal pairing theory.

</details>


### [5] [Inner products and closed universes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26253)
*Jordan Cotler,Kristan Jensen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了半经典德西特量子引力（semiclassical de Sitter quantum gravity）中晚期态的重叠。在德西特 JT 引力变形族中，作者计算了无边界波函数到一圈阶（one-loop），并导出了超局域晚期测度（ultralocal late-time measure）。关键发现是单宇宙贡献的范数恰好是球面振幅（sphere amplitude）的八倍，洛伦兹测度中的配对与欧几里得引力中的配对存在差异，且无边界范数指数化为 exp(8Z_sphere) 而非 exp(Z_sphere)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解半经典德西特量子引力中晚期态（late-time states）的重叠结构，并厘清洛伦兹测度（Lorentzian measure）与欧几里得引力（Euclidean gravity）在配对方式上的根本差异，这对研究微扰引力中的切割与拼接（cutting and gluing）具有重要启示。

Method: 在德西特 JT 引力（de Sitter JT gravity）的若干变形中，计算无边界波函数（no-boundary wavefunction）至一圈阶（one-loop），并推导超局域晚期测度。通过对不相连的最终宇宙求和，使无边界范数指数化单宇宙结果。同时回顾正宇宙学常数爱因斯坦引力（Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant）中的类似失配现象。

Result: 单宇宙贡献的范数恰为球面振幅的八倍，即洛伦兹测度中的配对与欧几里得引力隐含的配对不同。对不相连最终宇宙求和后，无边界范数满足 ⟨⟨HH|HH⟩⟩ ≈ exp(8Z_sphere)，而闭合几何求和给出 Z_closed ≈ exp(Z_sphere)。此外，正宇宙学常数爱因斯坦引力中晚期无边界范数在一圈阶消失，与欧几里得球面振幅不同。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了这一失配现象对微扰引力中切割与拼接的影响，以及将该构造推广到拓扑求和（sums over topologies）时出现的特征，为深入理解半经典德西特量子引力的测度结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We study overlaps of late-time states in semiclassical de Sitter quantum gravity. In a family of deformations of de Sitter JT gravity, we compute the no-boundary wavefunction to one-loop and derive the ultralocal late-time measure. The one-universe contribution to the norm is exactly eight times the sphere amplitude, so the pairing used by the Lorentzian measure differs from that implicit in Euclidean gravity. After summing over disconnected final universes, the no-boundary norm exponentiates the one-universe result, giving $\langle \!\langle \text{HH}|\text{HH}\rangle\!\rangle \approx \exp(8Z_{\rm sphere})$ rather than the sum over closed geometries, $Z_{\rm closed}\approx \exp(Z_{\rm sphere})$. We also review an analogous mismatch in Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant, where the late-time no-boundary norm vanishes at one-loop, differing from the Euclidean sphere amplitude. We discuss the implications for cutting and gluing in perturbative gravity, and features that arise when extending this construction to sums over topologies.

</details>


### [6] [Contact Structure-induced Symplectic Nearly Half-Flat $SU(3)$ Structure in Finite-$N$ Thermal ${\cal M}$-Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26267)
*Aalok Misra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明arXiv:1306.4339等构造的热QCD类理论的M理论对偶中所含的$G_2$-结构七流形$\mathcal{M}_7$上的横向$SU(3)$-结构属于辛近半平坦（symplectic nearly half-flat, S(nearly)HF）类，并利用该结构数据通过Kaluza-Klein约化得到径向"磁场"$\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$，为有限温度弦论中弱磁场的产生提供了自上而下的全息机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（arXiv:1507.02692, 2004.07259, 2211.13186）已对MQGP对偶的$G$-结构分类进行了开创性研究，但诱导出的横向$SU(3)$-结构的具体Chiossi-Salamon挠率类别尚未完全确定，本文旨在完成这一分类并探索其物理应用。

Method: 作者通过直接计算证明诱导$SU(3)$-结构满足$d\omega_\Phi=0$精确成立，而$d\Omega_+$在红外MQGP极限下被抑制为$\mathcal{O}(e^{-N^{1/3}/\mathcal{O}(1)})$量级，从而确定其属于S(nearly)HF类，仅剩$W_\Phi^{SU(3)}=W_2^-$一项非零挠率。随后利用$G_2$/S(nearly)HF挠率数据对十一维Hodge对偶施行Kaluza-Klein约化，推导出径向磁场$\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$及其满足的Bessel型常微分方程。

Result: $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$的平方范数可精确分解为外部因子与内部因子的乘积；该磁场满足具有正则奇点的Bessel型ODE，正则性条件选取$J_2$解；内部因子的调和性由大$N$抑制恢复；$\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}$与类型IIA味$D6$-膜磁场通过非线性幂律关联，从而为弱磁场产生提供了全息机制。

Conclusion: 本文完成了MQGP对偶的$G$-结构分类，证明其属于S(nearly)HF类，并通过全息对应给出了有限温度弦论中产生弱磁场的自上而下机制，为该方向的理论框架提供了完整且自洽的结构基础。

Abstract: The ${\cal M}$-theory dual of thermal QCD-like theories constructed in arXiv:1306.4339, 2004.07259 involves, as shown in arXiv:2211.13186, a closed $G_2$-structure seven-fold $\mathcal{M}_7$ supporting Contact 3-Structures (C3S) that induce a ``transverse'' $SU(3)$-structure. We prove that this induced structure belongs to the symplectic nearly half-flat (S(nearly)HF) class: $dω_Φ=0$ exactly, while $dΩ_+$ is suppressed as $\mathcal{O}(e^{-N^{1/3}/\mathcal{O}(1)})$ in the infrared "MQGP limit", leaving $W_Φ^{SU(3)}=W_2^-$ as the sole non-vanishing Chiossi--Salamon torsion class and completing the $G$-structure classification of the MQGP dual initiated in arXiv:1507.02692,2004.07259, 2211.13186. As a physical application, Kaluza--Klein reduction of the eleven-dimensional Hodge dual of the differential of the $G_2$-structure positive three-form on $\mathcal{M}_7$, organised through the $G_2$/S(nearly)HF torsion data, yields a radial ``magnetic field'' $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ whose squared norm factorises exactly into external and internal factors. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ satisfies a Bessel-type ODE with a regular singular point at the horizon; normalisability and infrared regularity select $J_2$, with harmonicity of the aforementioned internal factor recovered from the large-$N$ suppression. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}$ is related to the magnetic field of the type~IIA flavour $D6$-branes via a non-linear power law, furnishing a top-down holographic mechanism for generating weak magnetic fields in finite-temperature string theory.

</details>

### [19] [The off-diagonal low rank property: new opportunities for low-scaling computational chemistry methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26824)
*Zikuan Wang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇Perspective综述，指出计算化学中的许多重要矩阵（如库仑矩阵、电子排斥积分张量、密度矩阵、局域分子轨道系数矩阵、Fock矩阵和核Hessian矩阵）在基函数适当排序时具有"非对角低秩"（off-diagonal low rank, ODLR）性质，并回顾了数学界与计算化学界对该性质的研究，首次证明Fock矩阵和局域分子轨道系数矩阵即使在无带隙（gapless）系统中也满足ODLR性质，为无带隙系统的线性标度电子结构计算铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算化学中许多矩阵既非稀疏也非低秩，使得设计低标度算法非常困难；作者希望找到这些矩阵的共性结构特征，以推动新的线性标度方法的发展。

Method: 作者以Perspective形式系统梳理了数学界关于ODLR性质的现有文献（包括矩阵的紧凑存储、高效计算与使用），随后回顾了该性质在计算化学中的应用，并首次严格证明了Fock矩阵与局域分子轨道系数矩阵在无带隙系统中仍满足ODLR性质。

Result: 证明即使在系统无带隙（此时矩阵为稠密矩阵）的情况下，Fock矩阵和局域分子轨道系数矩阵依然满足ODLR性质，这为此前被认为不适用于低标度算法的稠密全秩矩阵开辟了利用ODLR性质设计新方法的可能。

Conclusion: ODLR性质是计算化学中多种重要矩阵共有的关键结构，有望成为设计面向稠密、全秩矩阵的新型低标度算法的基础，特别是在零电子温度下实现无带隙系统的线性标度电子结构计算。

Abstract: Many matrices in computational chemistry are neither sparse nor low-rank, making the design of low-scaling algorithms difficult. In this Perspective, we point out that many important matrices in computational chemistry, such as the Coulomb matrix, the electronic repulsion integral tensor, the density matrix, the localized molecular orbital (LMO) coefficient matrix, the Fock matrix, and the nuclear Hessian matrix share the same property: when their basis functions are suitably ordered, their off-diagonal blocks have low numerical ranks (despite that they as a whole have high numerical ranks). This property, termed off-diagonal low rank (ODLR), has been extensively studied in the mathematics community, but has surprisingly found very little use in computational chemistry. This Perspective reviews the existing mathematical literature on how to use the ODLR property of matrices to compactly store, as well as efficiently calculate or use them. Subsequently, we review the use of the ODLR property in computational chemistry, and point out possible future opportunities of devising new low-scaling methods for dense, full-rank matrices, exploiting the ODLR property. In particular, we prove for the first time that Fock matrices and LMO coefficient matrices satisfy the ODLR property, even if the system is gapless (in which case the matrices are dense). This paves the way to linear scaling electronic structure calculations of gapless systems at zero electronic temperature.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子结构计算领域需要集成多种先进相关方法的开源软件工具，尤其是包含较新的可区分簇方法及其张量分解实现；同时，现有软件包在用户友好性和方法可扩展性方面仍有改进空间。因此开发一个兼具高性能、易用性和开放接口的 Julia 软件包，以支持常规计算和前沿方法探索。

Result: 该工作将 EOM-DCSD（含 EOM-CCSD）在 QUEST3 基准集上对标 CC3 进行了基准测试。文中还详细综述了 ElemCo.jl 的方法学储备、技术细节与实现、性能、接口及用法。

Conclusion: ElemCo.jl 作为一个功能全面且开放扩展的电子结构计算软件包，既能满足常规使用需求，也为高级用户提供了独特的先进方法（如张量分解 DC 方法）和灵活的二次开发接口，适用于从基态到激发态、从单参考到多参考体系的广泛量子化学计算场景。

Abstract: We present ElemCo.jl, an open-source Julia package for molecular electronic structure and properties calculations with a particular emphasis on Coupled Cluster and Distinguishable Cluster methods. The package provides a high-level, macro-based user interface which makes routine calculations accessible to users with no prior Julia experience. A newly developed visualizer, JLmol, assists in the graphical preparation of ElemCo.jl input files and displays results such as molecular orbitals. ElemCo.jl offers restricted closed-shell and unrestricted variants of state-of-the-art electron-correlation methods such as FCI, MP2, CCSD(T) as well as EOM-CCSD and the DC methods DCSD and DC-CCSDT. In addition to traditional approaches, ElemCo.jl provides recently developed methods that are currently unique to the package. These include tensor-decomposed implementations of DCSD and DC-CCSDT (SVD-DCSD and SVD-DC-CCSDT) as well as two-determinant and fixed-reference CC and DC methods. For excited-state calculations, ElemCo.jl also offers EOM-DCSD, which is benchmarked in this work against CC3 on the QUEST3 benchmark set, alongside EOM-CCSD. Furthermore, both ground and excited states, including those of multireference character, can be treated using CIPHI - an efficient selected Configuration Interaction (CI) approach employing a CIPSI/Heat-Bath-CI-based algorithm. Users can directly invoke internal functions from the input file, enabling them to test and compose new methods without the need to modify ElemCo.jl's source code. Also, ElemCo.jl can be readily interfaced with external quantum chemistry codes through the Fcidump format, for example as the high-level solver for transcorrelated Hamiltonians, periodic embedded fragments, etc. We provide a detailed overview of ElemCo.jl's methodological repertoire and discuss its technical details and implementation, performance, interfaces, and usage.

</details>

Method: 作者对架构和目标函数相同、训练精度相同的循环Transformer网络进行分析，依据初始化条件的不同将其解分类为不同的动力学相；通过分岔机制（鞍结分岔与Neimark-Sacker型转变）区分各相，并利用一维正规形约化（normal-form reduction）从训练映射的局部导数推导弛豫时间与谱隙（spectral gap）的幅度关系。

Result: 在鞍结分岔相，得到参数无关关系τ(ε)[1-λ_max(-ε)]→π；结合问题难度（problem difficulty）的规则分布，产生工作量级尾分布P(τ>N)~N^{-2}。在Neimark-Sacker相，该标度律完全消失而不仅是改变前置系数。结论是测试时计算量不由架构单独决定，而是由训练所得解的动力学相所支配。

Conclusion: 测试时计算量的标度行为由训练找到的解所处的动力学相决定，而非仅由网络架构决定。初始化显著影响所到达的动力学相，进而决定推理代价的标度规律，为理解推理成本提供了动力学视角的理论框架。

The phases are distinguished by their bifurcation mechanisms, including a saddle-node fold and a Neimark-Sacker-type transition to bounded nonstationary motion. In the fold phase, a one-dimensional normal-form reduction predicts both the relaxation-time and spectral-gap amplitudes from local derivatives of the trained map, yielding the parameter-free relation $τ(\varepsilon)[1-λ_{\max}(-\varepsilon)]\toπ$. Composed with a regular distribution of problem difficulty, the same critical slowing down produces the workload-level tail $P(τ>N)\sim N^{-2}$. In the Neimark--Sacker phase, the fold scaling law disappears rather than merely changing its prefactor. Thus, test-time compute is not determined by architecture alone. It is governed by the dynamical phase of the solution found by training.

</details>

Conclusion: 学习到的潜在表征与重整化群（renormalization group）结构的一致性（即临界涨落与尺度流）是模型获得优异泛化性能的核心因素。

Trained only on small lattices, the model extrapolates to substantially larger systems, recovers the spanning cluster with high fidelity, and produces observables obeying the expected finite-size scaling near the critical point. Conclusion: LCYBO被确立为一种结构无序的阻挫三角形晶格磁体，其低能自旋动力学具有关联性，无序与几何阻挫的耦合在塑造该体系的非常规磁基态中起关键作用。

Abstract: The interplay between geometric frustration, spin--orbit coupling, and structural disorder can give rise to unconventional magnetic ground states in rare-earth triangular-lattice magnets. We report low-temperature magnetic and $^7$Li NMR investigations of the disordered triangular-lattice compound LiCaYb$_5$(BO$_3$)$_6$ (LCYBO). Rietveld refinement confirms a hexagonal $P6_522$ structure with partial Ca/Yb antisite disorder and fractional Li occupancy. Magnetic susceptibility and magnetization measurements indicate a well-isolated effective $J_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ Kramers doublet with weak antiferromagnetic interactions ($θ_{\mathrm{CW}} \approx -0.54$~K). The specific heat reveals a weak anomaly near 0.43 K, suggesting the development of short-range correlated magnetism rather than conventional long-range order. $^7$Li NMR spectra broaden strongly upon cooling, consistent with increasingly inhomogeneous internal magnetic fields. The spin-lattice relaxation exhibits two relaxation components consistent with disorder-induced distributions of local magnetic environments arising from antisite disorder and competing exchange pathways. Our results establish LCYBO as a structurally disordered frustrated triangular-lattice magnet with correlated low-energy spin dynamics.

</details>

### [33] [When chemical potential continuity fails: kinetic interface models for hydrogen isotope transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26231)
*Remi Delaporte-Mathurin,James Dark*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文指出宏观氢输运（hydrogen transport）代码中局部热力学平衡（local thermodynamic equilibrium, LTE）界面对化学势连续性的三个隐含假设，并以可逆反应通道（reversible reaction channels）框架替代之，在festim中实现，恢复LTE为单通道快动力学极限，并以镍/FLiBe体系展示氢在分子载体和氟化物载体间的动力学分配行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观氢输运代码以每物种（per-species）化学势连续作为界面约束，其中隐含三个假设：界面快速平衡、两侧单一交换路径、每侧载体物种已知。文献关注第一个假设，但后两个在金属/熔盐界面上均不成立，构成更严重的失败，因此需要更一般的界面模型。

Method: 以遵循质量作用定律（mass action law）的界面可逆反应通道替代LTE约束，利用详细平衡（detailed balance）从已参数化LTE的热力学数据固定各速率常数之比，并在开源软件festim中实现该框架。

Result: 在典型镍/FLiBe体系中，氢以动力学方式在分子载体与氟化物载体之间分配；表观界面定律随载量和熔盐氧化还原态在Sieverts与Henry定律之间漂移，LTE条件低估稳态渗透通量；压力指数由通道分支比（branching ratio）决定而非熔盐固有性质，氧化还原扫描可在固定温度下使该指数在0.5与1之间连续移动；双同位素情形下每物种LTE条件不适定。

Conclusion: 所提反应通道框架推广了LTE而非与其竞争，LTE是单通道在快动力学极限下（含Sieverts/Sieverts和Sieverts/Henry两种形式）的恢复结果；通道内有效性由Damköhler数界定，通道之间由分支比界定。

Abstract: Macroscopic hydrogen transport codes model material interfaces with local thermodynamic equilibrium (LTE), imposing continuity of chemical potential as a per-species constraint. Three assumptions hide in that condition: fast interfacial equilibration, a single exchange pathway between the two sides, and a carrier species known in advance on each side. The literature scrutinises the first, but the other two are the more consequential failures, and neither survives at a metal/molten-salt interface. We replace the constraint with reversible reaction channels at the interface that obey mass action, with detailed balance fixing each ratio of rate constants from the thermodynamic data that already parameterise LTE, and we implement the framework in festim. LTE is recovered as the fast-kinetics limit of a single channel, in both its Sieverts/Sieverts and its Sieverts/Henry form, so the framework generalises LTE and does not compete with it. A Damköhler number delimits validity within a channel, a branching ratio between channels. In a representative nickel/FLiBe system, hydrogen partitions kinetically between molecular and fluoride carriers. The apparent interfacial law then drifts between Sieverts and Henry with loading and salt redox state, and an LTE condition underestimates the steady permeating flux. The measured pressure exponent is set by the branching ratio, not by any fixed property of the salt; a redox sweep at fixed temperature should continuously shift it between 0.5 and 1. With two isotopes, two metal-side species feed five salt-side carriers, and a per-species LTE condition is ill-posed.

</details>

*Susan A. Rigter,Loreta A. Muscarella*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文探讨机械化学研磨（mechanochemical milling）作为合成卤化物钙钛矿（halide perovskites）的可扩展无溶剂方法，并论证其更深层潜力在于将机械力发展为合成控制的新维度，使研磨从经验方法转变为预测科学。文章提出需通过原位表征和计算工具回答研磨的原子尺度机制问题，并阐明由此解锁的设计能力及推向实际应用所需的两个检验条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械力有望成为合成控制的新轴心，但目前研磨过程究竟制备了什么、如何制备等问题多依赖假设回答，缺乏严格的原子尺度理解。卤化物钙钛矿因其软晶格和低形成能，对机械激活内在敏感，是建立这一预测科学的理想平台。

Method: 文章指出现有用于其他材料类别的原位（in-situ）表征与计算工具已经存在，关键是将这些工具应用于卤化物钙钛矿研磨体系，以澄清研磨相是否真正被俘获在非平衡态（non-equilibrium），还是仅是到达平衡态的便捷路径，并明确其原子尺度路径（atomistic pathway）。

Result: 若弥合上述认知缺口，将解锁两种设计能力：其一，将机械能用作合成变量以调控相选择（phase selection）；其二，使亚稳态（metastability）变得可编程，产生被俘获的相、特定中间体和无溶液合成路径可得的组分。此外，提出两个检验标准：研磨过程中工程化的状态必须在工作器件中存续，且该方法须在全器件生命周期内被证明真正绿色而非仅无溶剂。

Conclusion: 机械化学研磨对卤化物钙钛矿的深层承诺在于将机械力转化为合成控制变量，将研磨从经验方法提升为预测科学；实现这一目标需借助已有的原位与计算工具阐明其原子机制，并通过器件存活性与全生命周期绿色性两个测试来确认其实际可行性。

Abstract: Mechanochemical milling is regarded as a scalable, solvent-free method that makes halide perovskites that solution processing cannot. Its deeper promise, though, is that mechanical force could become a new axis of synthetic control to turn milling from an empirical method into a predictive science. Halide perovskites are the ideal platform to establish this: their soft lattices and low formation energies make them intrinsically responsive to mechanical activation. Realising this requires settling what milling actually makes, and how: can a milled phase genuinely be trapped outside equilibrium, or is it just a convenient way to the equilibrium phase, and by what atomistic pathway does it work? These questions are mostly answered by assumption, while the in-situ and computational tools that resolved them for other material classes already exist. Closing this gap will unlock two design capabilities: mechanical energy used as a synthetic variable to tune phase selection, and metastability made programmable, producing trapped phases, specific intermediates, and compositions that have no possible solution route. Finally, two tests decide whether that control reaches practice: the state engineered during milling must survive into a working device, and the method must prove genuinely greener across the full device life cycle, rather than just solvent-free.

</details>

The transient change in magnetic ordering, observed as variations in the coercive field, is attributed to laser-induced modifications of the ferroelectric sublattice displacement, which affect the ferroelectric polarization and the associated Rashba spin-orbit interaction.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Nickel phosphorus trisulfide (NiPS$_3$) stands out as a Mott insulator exhibiting XY-type antiferromagnetic order. In this work, we investigate the electrodynamic response of single-crystalline NiPS$_3$ using Terahertz Time-Domain Spectroscopy (THz-TDS) as a function of temperature. In cases where the magnetic permeability cannot be approximated to unity, conventional THz transmission analysis is often restricted to the complex refractive index, hindering the distinction between magnetic and dielectric contributions. To overcome this limitation, we demonstrate an extraction methodology capable of decoupling the magnetic permeability from the electric permittivity. Assuming that the dielectric contribution remains invariant below the N'eel temperature (TN), we used data from the paramagnetic phase to isolate the intrinsic magnetic component at 13 K. The angular and thermal dependence of the spectra revealed a magnon mode near 1 THz (for 0$^\circ$ orientation) and, distinctively, a zone-folded phonon activated by symmetry breaking (at 90$^\circ$). The isolated magnetic permeability was successfully modeled using Drude-Lorentz oscillators, enabling the extraction of fundamental spin dynamics parameters in this material.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Allen-Heine-Cardona（AHC）电子-声子自能微扰方法在壳上（on-shell）计算固体的温度依赖带隙是有效的，但将其推广到任意频率以通过Dyson方程获取完整谱函数时会失效——卫星峰位置错误、能带极值处无展宽。已有的非微扰超胞方法仅关注本征值平均而非谱线形，特殊位移方法也无法描述完整线形或简并能带的线移。因此需要一种能同时捕获线移、线宽和不对称性的非微扰方法。

Method: 该方法采用非微扰格林函数方法（NPG），随机采样畸变超胞（supercell）构型，直接从采样中构建谱函数，无需依赖微扰展开。通过自洽传播子（propagator）构造，避免了裸传播子进入Dyson方程导致虚部在裸带隙内消失的问题，并自然包含所有非泡利图（non-bubble diagrams）的贡献。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在描述谱函数时超越裸微扰理论不仅是更精确的选择，而且是必要的。NPG提供了从第一性原理捕获谱展宽和高阶效应的稳健框架。

Abstract: The temperature-dependent band gap of solids is usually computed from the perturbative Allen-Heine-Cardona (AHC) electron-phonon self-energy evaluated on-shell. Extending AHC to arbitrary frequency $ω$ to determine the full spectral function via the Dyson equation is known to fail, misplacing satellites and yielding no broadening at band extrema, while non-perturbative supercell (SC) methods have focused on eigenvalue averages rather than lineshapes, and approaches based on special displacements cannot describe the full lineshape, or the lineshift at degenerate bands. Here we use a non-perturbative Green's function method (NPG), stochastically sampling distorted SC configurations, from which the spectral function, including lineshift, linewidth, and asymmetry, follows directly, and we recover finite spectral weight at the renormalized band extrema. We prove that the perturbative self-energy, computed to any order with the bare propagator and introduced into the Dyson equation, has an imaginary part that vanishes within the bare gap, giving incorrect spectral functions: self-consistency of the propagator is essential to broaden the band edges. NPG satisfies this property by construction, and contains all non-bubble diagrams. We also give a simple explanation of why SC methods converge with much smaller SCs than the corresponding $\mathbf{q}$-grids required by perturbation theory. For the band gap shift itself, the NPG and on-shell AHC results are found to be comparable, demonstrating that higher-order terms do not significantly alter the resulting renormalization in diamond. When it comes to the spectral function though, our results show that going beyond bare perturbation theory is not merely more accurate, but necessary, and NPG provides a robust framework to capture spectral broadening and higher-order effects from first principles.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnet）是一种共线的补偿磁体（compensated magnet），其动量依赖的自旋劈裂不依赖于净磁化强度，且可在非相对论自旋群框架下自然描述。这引发了一个基本问题：在没有任何自旋轨道诱导各向异性的情况下，纯交换相互作用能否在二维体系中稳定交错磁序？

Method: 作者将Bogoliubov不等式应用于一个最小d波交错磁体自旋模型，通过分析交错磁性排斥交换模式在长波长极限下的Bogoliubov分母贡献，来检验二维体系中长程磁序的稳定性。

Result: 研究发现，负责交错磁性的各向异性交换模式在Bogoliubov分母中仅贡献二次长波项。因此，当连续自旋旋转对称性保持时，短程交换相互作用单独无法在二维体系中稳定长程交错磁序。相反，对应的三维体系具有有限的小动量贡献，不受Mermin-Wagner论证的限制。

Conclusion: 二维交错磁序无法仅通过纯交换相互作用在连续自旋旋转对称性下稳定，这一结论与Mermin-Wagner定理一致；而三维交错磁体则不受此限制，可借助短程交换相互作用稳定有序态。

Abstract: Altermagnets (AMs) are collinear compensated magnets that exhibit momentum-dependent spin splitting without requiring a net magnetization. Since local magnetic moments and exchange-driven order do not rely on relativistic effects, altermagnetism can be naturally formulated in a nonrelativistic spin-group framework. This raises a basic question: can two-dimensional altermagnetic order be stabilized by purely exchange interactions in the absence of spin-orbit-induced anisotropy? We address this question by applying Bogoliubov's inequality to a minimal $d$-wave altermagnetic spin model. We show that the anisotropic exchange pattern responsible for altermagnetism still contributes only a quadratic long-wavelength term to the Bogoliubov denominator. Consequently, short-ranged exchange interactions alone cannot stabilize long-range altermagnetic order in two dimensions when continuous spin-rotation symmetry is preserved. In contrast, the corresponding three-dimensional system has a finite small-momentum contribution and is not ruled out by the Mermin-Wagner argument.

</details>

Abstract: As integrated circuits advance toward higher power densities, three-dimensional integration, and heterogeneous packaging, chip thermal management has become a key bottleneck limiting device performance, reliability, and lifetime. This article systematically reviews numerical simulation methods and experimental measurement techniques for chip thermal design, with particular emphasis on the technical challenges associated with multiscale and multiphysics coupling, thermal boundary resistance measurement, and high-heat-flux cooling. We first introduce macro- and device-scale thermal simulation methods, including equivalent thermal-circuit models, the finite element method, and computational fluid dynamics, and discuss the application of phonon transport theory and molecular dynamics at microscopic scales. We then examine the advantages and limitations of infrared thermography, thermoreflectance, Raman thermometry, and embedded sensors. Current limitations include the enormous computational cost, inaccurate multiscale coupling, expensive experimental facilities, and the physical limits of conventional cooling technologies. Finally, we discuss emerging directions, including AI-accelerated thermal simulation, embedded microchannel liquid cooling, two-phase cooling, advanced high-thermal-conductivity materials, and multiphysics co-design, with the aim of advancing chip thermal management toward greater efficiency and intelligence.

Abstract: Site-selective nanostructure growth and material incorporation at the atomic scale offer a promising pathway for engineering quantum materials and nanodevices. A physically consistent description of kL therefore requires phonon renormalization, four-phonon scattering, and coherent transport to be treated on equal footing rather than inferred from three-phonon agreement at a single temperature. These results provide microscopic insight into thermal transport in ultrawide-bandgap stannate perovskites and establish a benchmark for anharmonic transport in strongly distorted oxides.

Abstract: Magnetic anisotropy defines the easy axis of a magnetic material and governs the spatial arrangement of its domains. Using low-temperature magnetic force microscopy, we observe that the preferred orientation of magnetic domains switches from the a-axis to the b-axis upon temperature variation, and that this switching can also be triggered by an out-of-plane magnetic-field reset. Magnetization measurements and density functional theory calculations confirm a robust c-axis easy magnetization, ruling out a conventional spin-reorientation transition. Instead, the texture switching is governed by the temperature dependence of the in-plane variation of the Magnetic anisotropy energy landscape, which arises from two competing interactions with different decay rates: single-ion anisotropy favors a-oriented spin components, while nearest-neighbor anisotropic exchange favors b-oriented ones. Furthermore, the critical switching temperature is substantially elevated in a mechanically exfoliated EuTi3Bi4 flake. Our findings establish that macroscopic magnetic textures can be effectively manipulated by tuning the competition between in-plane anisotropic interactions, without the energy cost of reorienting the easy axis.

Abstract: Battery lifetime is central to sustainable electrification, yet the particle cracking that drives lithium-ion cathode aging is hard to measure: quantitative microscopy of this degradation is bottlenecked by annotation, because each destructive electron-microscopy cross-section spans hundreds of megapixels and pixel-level expert labelling requires hours per image. Late intergranular crack coverage reaches 4.6% in the cycled sample versus 0.5% in the initial and calendar-aged samples, forming more tortuous, higher-coverage networks, consistent with degradation from repeated electrochemical cycling rather than elevated-temperature storage alone. A single destructive image yields the population-level statistics needed for lifetime-extending design, aging assessment and second-life decisions.

Abstract: We present a method for identifying crystal structure prototypes from local atomic environments. The model classifies each atom into one of 271 AFLOW crystal structure prototypes or the amorphous class (272 classes), reaching 99.38% accuracy on 538,190 per-atom samples. Abstract: Grain-boundary segregation and thermally induced interfacial disordering were investigated in four high-entropy transition metal carbides using Monte Carlo (MC) sampling and molecular dynamics (MD) with the universal MACE-OMAT-0 machine-learning interatomic potential. MC sampling segregated the group-VI element (Cr, Mo, or W) and Zr to grain boundaries, where the group-VI content reached approximately 45 at.%, consistent with STEM-EDS observations. During MD heating, the grain-boundary Lindemann index, a normalized measure of interatomic distance fluctuations, reached the liquid-like threshold of $δ=0.15$ near $1390^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ for the Cr-containing carbides, $1660^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ for Mo, and $1890^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ for W, while the grain interiors remained below the threshold. A chemically random (Cr,Hf,Ta,Ti,Zr)C reference crossed about $60^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ later and showed less boundary-localized disorder, highlighting the role of interfacial chemistry in premelting. Species-resolved displacements showed enhanced grain-boundary mobility, particularly for carbon. Overall, Cr-rich interfaces showed the earliest and most extensive premelting-like response, followed by Mo- and W-containing boundaries.

TL;DR: 本文评估了超快力场（ultra-fast force field）UF³势，该势采用线性回归与三次B样条基函数表示有效二体和三体相互作用，在精度上与GAP、MTP、NNP和qSNAP等机器学习原子间势（MLIP）相当，但其显式可解释的样条形式允许直接可视化学习到的相互作用，并可通过熔点计算揭示其对强角向或共价键合体系的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potentials, MLIPs）在分子动力学模拟中能以远低于密度泛函理论（DFT）的计算成本提供接近DFT的精度，但许多先进MLIP计算负担仍高且作为黑箱模型运行，缺乏物理可解释性。因此需要一种兼顾精度、效率和物理可解释性的势模型。

Method: 研究者评估了UF³势模型，其采用线性回归配合三次B样条（cubic B-spline）基函数来表示有效的二体和三体相互作用。通过计算六个元素体系的熔点来检验模型的迁移能力，其中势函数拟合不包含任何固-液界面构型或关于熔化的显式热力学信息。

Result: UF³在精度上与GAP、MTP、NNP（Behler-Parrinello）和qSNAP等既有MLIP模型相当。对简单金属（Ni、Cu、Li），模型再现实验熔点的误差约在6%以内；但对Mo和Si则显著低估，且对Ge无法获得稳定势，反映了将展开固定截断至三体项对强角向或共价键合体系的局限性。此外，样条基公式使学习到的相互作用可直接可视化，有助于识别黑箱方法常掩盖的非物理行为。

Conclusion: UF³是一种兼具DFT级精度、极低计算成本和物理可解释性的机器学习原子间势，但其截断至三体项的固定展开形式在处理具有强角向或共价键合特性的体系时存在明显不足，此类体系可能需要更高阶的多体项或更灵活的基函数形式。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to density functional theory (DFT) for molecular dynamics simulations, offering near-DFT accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost. However, many state-of-the-art MLIPs remain computationally demanding and act as black boxes, limiting physical interpretability. In this work, we evaluate the ultra-fast force field (UF$^3$) potential, which employs linear regression with cubic B-spline basis to represent effective two- and three-body interactions. We show that UF$^3$ displays accuracy comparable to established models such as GAP, MTP, NNP (Behler Parrinello), and qSNAP MLIPs. We further investigate the transferability of UF$^3$ by computing melting points for six elemental systems with potentials fitted without any solid-liquid interface configurations or explicit thermodynamic information about melting. The model reproduces experimental melting points within $\sim$6% for simple metals (Ni, Cu, Li), but substantially underestimates them for Mo and Si and fails to yield a stable potential for Ge, reflecting the limitations of a fixed expansion truncated at the three-body term for systems with strong angular or covalent bonding. We further illustrate how UF$^3$'s spline-based formulation allows direct visualization of the learned interactions, enabling identification of unphysical behavior that black-box approaches often obscure.

</details>


### [62] [3D Cloud Component Analysis of Atomic Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27304)
*Pai Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将原子结构分解为物理上有意义的组成部分的方法：将离散原子坐标转换为连续三维密度场，通过密度填值-模糊（bin-then-blur）算法结合周期性边界条件快速计算各元素的高斯涂抹密度图；每个体素被赋予化学式，经清理后仅保留具有真实块体内部成分的区域作为组件；再使用神经原型分类器（neural prototype classifier）确定晶体相，并由边界派生界面与表面，最后在晶体内检测缺陷。方法在Si/GaAs与Si/SiO2异质结、晶态/非晶硅结、含空位晶体等体系上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子结构的分析通常需要对材料体系进行合理的组分分解，但现有方法在如何区分块体（bulk）区域、表面（surface）与界面（interface）、以及处理薄层终止和边界层等细节上缺乏系统的自动化方案。作者旨在建立一种能从原子坐标出发、不依赖人工先验识别材料中各类几何与化学组成部分的通用分解框架。

Method: 将离散原子坐标转化为连续的三维密度场，每种元素以高斯涂抹密度图表示（数值0到1），通过“先分箱后模糊”的方法结合周期性边界条件高效计算。所有元素密度之和截断于1定义材料区域，其补集为真空。每个体素根据阈值上存在的元素集合被赋予化学式，生成的化学式图经清理后保留具有真实块体内部（以到自身边界的欧几里得距离衡量）的区域作为组件。薄表面终止层和晶体间1至2个体素的边界层被邻近稳定区域吸收，使分解结果只含块体化学式组件与真空。每个组件用神经原型分类器判定晶体相：若内部原子一致投票则为一相，若分为两个置信分组则视为两相并将组件分割。界面和表面作为材料-材料与材料-真空边界派生，每个原子标记为块体、表面、界面或顶点；晶态组件内缺陷通过配位数（coordination number）和局域密度的偏差检测。

Result: 该方法在Si/GaAs异质结、Si/SiO2异质结、晶态/非晶硅结、含空位晶体以及块体晶体上得到了演示验证。结果表明该方法能正确分解出仅含块体化学式组分与真空的结构，自动吸收如GaAs上Ga单分子层等薄表面终止层及晶体间边界层，并能区分晶态与非晶两相、识别顶点原子以及检测晶内缺陷。

Conclusion: 该密度场驱动的分解框架为原子结构提供了一种自动化、物理可解释的组分识别方法，能够同时区分块体、表面、界面、顶点以及晶内缺陷，适用于异质结、非晶/晶态界面和含缺陷晶体等多类材料体系。

Abstract: We present a method for decomposing atomic structures into physically meaningful components by converting discrete atomic coordinates into continuous three-dimensional density fields. Each element is represented by a Gaussian-smeared density map with values ranging from 0 to 1, computed efficiently through a bin-then-blur approach with periodic boundary conditions. The sum of all element densities, truncated at unity, defines the material region; its complement defines the vacuum. Every voxel is first assigned a chemical formula from the set of elements present above a threshold; the resulting formula map is then cleaned so that only regions with a genuine bulk interior-measured by the Euclidean distance to their own boundary-survive as components. Thin surface terminations (e.g., a Ga monolayer on GaAs) and one-to-two-voxel boundary layers between two crystals are absorbed by the neighboring stable region, so the decomposition contains exactly the bulk-like chemical-formula components and the vacuum, with no surface or interface components. For each component we determine the crystal phase with a neural prototype classifier: one phase if the trusted interior atoms vote unanimously, two phases (e.g., crystalline and amorphous Si) if they split into two confident groups, in which case the component is divided into two. Interfaces and surfaces are then derived as boundaries-material-material and material-vacuum-and every atom is labeled bulk, surface, interface, or vertex, with vertex reserved for geometric corners of a component. Inside crystalline components, inner defects are detected from coordination-number and local-density deviations. We demonstrate the approach on Si/GaAs and Si/SiO2 heterojunctions, crystalline/amorphous silicon junctions, vacancy-containing crystals, and bulk crystals.

</details>

*Md Sharier Nazim,Giacomo Po,Nikhil Chandra Admal*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的位错台阶攀移被认为必须依赖长程体相扩散供给点缺陷，但晶界原子结构固有的构型自由度可能提供替代路径。该研究旨在探索这一替代机制，以更全面地理解位错-晶界相互作用如何控制多晶材料的力学响应。

Method: 采用双晶学分析与分子动力学模拟相结合的方法，系统研究了Al中剪切位错环与[1 1 0](-5 5 14)对称倾转晶界（symmetric tilt grain boundary）的相互作用过程，并追踪外来位错台阶的运动及其引起的晶界微结构演化。

Result: 研究显示，位错吸收会生成一个具有非零攀移分量且可迁移的外来位错台阶，该台阶沿界面守恒传播且无需长程体相点缺陷输运。其运动伴随由晶界核内协同原子重排介导的局域晶界相变，产生一系列亚稳晶界微态。

Conclusion: 这些发现建立了晶格位错与晶界相演化之间的直接耦合，揭示了一种与常规空位介导攀移在本质上截然不同的守恒型位错台阶攀移机制。

Abstract: Dislocation-grain boundary (GB) interactions govern the mechanical response of polycrystalline materials by controlling dislocation absorption, transmission, and interfacial plasticity. While disconnection climb is conventionally understood to require the absorption or emission of point defects supplied through long range bulk diffusion, GBs possess intrinsic configurational degrees of freedom associated with their atomic structure, or microstate, that may provide an alternative mechanism. Here, using bicrystallography and molecular dynamics simulations, we investigate the interaction of shear dislocation loops with the [1 1 0](-5 5 14) symmetric tilt grain boundary in Al. We show that dislocation absorption generates a mobile extrinsic disconnection with a nonzero climb component that propagates conservatively along the interface without long-range bulk point-defect transport. Its motion is accompanied by a localized GB phase transformation mediated by cooperative atomic rearrangements within the GB core, producing successive metastable GB microstates. These findings establish a direct coupling between lattice dislocations and GB phase evolution and reveal a conservative mechanism for disconnection climb fundamentally distinct from conventional vacancy-mediated climb.

</details>


### [64] [Coupled anisotropic weak topological states and Floquet mixed-parity altermagnetism in two-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27329)
*Kunyuan Feng,Xibin Liu,Chenchen Liu,Siyuan Liu,Lixiu Guan,Xiaobiao Liu,François M. Peeters,Linyang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过紧束缚方法构建了五种二维Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）拓扑模型，系统研究了圆偏振光和相对原子位移对SSH弱拓扑态与交替磁（altermagnetic, AM）序耦合相的调控，实现了多宇称AM相与拓扑边缘态的同步光场控制。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSH拓扑系统与交替磁态是凝聚态物理中的两个重要研究方向，但在单一体系中实现二者的耦合态仍具挑战性，Floquet工程为此提供了新的路径。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维体系中各向异性SSH弱拓扑态与多宇称AM序的耦合建立了有效的理论平台，其物理机制可由紧束缚方法完整理解，并与第一性原理计算良好吻合。

Abstract: Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) topological systems and altermagnetic (AM) states are two important research areas in condensed matter physics. Realizing the coupled states between the SSH lattices and AM phanse in a single syetem remains challenging. However, the Floquet engineering change it. Our work constructs five two-dimensional-(2D-) SSH models to describe the coupled phases of SSH weak topological state and AM order by tight-binding (TB) method. The evolution of band structures, spin-splitting, and topological phase transitions under the circularly polarized light (CPL) and relative atomic displacement (RAD) in 2D SSH lattices were systematically investigated. The results reveal that the inequality of hopping parameters (t1 and t2) serves as the fundamental origin of SSH topological states and AM order. For the 2D nonmagnetic state, anisotropic weak topological states with Zak phase governed edge states are realized by unit cell selection, similar to the conventional 1D SSH model. The collinear antiferromagnetic state preserves the spin-degenerate band structure and intrinsic weak topological properties. Furthermore, the Floquet engineering introduces the AM phase of odd-parity p-wave while the RAD introduces the AM phase of even-parity d-wave. By combining the two effects, the mixed-parity (non-odd/non-even parity) AM phase could be realized, achieving the simultaneous control of the light field of spin-splitting and topological edge states. The physical mechanisms of Floquet engineering and RAD for the 2D rectangular SSH lattice are also from the inequality of t1 and t2, which can be not only fully understand by the TB methods, but also in good agreement with the first-principle calculations of 2D carbon-based materials. This work establishes an effective theoretical platform for coupling anisotropic SSH weak topological states and AM orders with multi-parities in 2D systems.

</details>


### [65] [Temperature dependence of the charge density from first principles: application to the (222) forbidden reflection in silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27361)
*Jean Paul Nery,Raveena Gupta,Olle Hellman,Philip B. Allen,Matthieu J. Verstraete*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，利用微扰论和热畸变超胞平均两种方法首次从理论上得到硅（silicon）中(222)禁戒反射（forbidden reflection）的随温度变化的价电荷密度ρ(r,T)，得到的反射强度与实验合理吻合，无需引入任何费心调整（ad-hoc）的Debye-Waller因子，并揭示了价电荷随温度从核心区间向成键区转移的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 禁戒反射长期被用作探测对称性破缺、局域应变、杂质和弱电荷重分布的灵敏工具，但此前对其温度依赖性的理论处理多依赖简化模型，尤其是对决定(222)反射强度的原子间价电荷使用原子Debye-Waller因子其适用性存疑，且以往理论与实验的一致依赖于经验性修正，缺乏第一性原理层面的严格描述。

Method: 作者采用两种互补的第一性原理方法计算硅的随温度变化的价电荷密度：一是微扰论（perturbation theory）方法，其中电荷密度对波函数本身的依赖要求对未占据带进行显式求和，计算代价远高于电子能量；二是对热畸变超胞（thermally distorted supercells）进行非微扰平均的方法。为使微扰表达式在现有框架内可行，作者利用声学求和规则（acoustic sum rule）将势的二阶导数用一阶导数表示。(222)禁戒反射强度直接由ρ(r,T)的傅里叶变换得到，无需任何人为修正因子。

Result: 两种计算方法给出相似的结果，并与实验合理吻合；热膨胀（thermal expansion）对温度依赖性有明显影响。电荷密度揭示：与刚性模型相比，随着温度升高，成键区（bond regions）电荷增多而核心区（core regions）电荷减少，这一重分布在反射强度上表现为(222)禁戒反射具有较弱的温度依赖性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在无任何ad-hoc参数的前提下，从第一性原理准确描述了禁戒反射的温度依赖行为，同时回答了实验测量无法回答的问题——价电荷如何随温度在实空间重新分布，为禁戒反射的理论理解和热效应下的电荷演化提供了新的定量框架。

Abstract: Forbidden reflections (FRs) in X-ray diffraction have inherently weak intensity and have long been studied, in particular in semiconductors like silicon. They serve as sensitive probes of symmetry breaking, local strain, impurities, and weak charge redistribution. Despite extensive experimental work, the theory of their temperature dependence has typically relied on simplified models. While atomic Debye-Waller factors work well on allowed reflections, their applicability to the valence charge between atoms, which determines the intensity of FRs such as Si (222), is questionable, and previous agreement between theory and experiment relied on ad-hoc Debye-Waller corrections. We compute the temperature-dependent valence charge density $ρ(\mathbf{r},T)$ of silicon from first principles, using two methods: (i) perturbation theory, and (ii) averaging over thermally distorted supercells in a non-perturbative approach. The perturbative expression for the charge density is far more demanding than that for electronic energies, since it depends on the wavefunctions themselves and requires an explicit sum over unoccupied bands. We use an acoustic sum rule to express the second derivatives of the potential in terms of first derivatives, making the expression tractable within existing frameworks. The (222) FR then follows directly from the Fourier transform of $ρ(\mathbf{r},T)$, with no ad-hoc factors. Both methods give similar results, in reasonable agreement with experiment, with thermal expansion noticeably affecting the temperature dependence. The charge density answers a question the measured intensities could not settle: how the valence charge actually redistributes with temperature. Relative to the rigid model, we find more charge in the bonds and less in the core regions, a redistribution that shows up in the intensity as a somewhat weaker temperature dependence of the (222) FR.

</details>

### [66] [Paleo-Detectors as a Novel Probe of Dark Matter-Nucleus Effective Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26289)
*Dionysios P. Theodosopoulos*

TL;DR: 本文综述了古探测器（paleo-detectors）在非相对论有效场论（NREFT）框架下对WIMP-核子相互作用的灵敏度预测。古探测器利用天然矿物在地下深处累积的约十亿年（gigayear）辐射损伤径迹来实现超长积分时间。结果表明，对于1 GeV/c²至10 GeV/c²的暗物质质量，古探测器在所有NREFT算子介导的WIMP-核相互作用中均优于常规直接探测实验；对于10 GeV/c²至5 TeV/c²的质量范围，其灵敏度在多个算子下可达到或超越常规实验水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗物质（dark matter）直接探测依赖大质量探测器，成本高且灵敏度受限。古探测器通过利用天然矿物在地质时间尺度上累积的损伤径迹，以极小样品质量实现超长积分时间，为暗物质直接探测提供了新的替代途径。本文旨在系统综述古探测器在NREFT框架下对WIMP-核子相互作用的灵敏度预测，并与常规实验进行对比。

Method: 在非相对论有效场论（NREFT）框架内，同时考虑弹性散射（elastic scattering）和非弹性散射（inelastic scattering）过程，并纳入等标量耦合（isoscalar coupling），将中微子与放射性成因（radiogenic）源作为背景加以考虑。在此基础上，针对不同读出方案（readout scenario）和曝光条件计算古探测器的预期灵敏度，并与常规直接探测实验进行对比。

Result: 对于1 GeV/c²至10 GeV/c²的暗物质质量区间，古探测器在所有NREFT算子介导的WIMP-核相互作用中均展现出优于常规实验的灵敏度，且对读出方案或靶矿物的选择依赖较弱。对于10 GeV/c²至5 TeV/c²的质量区间，古探测器在若干NREFT算子下可达到或超越常规实验的灵敏度水平，具体表现取决于读出方案和靶矿物的选择。

Conclusion: 古探测器在中低质量暗物质区间（1 GeV/c²至10 GeV/c²）具有显著的灵敏度优势，在更大质量区间也可作为常规直接探测实验的有力补充。其灵敏度对读出方案和靶矿物选择具有一定依赖性，这为未来实验设计提供了优化空间。

Abstract: Paleo-detectors are a proposed approach to the direct detection of dark matter (DM) based on the observation of DM-induced nuclear recoils. Rather than relying on large detector masses required for conventional detectors, paleo-detectors exploit extremely long integration times, using small samples of naturally occurring minerals that have resided deep underground and accumulated damage tracks over geological timescales of order a gigayear. In this contribution, we review recent theoretical predictions for the sensitivity of paleo-detectors to WIMP-nucleus interactions within the framework of a Non-Relativistic Effective Field Theory (NREFT). We discuss both elastic and inelastic scattering processes and consider isoscalar couplings, taking into account backgrounds from neutrinos and radiogenic sources. We present projected sensitivities of paleo-detectors for different readout and exposure scenarios and compare the reach of paleo-detectors to that of conventional direct-detection experiments. For DM masses in the range $1\ \mathrm{GeV}/c^2$-$10\ \mathrm{GeV}/c^2$, paleo-detectors are expected to achieve sensitivities exceeding those of conventional experiments for WIMP-nucleus interactions mediated by all NREFT operators, with only a weak dependence on the read-out scenario or target mineral. For larger DM masses, $10\ \mathrm{GeV}/c^2$-$5\ \mathrm{TeV}/c^2$, paleo-detectors are projected to attain sensitivities comparable to or surpassing conventional experiments for interactions mediated by several NREFT operators, depending on the specific read-out scenario and choice of target mineral.

</details>


### [67] [Near universality of nonlinear transverse and radial velocity responses in spherical collapse with arbitrary radial profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27376)

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对非均匀球对称密度分布（既非均匀顶帽模型）下的膨胀响应问题，提出了一个完整的壳层（shellwise）解析框架。研究表明，对于含宇宙学常数的无压物质增长模态，密度与膨胀之间的非线性关系虽通常只对均匀顶帽成立，但通过横向与径向膨胀率（$H_\perp$ 与 $H_\parallel$）及局部与包围密度对比（$δ$ 与 $Δ$），可以构造精确的非线性响应，且无需求解演化方程，仅由一个顶帽函数决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上密度与膨胀之间的非线性关系仅针对均匀球状顶帽（top hat）模型推导，该模型只有一个局部哈勃率。然而光滑球状密度剖面在径向和横向方向上膨胀率不同，这种径向不均匀性（radial inhomogeneity）的直接信号此前缺乏统一且完整的解析描述，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者证明，对于含宇宙学常数的无压物质（growing-mode pressureless matter），完整的壳层响应可由一个顶帽函数固定。利用局部密度对比 $δ(t,r)$ 和包围对比 $Δ(t,r)$，通过横向响应及其导数确定 $H_\perp$ 和 $H_\parallel$。线性与二阶极限退化为仅依赖通常的增长率（growth rate）$f$ 的代数映射；精确同龄构造（equal-age construction）无需积分演化方程即可提供非线性响应。此外，还提供了一个考虑导数、与宇宙学无关的三项符号拟合公式，仅需 $f$、$δ$ 和 $Δ$。

Result: 该方法可精确重建完整的 $Λ$LTB 剖面，达到数值精度。在代表性物质-曲率-红移组合及补偿过渡区（compensated transition）中的壳层处，横向与径向响应的最大相对误差分别为 $0.3\%$ 和 $0.7\%$。

Conclusion: 该紧凑表述将密度剖面的生成与其膨胀响应分离，使得径向梯度（radial gradients）的影响显式化。这一框架为在非均匀背景下理解膨胀动力学提供了简洁且精确的工具。

Abstract: The nonlinear relation between density and expansion is usually formulated for a homogeneous spherical top hat, which has a single local Hubble rate. A smooth spherical profile instead expands differently along ($H_{\parallel}$) and across ($H_{\perp}$) the radial direction-a direct signature of radial inhomogeneity. We show that, for growing-mode pressureless matter with a cosmological constant, the complete shellwise response is nevertheless fixed by one top-hat function. Given the local density contrast $δ(t,r)$ and the enclosed contrast $Δ(t,r)$, a transverse response and its derivative determine $H_{\perp}$ and $H_{\parallel}$. The linear and second-order limits are algebraic maps whose only dynamical input is the usual growth rate $f$. An exact equal-age construction supplies the nonlinear response without integrating an evolution equation. We check that it reconstructs full $Λ$LTB profiles to numerical precision. We also provide a derivative-aware, cosmology-independent three-term symbolic fit that requires only $f$, $δ$, and $Δ$. Across a representative set of matter-curvature-redshift combinations and for a shell located in the compensated transition, the maximum relative errors are $0.3\%$ and $0.7\%$ in the transverse and radial responses, respectively. This compact formulation separates the production of a density profile from its expansion response and makes the effect of radial gradients explicit.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质晕承载星系和星系团，其特殊速度分布远离热平衡，具有非线性和非高斯特征。刻画这一分布特征有助于理解宇宙网（cosmic web）的引力演化，并支持相关宇宙学应用。研究者希望建立晕速度分布与远离平衡态统计力学之间的联系，以获得既简洁又实用的模型。

Method: 从大规模N体模拟中提取晕样本，采用极大似然拟合（maximum-likelihood fitting），使用源于非广延统计力学（non-extensive statistical mechanics）的双参数Tsallis模型拟合特殊速度分布。在理论方面，在超统计（superstatistics）框架下，基于伽马分布（gamma distribution）的广义Gram-Charlier展开重新表述了晕分布。

Abstract: Dark matter halos, which host galaxies and galaxy clusters, have peculiar velocities far from thermal equilibrium. Characterizing the nonlinear and non-Gaussian features of this velocity distribution improves our understanding of the gravitational evolution of the cosmic web and supports related cosmological applications. We endeavor to establish a connection between the peculiar velocity distribution of halos and nonequilibrium statistical mechanics, with the objective of obtaining a model that is both concise and accurate for practical applications. We extracted halo samples from large N-body simulations and performed maximum-likelihood fits to the peculiar velocity distributions using a two-parameter Tsallis model, derived from non-extensive statistical mechanics. On the theoretical side, we reformulated the halo distribution in the superstatistics framework by means of a generalized Gram-Charlier expansion based on the gamma distribution. For halo peculiar velocities below 1000 km/s, the Tsallis model achieves 5 percent accuracy over z=0-2, with performance improving toward lower redshifts. Our results show that the halo velocity distribution becomes increasingly non-Gaussian and departs further from equilibrium over time. The best-fit parameters depend only weakly on mass, though low-mass halos exhibit slightly stronger non-Gaussianity. The two parameters, especially the velocity dispersion, offer promising probes of cosmological parameters. Theoretically, we find that in general the halo peculiar velocity distribution function is expressible as a superposition of a series of Tsallis distribution functions, while simulation results demonstrate that the zeroth-order approximation, namely a single Tsallis function, already achieves sufficient accuracy.

Abstract: We find that long-range radiative coupling through a passive reciprocal chiral medium produces an unusual non-Hermitian skin effect in a chain of electric dipoles. Although the system is reciprocal, the chirality generates polarization-dependent phase accumulation and attenuation, leading to degenerate pairs of skin modes localized at opposite boundaries. We find that the skin mode profile consists of an exponential contribution from an isolated complex-$β$ pole and a longer-range tail proportional to $1/[n(\ln n)^2]$, where $n$ is the distance from the occupied boundary measured in lattice sites. Finite-chain calculations show that part of the OBC spectrum contracts toward the Bloch spectrum, whereas the number of boundary-localized modes remains extensive.

### [71] [Efficient binned profile likelihood minimization for precision measurements with RABBIT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26376)
*David Walter,Josh Bendavid,Kenneth Long*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出Rabbit（Rapid Automatic Bin-Based Inference Tool），一个基于TensorFlow 2的Python框架，利用可微分编程（differentiable programming）实现自动微分、信任域最小化（trust-region minimizer）和即时编译（just-in-time compilation），用于加速LHC实验中对包含数千个分箱（bins）和恼人参数（nuisance parameters）的分箱轮廓极大似然拟合（binned profile maximum likelihood fits）。基准测试表明其在合成模型上展现出优秀的可扩展性，在传统工具无法收敛的挑战性场景中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC的精密测量日益依赖包含数千个分箱和恼人参数的分箱轮廓极大似然拟合，而高亮度LHC（High-Luminosity LHC）将进一步推高这些规模，因此需要快速且稳健的似然最小化方法以保证及时的分析开发和准确的推断。

Method: Rabbit利用TensorFlow 2中的可微分编程，在CPU和GPU上运行；采用自动微分（automatic differentiation）为在Krylov子空间（Krylov subspaces）中运行的信任域最小化器提供精确的梯度和Hessian-向量积，并通过即时编译获得接近C++的执行速度。框架支持灵活的统计模型及可能的解析处理，提供对称化选项以建立高斯近似（Gaussian approximations）和具有确定性解的线性化似然公式，并通过模型的可微分变换测量物理观测量，包括未折叠微分截面（unfolded differential cross sections）。

Result: 在合成模型上的基准测试表明，Rabbit在分箱数目和参数数目上展现出优异的表现扩展性，在具有挑战性的场景中优于既有工具——当这些工具在合理时间内无法收敛时，Rabbit仍能成功完成拟合。

Conclusion: Rabbit为大规模分箱似然拟合提供了一种快速、稳健且可扩展的解决方案，有望加速LHC及高亮度LHC时代的分析开发与精密物理量测量。

Abstract: Precision measurements at the LHC increasingly rely on binned profile maximum likelihood fits with thousands of bins and nuisance parameters, and the High-Luminosity LHC will push these numbers further. Fast and robust minimization of such likelihoods is crucial for timely analysis development and accurate inference. We present Rabbit (Rapid Automatic Bin-Based Inference Tool), a Python framework that exploits differentiable programming in TensorFlow 2 to perform this task on CPUs and GPUs. Automatic differentiation provides exact gradients and Hessian-vector products for a trust-region minimizer operating in Krylov subspaces, and just-intime compilation yields near-C++ execution speed. Rabbit implements flexible statistical models with analytic treatments where possible, supports symmetrization options that establish Gaussian approximations and a linearized likelihood formulation with deterministic solutions, and focuses on measuring physical observables through differentiable transformations of the model, including unfolded differential cross sections. Benchmarks on synthetic models demonstrate excellent scaling with the number of bins and parameters, outperforming established tools in challenging regimes where these fail to converge within reasonable time.

</details>

*ATLAS Collaboration*

TL;DR: 基于ATLAS实验在LHC上13 TeV质子-质子对撞中采集的140 fb⁻¹数据，通过研究高横动量光子与喷注（jet）事件中的γ-喷注不变质量谱，搜索超出标准模型（Standard Model）背景的新高质量现象。未观察到显著偏差，并对激发夸克、量子黑洞等新物理模型设定了严格的排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找新物理信号，特别是探索夸克复合性（quark compositeness）和额外维度相关的理论框架（如Randall-Sundrum和Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali模型）所预言的高质量现象。

Result: 未观测到与标准模型预期有显著偏离。在f=1条件下，排除质量上限：轻激发夸克q*L为6.0 TeV，c*为3.5 TeV，b*为2.6 TeV。在95%置信水平下，量子黑洞质量阈值在Randall-Sundrum和Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali模型中分别被排除至5.3 TeV和7.5 TeV。此外，利用通用高斯共振谱型获得了可见截面的约束。

Conclusion: 该分析提供了在LHC上γ-喷注末态中首次对c*产生过程施加的直接约束，同时进一步限定了Randall-Sundrum与Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali等理论框架的参数空间。

Abstract: A search for new high-mass phenomena in events with a high-transverse-momentum photon and a jet is performed using data collected by the ATLAS experiment from proton-proton collisions at the LHC, with a centre-of-mass energy of 13 TeV. The analysis is based on a total integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The $γ$$-$jet invariant mass spectrum measured in data is examined for localised excesses above the Standard Model background. No significant deviation from the Standard Model prediction is observed, allowing stringent limits to be set on the production cross sections of specific new physics scenarios. To investigate quark compositeness, excited quark models are studied for different couplings ($f$) and flavours: light $q_{\mathrm{L}}^{*}$ $(u^{*}/d^{*})$ and heavy ($b^{*}$, $c^{*}$). For $f=1$, masses up to 6.0 TeV ($q_{\mathrm{L}}^{*}$), 3.5 TeV ($c^{*}$), and 2.6 TeV ($b^{*}$) are excluded. These results provide the first direct constraints on $c^{*}$ production in the $γ$$-$ jet final state at the LHC. Additionally, the analysis excludes quantum black hole mass thresholds up to 5.3 (7.5) TeV in the Randall-Sundrum (Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali) models at a 95% confidence level, further constraining these theoretical frameworks. Constraints on the visible cross-section are also obtained using generic Gaussian-shaped resonance production.

</details>

*Ilker Parmaksiz,Aaron Higuera,Laura Paulucci,Viktor Pec,Estanislao Forino*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在10千吨（10 kt）尺度上实现了基于Opticks的GPU加速光学光子模拟，应用于DUNE远探测器水平漂移（FD-HD）几何结构。相比单线程和多线程GEANT4，分别实现了313倍和83倍的加速，同时保持了与GEANT4等价的蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂且大型的探测器几何结构中，光学光子模拟是计算需求最苛刻的任务之一。在DUNE实验中，远探测器模块的规模使得基于CPU的逐光子GEANT4输运在计算上不可行，亟需更高效的模拟方案。

Method: 基于Opticks框架，将光学光子模拟移植到GPU平台上，应用于完整的DUNE FD-HD几何结构；同时将Opticks集成到基于LArSoft的DUNE软件栈中，确保GPU模拟与GEANT4具有等价的物理过程。

Result: 在完整FD-HD几何上，Opticks与GEANT4模拟相同的光子，相对单线程GEANT4加速313±3倍，相对四线程GEANT4加速83±1倍；所有评估指标均与参考GEANT4模拟验证一致。

Conclusion: GPU模拟在保持完整蒙特卡罗保真度的前提下，大幅降低了计算成本，使高统计量光学研究和用于机器学习的有标签数据集生成在千吨（kiloton）尺度上成为可行。

Abstract: Optical photon simulation is among the most computationally demanding tasks in complex and large detector geometries. In the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE), the scale of the far-detector modules makes photon-by-photon transport with \texttt{GEANT4} prohibitively expensive on CPUs. We present the first implementation and performance evaluation of GPU-accelerated optical photon simulation at the 10~kt scale, based on \texttt{Opticks} and applied to the DUNE far-detector horizontal-drift (FD-HD) geometry. On the full FD-HD geometry, \texttt{Opticks} propagates the same photons as \texttt{GEANT4} with a speedup of $313 \pm 3$ over single-threaded and $83 \pm 1$ over four-thread \texttt{GEANT4}, and is validated against the reference \texttt{GEANT4} simulation across all metrics considered. We further integrate \texttt{Opticks} into the \texttt{LArSoft}-based DUNE software stack. Photons simulated on the GPU with \texttt{GEANT4}-equivalent physics retain full Monte Carlo fidelity at a computational cost that makes high-statistics optical studies and the generation of labeled datasets for machine learning feasible at the kiloton scale.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锶的圆态量子平台具有远超激光可及里德伯态（Rydberg states）的寿命，并拥有可用激光操控的光学活性离子核，有望用于量子技术。作者旨在探索利用光学离子核的动态极化率（dynamical polarizability）在光镊中囚禁圆态，并借此高精度表征其内部自旋动力学与相对论修正。

Method: 利用光镊中离子核的动态极化率实现圆态囚禁；测量n=51圆态轨道分量的衰变，并观测由光镊诱导的矢量光移（vector light shift）与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）以及离子核与里德伯电子间朗德g因子差异共同作用导致的单重态-三重态自旋振荡（singlet-triplet spin oscillations）。通过分析两电子自旋动力学，提取自旋轨道相互作用及δg修正。

Result: 观测到圆态轨道分量衰减及单重态-三重态自旋振荡；测量得到自旋轨道相互作用以及离子核电子与自由电子之间的朗德g因子修正δg ≈ 10⁻⁵，最终给出高精度结果g_{Sr⁺, 5s₁/₂} = 2.002290(1)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现锶圆态的高精度朗德g因子测量，证明了光学可及离子核与光镊囚禁相结合的平台在量子传感与精密测量中的潜力。

Abstract: Circular states of strontium are promising platforms for quantum technologies. They exhibit much longer lifetimes than laser-accessible Rydberg states and possess an optically active ionic core that can be manipulated with laser light. Here we trap circular states in optical tweezers by exploiting the dynamical polarizability of the ionic core. We measure the decay of the orbital component of a n=51 circular state and observe singlet-triplet spin oscillations arising from the interplay of vector light shifts induced by the optical tweezers, spin-orbit coupling associated with the Rydberg electron, and the difference in Landé $g$-factor between the ionic core and the Rydberg electrons. By observing the two electron spin dynamics, we measure the spin-orbit interaction and the correction $δg \sim$ 10^(-5) of the Landé $g$-factor of the Sr$^+$ 5s$_{1/2}$ ionic core electron with respect to that of a free electron. This provides a first high-precision measurement of $g_{\textrm{Sr}^+, 5s_{1/2}}$ = 2.002290(1).

</details>

### [75] [Holography as an Information Principle in Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26282)
*Sebastian De Haro,Enrico Cinti,Aude Corbeel*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于特胡夫特（'t Hooft）的全息原理（holographic principle）原始表述，分析了信息在量子引力（quantum gravity）中的方法论与概念角色，提出信息在其中发挥三种实质性的引导原则（guiding principle）作用，并论证纯粹的认知性或香农式（Shannon-style）信息概念在此语境下是不充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论的发展中，信息概念被广泛使用但缺乏系统的哲学审视。作者旨在阐明信息在全息原理中不仅是一个技术工具，而是具有实质性的方法论和解释性功能，并据此反驳仅将信息视为认知性或香农式度量的观点。

Method: 作者从特胡夫特的全息原理原始论文出发，结合后续全息发展（体重建、全息量子纠错、ER=EPR），区分信息在全息量子引力中发挥的三个角色：作为理论选择标准的全息界（holographic bounds）、作为理论等价原则的全息性质、以及联系量子纠缠和涌现时空结构的信息分布与编码。基于这三种角色，作者进一步区分"最大"（maximal）与"中间"（intermediate）两种信息概念。

Result: 论文得出三项主要结果：第一，全息界作为理论选择标准约束可行的量子引力理论；第二，体理论与边界理论需保持物理内容等价，尽管特胡夫特赋予更低维且可能确定性的边界描述以更基本的地位；第三，信息分布与编码将特胡夫特的建议与当代体重建及全息量子纠错等研究联系起来，其中涌现的时空结构与纠缠及冗余模式相关。

Conclusion: 文章结论认为，纯粹认知性或香农式信息概念不足以捕捉信息在全息量子引力中的方法论功能，需要区分"最大"与"中间"两种概念来刻画其解释引导作用，并呼吁对信息作为解释引导原则的角色进行更系统的哲学处理。

Abstract: We draw on 't Hooft's seminal formulation of the holographic principle to analyse the methodological and conceptual role of information in quantum gravity. We argue that, in 't Hooft's work and in later developments, information functions as a substantive guiding principle. We distinguish three aspects of this role: First, holographic bounds on the amount of information that can be stored in a region function as theory selection criteria that constrain viable quantum gravity theories. Second, holography functions as a principle of theoretical equivalence: the bulk and boundary theories must describe the same physical content, even though 't Hooft privileges a more fundamental, lower-dimensional, and potentially deterministic boundary description. Third, the distribution and encoding of information connect 't Hooft's proposals to contemporary work on bulk reconstruction, holographic quantum error correction, and ER=EPR, where emergent spacetime structure is tied to patterns of entanglement and redundancy. On the basis of these three roles, we argue that purely epistemic or Shannon-style conceptions of information are inadequate in this context: we instead outline a distinction between what we call maximal and intermediate conceptions that aim to capture the methodological and interpretative roles of information in holographic quantum gravity. We therefore suggest that, in the context of holography and quantum gravity, a more systematic philosophical treatment of the role of information as an interpretation-guiding principle would be desirable.

</details>

*Bing-Long Chen,Ruo-Bin Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在真空爱因斯坦约束方程成立的任意渐近平坦柯西初值曲面（Cauchy surface）附近，在平均曲率（mean curvature）满足适当小性条件下，构造了最大叶化（maximal foliations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦方程是洛伦兹度量的张量方程，求解时必须选取合适的规范条件。最大叶化作为强有力的规范条件，在闵可夫斯基时空整体稳定性和有界L^2曲率猜想两个广义相对论奠基性工作中发挥了关键作用。然而当初值超曲面并非最大时，通常的椭圆方法在求解爱因斯坦方程时构造最大叶化会遇到根本性困难，因此需要一种更一般的构造方法。

Method: 作者在任意满足真空爱因斯坦约束方程的渐近平坦柯西初值曲面附近，施加平均曲率的适当小性条件，从而提供了一种最大叶化的构造方案。

Result: 在平均曲率足够小的条件下，成功完成了对任意渐近平坦初值曲面附近最大叶化的构造。

Conclusion: 该工作将最大叶化的可构造性从最大初值曲面推广到具有小平均曲率的一般渐近平坦初值曲面，为求解爱因斯坦方程提供了一种更普适的规范条件选取途径。

Abstract: It is well known that the Einstein equations are tensor equations for a Lorentzian metric. Hence, choosing a suitable gauge condition is crucial for solving them. As a powerful gauge condition, maximal foliations have played a pivotal role in two groundbreaking works in general relativity: the global stability of Minkowski spacetime and the bounded L^2 curvature conjecture. Nevertheless, if the initial hypersurface fails to be maximal, the usual elliptic approach for constructing maximal foliations encounters fundamental difficulties when solving the Einstein equations.
  The purpose of the paper is to provide a construction of maximal foliations around any initial asymptotically flat Cauchy surface satisfying vacuum Einstein constraint equations, under a suitable smallness condition on the mean curvature.

</details>

*Md Mahmudur Rahman,Ivan C. Christov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文表明声学（acoustics）中的变分原理（variational principle）无需凭经验猜测拉格朗日密度（Lagrangian density），而可从经典连续介质力学（classical continuum mechanics）的基本拉格朗日量系统推导；并评述了拉格朗日坐标系下可压缩无耗散流体的作用量构造，通过微扰展开恢复线性声学方程及二阶声能平衡，亦展示了弱非线性近似的变分结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中许多声学变分原理常以临时拼凑（ad hoc）方式引入，为获得特定偏微分方程而任意选取拉格朗日密度项。本文旨在说明这种猜测方法并不必要，声学控制方程可从经典连续介质力学的基本拉格朗日量出发系统推导，并强调欧拉坐标并非唯一可用的推导途径。

Method: 本文在拉格朗日坐标系（Lagrangian frame）中为可压缩无耗散流体构造作用量（action），考察物质重标定对称性（material relabeling symmetry）及其伴随的赝动量平衡（pseudomomentum balance）以及相应的边界与跃变条件。通过微扰展开（perturbation expansion），从各变分原理恢复线性声学方程，并从拉格朗日框架额外获得二阶声能平衡。此外，针对一般正压流体（barotropic fluid）推导两坐标系下具有变分结构的弱非线性近似，非线性由参数B/A承载，理想气体结果作为特例恢复。

Result: 声学控制方程可从连续介质力学的基本拉格朗日量系统推导，无需临时猜测；拉格朗日坐标系与欧拉坐标系均可导出有效变分原理，前者额外给出二阶声能平衡；两种坐标系下均能构造具有变分结构的弱非线性近似，适用于一般正压流体，理想气体情形作为特例包含其中。

Conclusion: 声学变分原理的基础性来源是经典连续介质力学的基本拉格朗日量，猜测式构造并非必要；拉格朗日坐标系提供了与欧拉坐标系互补且信息更丰富的变分框架，并可通过统一途径恢复线性与弱非线性声学方程。

Abstract: Many variational principles for acoustics have appeared in the literature, often introduced in an \textit{ad hoc} manner by picking and choosing terms in a Lagrangian density to yield a desired governing partial differential equation. In this chapter, we show that such guesswork is unnecessary, as the governing equations of acoustics can be derived systematically from the primitive Lagrangians of classical continuum mechanics. Furthermore, we note that using Eulerian coordinates is not the only way to derive variational principles in acoustics. To this end, we review the construction of an action in the Lagrangian frame for compressible nondissipative fluids. We explore the consequences of material relabeling symmetry and the associated pseudomomentum balance, together with the boundary and jump conditions it implies. Via perturbation expansions, we show how to recover the equations of linear acoustics from each variational principle, the material frame yielding in addition the second-order acoustic energy balance. We show how weakly nonlinear approximations with variational structure emerge in each frame, in both cases for a general barotropic fluid, with the nonlinearity carried by the fluid's parameter $B/A$ and the perfect-gas results recovered as a special case.

</details>

*Emeric Boigné,Mohamed M. Ahmed,Collin Foster,Edna R. Toro,Dilworth Y. Parkinson,Alastair A. MacDowell,Harold S. Barnard,Francesco Panerai,Chiara Saggese,Matthias Ihme*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体燃料燃烧中输运、化学动力学与结构响应之间的耦合理解需要高空间分辨率的原位测量，而现有手段难以同时实现高分辨率成像与可控热解条件的结合。

Abstract: Understanding the coupling between transport, chemical kinetics, and structural response within solid fuel combustion requires in situ measurements at high spatial resolution. To examine the dynamics of biomass pyrolysis, we optimize synchrotron X-ray micro-computed tomography ($μ$CT), and conduct 3D particle-resolved simulations. A micro-focused heating cell with controlled heating and flow rate is employed, achieving temperatures of up to 1240 K and 3D imaging at 3.24 $μ$m spatial and sub-minute temporal resolutions. The pyrolysis of three biomass materials at sample heating rates of 10-14 K/min are examined. The $μ$CT measurements capture pore deformation, cracking, anisotropic shrinkage, and provide simultaneous thermogravimetric and thermovolumetric analyses to examine specific secondary pyrolysis pathways. Complementary 3D simulations considering detailed kinetics and structural response reproduce the key trends observed, and identify deficiencies in capturing simultaneous mass and volume losses during pyrolysis.

### [79] [Dynamical slowdown, bottlenecks, and multiscaling in Voigt-regularised turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27355)
*Anikat Kankaria,Bikram Pal,Edriss S. Titi,Samriddhi Sankar Ray*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过Voigt正则化SABRA壳模型及对应Voigt-Navier-Stokes（NSV）方程的DNS模拟，研究了湍流中的瓶颈（bottleneck）形成机制。研究发现三个不同的谱区域（惯性区、中间平衡区、高热区），并揭示了瓶颈形成可能与尺度依赖的动力学减速和初始热化（thermalisation）相关，而非纯粹源于耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清湍流中瓶颈形成的物理起源。Voigt正则化在引入尺度依赖的非线性相互作用减速的同时不增强耗散，为分离非线性传递与热化行为之间的相互作用提供了天然的研究框架。

Method: 采用Voigt正则化SABRA壳模型（shell model）进行数值模拟，并对相应的Voigt-Navier-Stokes（NSV）方程进行直接数值模拟（DNS），分析能量谱、等时与多时统计量（equal- and multi-time statistics），以比较壳模型与DNS的结果。

Result: 识别出三个截然不同的谱区域：惯性区（k<k_I）、与部分热化相关的中间类平衡区（k_I<k<k_{II}）以及Voigt贡献主导守恒不变量（conserved invariant）的高k热区（k>k_{II}）。k_{II}~1/α标记进入高k热区的交叉点，k_I则标志着热化行为的起始。统计结果显示小尺度上间歇性（intermittency）被渐进抑制并趋向高斯性（Gaussianity），动态多重标度（dynamic multiscaling）在平衡区退化为简单标度。当正则化参数α远小于耗散特征长度时，Voigt模型复现了与Navier-Stokes（NS）方程一致的惯性区湍流谱和统计特性。

Conclusion: 瓶颈形成可能与尺度依赖的动力学减速和初始热化有关，而非单纯的耗散性起源。对于小α，Voigt模型是Navier-Stokes（NS）方程的一种优秀实用近似，同时壳模型和DNS在三个谱区域的行为上呈一致的定性趋势。

Abstract: We investigate bottleneck formation in turbulence using the Voigt-regularised SABRA model and DNS of the corresponding Voigt-Navier-Stokes (NSV) equations. The Voigt regularisation introduces a scale-dependent slowdown of nonlinear interactions without enhancing dissipation, providing a natural setting to study the interplay between nonlinear transfer and thermalised behaviour. We find three distinct spectral regimes: an inertial range at $k<k_I$, an intermediate equilibrium-like range associated with partial thermalisation for $k_I<k<k_{II}$, and a high-$k$ thermal regime for $k>k_{II}$, where the Voigt contribution dominates the conserved invariant. The crossover to the high-$k$ regime occurs at $k_{II}\sim 1/α$, while $k_I$ marks the onset of thermalised behaviour. Equal and multi-time statistics reveal a progressive suppression of intermittency and a tendency towards Gaussianity at small scales, together with a transition from dynamic multiscaling in the turbulent regime to simple scaling in the equilibrium ranges. The shell model resolves these three regimes over a broad range of scales, while DNS of the corresponding NSV equations reproduces the same qualitative trends, including bottleneck formation, delayed cascade completion, reduced intermittency, and a tendency towards Gaussianity at small scales. We find that bottleneck formation might be associated with scale-dependent dynamical slowdown and incipient thermalisation, rather than being purely dissipative in origin. We provide strong evidence that, in the regime where the regularization parameter $α$ is much smaller than the dissipation length scale, the Voigt model reproduces the same inertial-range turbulent regime and turbulence statistics as the Navier-Stokes (NS) equations. This provides evidence that the Voigt model constitutes an excellent practical approximation to the NS equations for small $α$.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究一类随机递归单纯复形（random recursive simplicial complex）的生成与增长特性：每一步加入一个新顶点，并与一个随机选取的已有单纯形（simplex）连接形成新单纯形，同时补充其所有面以保持复形结构。当已有单纯形的选择在维度小于m的单纯形中均匀进行时，任意允许维度d≤m的单纯形数量S_d是渐近自平均（asymptotically self-averaging）的随机变量，据此可确定当顶点数发散时S_d平均值的渐近增长律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机递归单纯复形的渐近结构特性，特别是各维单纯形数量的增长规律，并补充研究度分布、极端事件概率及首个顶点的特征，以刻画这类高维随机结构的全局行为。

Method: 通过每一步添加一个新顶点及由该顶点与随机选取的现有低维单纯形构成的单纯形的递归构造过程，利用渐近自平均性质将随机变量的行为约化为其平均值的确定性增长规律进行分析。

Result: 证明了当选择规则为在维度小于m的单纯形中均匀选取时，任意允许维度d≤m的单纯形数量S_d满足渐近自平均性质，并据此导出了顶点数趋于无穷时S_d平均值的渐近增长律。

Conclusion: 随机递归单纯复形在均匀选取规则下的各维单纯形数量表现出渐近自平均行为，其增长规律可由平均值确定；论文同时考察了度分布、极端结果概率和首个顶点的性质，但仍需进一步探索其他选择规则下的行为。

Abstract: We investigate random recursive simplicial complexes growing by adding, at each step, a vertex together with a simplex formed by joining the new vertex with a randomly chosen existing simplex. We also add all faces of the new simplex to ensure that the resulting object remains a simplicial complex. If the choice of an existing simplex is uniform among simplices of dimension $<m$, the number $S_d$ of simplices of any admissible dimension $d\leq m$ is an asymptotically self-averaging random variable. This feature allows us to determine the asymptotic growth law of the average of $S_d$ when the number of vertices diverges. We also probe the degree distribution, examine the probabilities of various extreme outcomes, and analyze the characteristics of the first vertex.

</details>
