### [81] [Classical and Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning for Trigger-Like Event Selection on CMS Open Data: An Eight-Qubit, PCA-Constrained Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26224)
*Tariq Mahmood,Muhammad Awais Rafique,Talab Hussain,Juan Pablo Perez Aguilar,Alfredo Raya,Muhammad Ahsan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在高能物理（high-energy physics）触发（trigger）型二分类任务中，基于CMS开放数据比较了四种经典机器学习模型与四种混合量子模型的性能，均受限于8量子比特（qubit）资源预算。结果显示经典人工神经网络（artificial neural network）最优（准确率93.53%，ROC-AUC 0.9819），量子侧以量子卷积网络（quantum convolutional network）最优（准确率90.89%，ROC-AUC 0.9731），研究定位为受控参照基准而非量子优势的宣称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高能物理触发系统中，需要在严格的延迟（latency）和带宽（bandwidth）预算下保留稀有感兴趣事件并丢弃海量背景事件，因此评估经典与量子机器学习模型在该任务上的表现具有实际意义。本研究旨在提供一个公平受控的对照基准（controlled reference point），而非宣称量子优势（quantum advantage）。

Method: 研究基于CMS开放数据构造触发型二分类任务，标签由不变质量（invariant-mass）窗口定义；输入结合重建运动学量（reconstructed kinematics）与物理动机派生变量（pseudorapidity差、环绕方位角差、角分离、总横动量）。比较四种经典模型（支持向量机、人工神经网络、卷积网络、长短期记忆网络）与四种混合量子模型；量子模型在8量子比特固定预算、主成分压缩至16特征及态矢量模拟（state-vector simulation）下运行。所有模型共享相同的分层划分（stratified split）、预处理和决策阈值，性能通过准确率、ROC-AUC、F1分数、精确率和召回率报告。

Result: 最强的经典模型为人工神经网络，达到93.53%准确率和0.9819的ROC-AUC；最强的量子模型为量子卷积网络，达到90.89%准确率和0.9731的ROC-AUC，量子神经网络紧随其后。量子核（quantum-kernel）和递归量子方法表现落后，表明在给定预算下可训练的混合嵌入（trainable hybrid embedding）更优。

Conclusion: 在固定资源预算下，经典人工神经网络在触发型分类任务上表现略优于所有量子模型，而量子卷积网络是量子方法中的最佳选择。研究定位为受控参照点，并不主张量子优势。

Abstract: Event triggering sits at the heart of high-energy physics, where the rare events of interest must be retained while an overwhelming background is discarded under tight latency and bandwidth budgets. This work compares four classical machine learning models, namely a support vector machine, an artificial neural network, a convolutional network and a long short-term memory network, with four hybrid quantum counterparts, on a trigger-like binary classification task built from CMS open data. The label is defined by an invariant-mass window, and the inputs combine reconstructed kinematics with physics-motivated derived variables: the pseudorapidity difference, the wrapped azimuthal difference, the angular separation and the total transverse momentum. The quantum models run under a fixed resource budget of eight qubits, a principal-component compression to sixteen features and state-vector simulation. Every model shares the same stratified split, the same preprocessing and a common decision threshold, and performance is reported through accuracy, ROC-AUC, F1-score, precision and recall. The strongest classical model is the artificial neural network, at 93.53 percent accuracy and 0.9819 ROC-AUC, while the strongest quantum model is the quantum convolutional network, at 90.89 percent accuracy and 0.9731 ROC-AUC, with the quantum neural network close behind. The quantum-kernel and recurrent quantum approaches trail both, which places the trainable hybrid embeddings ahead within this budget. The study is meant as a controlled reference point rather than a claim of quantum advantage.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强度前沿（intensity frontier）的加速器实验需要精确的靶核建模以获得可靠的信号预言，而此前对轻暗物质中介子产生信号率的计算在核建模精度上可能存在不足，因此需要引入更精确的多体从头算方法。

Method: 利用手征有效场论相互作用构建的多体从头算方法，计算了电子定点靶实验中轻暗物质中介子产生的信号率；同时考虑弹性（elastic）与准弹性（quasi elastic）散射两种机制，基于从头算核弹性形状因子（elastic form factors）和谱函数（spectral functions），通过蒙特卡罗事件生成器（Monte Carlo event generator）实现计算不同电子束能量下三个代表性原子核的反应截面。

Result: 与常用唯象参数化相比，准弹性处理导致信号产额最多增加约两个数量级；对于较轻的中介子，两种方法的结果一致。

Conclusion: 从头算多体方法对靶核的精确建模能够显著提高准弹性散射下的信号预言，尤其在重中介子区域可能修正此前唯象估计的不足，表明精确核结构输入对暗物质搜索实验信号预测具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accelerator-based experiments at the intensity frontier, in which a high-energy beam impinges on a nuclear target, serve as powerful probes of the light dark matter paradigm. Such experiments require precise modeling of the target nucleus for reliable signal predictions. We present the application of a many-body ab initio method to calculate light dark matter mediator production signal rates at electron fixed-target experiments, using chiral effective field theory interactions. Considering both elastic and quasi elastic scattering, we compute cross sections using a Monte Carlo event generator implementation informed by ab initio nuclear elastic form factors and spectral functions for three representative nuclei, $^{20}$Ne, $^{34}$Si, and $^{56}$Fe, at varying electron beam energies. We compare our results to a commonly used phenomenological parameterization, finding an increased signal yield by up to two orders of magnitude with our quasi elastic treatment and an agreement for lighter mediators.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文在矢量门户（vector-portal，暗光子）和标量门户（scalar-portal，希格斯门户）两种现实模型中，系统比较了亚GeV暗物质（dark matter, DM）的直接探测（direct detection, DD）与间接探测（indirect detection, ID）约束的互补性，重点对比了DAMIC-M实验的电子散射上限及未来投影与多波段X射线/伽马射线和宇宙线观测以及未来MeV望远镜COSI给出的湮灭率限制。研究发现，矢量门户模型下互补性最佳，尤其在重介子情形下DAMIC-M对3–20 MeV范围内的暗物质约束可与间接探测相当甚至更好；其余情形下间接探测普遍更具约束力，部分质量区间能探测到中微子地板（neutrino floor）以下的参数空间。


<details>

Result: 对于矢量门户模型中矢量介子重于暗物质粒子的情形，当前DAMIC-M约束在约3–20 MeV暗物质质量范围内与（部分天体物理配置下甚至超过）综合间接探测限制相当；其他情形和质区间内ID普遍更具约束力，且部分质量区间可探测中微子地板以下的参数空间。

Abstract: We analyze the complementarity between direct detection (DD) and indirect detection (ID) searches of sub-GeV dark matter (DM) in the context of two realistic models: the vector-portal (dark photon) and scalar-portal (higgs-portal) models. For DD we focus on the latest constraints on the DM-electron scattering from the present DAMIC-M experiment as well as its future projection. For ID we consider several existing X-rays/gamma-rays and cosmic-ray ($e^\pm$) observations as well as the upcoming MeV telescope COSI to obtain the corresponding bounds and projections on the DM annihilation rate, which are then translated in terms of the DM-electron scattering cross-section and compared with the DD bounds/projections. We properly take into account astrophysical and galactic propagation uncertainties. We find that the complementarity between DAMIC-M and ID works best for the vector-portal model, especially for the case where the vector mediator is heavier than the DM particle: in this case, the present DAMIC-M bound becomes comparable to (or better than, for some astrophysical configurations) the combined ID limit for $3\,{\rm MeV} \lesssim m_{\rm DM} \lesssim 20\,{\rm MeV}$. For other cases and mass ranges, the ID is in general more constraining and can also, for some mass ranges, constrain the parameter space below the neutrino floor, which is inaccessible to DD experiments.

*Wen Yin*

TL;DR: 本文提出一类名为化学势匹配暗物质（chemical-potential-matched dark matter, μDM）的新模型，通过使重子数化学势 $|μ_B|$ 与暗物质质量 $m_{\rm DM}$ 同数量级，建立重子-暗物质丰度巧合（baryon-dark matter coincidence）的新路径。以 Affleck-Dine 场的激发态作为暗物质的具体示例，并讨论了自发电弱重子生成等关联机制；无熵稀释时暗物质质量落在 eV–keV 范围，借助玻色增强受激发射可实现冷动量分布，而熵稀释可将质量上限扩展到 MeV 量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的热暗物质（hot dark matter）丰度关系中，暗物质能量熵比满足 $ρ_{\rm DM}/s\sim 10^{-3}m_{\rm DM}$，而重子不对称可写为 $ρ_{\rm baryon}/s\sim 10^{-4}|μ_B|$。当两者巧合时，自然引出 $|μ_B|\sim m_{\rm DM}$ 的关联，这为解释暗物质与重子丰度的一致性（coincidence）提供了新的理论动机。

Method: 提出 μDM 模型类别，其核心设定是令重子数化学势与暗物质质量同阶。以 Affleck-Dine 场为例给出具体构造，其激发态构成暗物质；同时探讨与轴子类粒子（axion-like-particle）壁相关的自发电弱重子生成（spontaneous electroweak baryogenesis）；分析无熵稀释与有熵稀释两种情景下暗物质的质量范围及动量分布特征。

Result: 在无熵稀释的情况下，该巧合机制一般将暗物质质量指向 eV–keV 范围；若热产生由玻色增强的受激发射（Bose-enhanced stimulated emission）主导，其动量分布仍可为冷的。若在暗物质与重子产额均已固定后进行熵稀释，质量上限可扩展到 MeV 量级。

Conclusion: 通过 μDM 机制，重子数化学势与暗物质质量的匹配为暗物质-重子丰度巧合提供了一条全新的理论路线，同时给出了具体的模型构造与质量/冷度约束，为后续实验检验和模型扩展指明了方向。

Abstract: For a relativistically decoupled thermal relic, the dark-matter energy-to-entropy ratio scales as $ρ_{\rm DM}/s\sim 10^{-3}m_{\rm DM}$. This is the familiar hot-dark-matter abundance relation. Immediately after weak-sphaleron freeze-out, the baryon asymmetry can be parametrized as $ρ_{\rm baryon}/s\sim 10^{-4}|μ_B|$. Here $μ_B$ is the baryon-number chemical potential. We propose a new class of models, called chemical-potential-matched dark matter ($μ$DM), in which $|μ_B|\sim m_{\rm DM}$, thereby providing a new route to the baryon-dark matter coincidence. As a concrete example of $μ$DM, we consider an Affleck-Dine field whose excitations constitute dark matter. We also briefly discuss spontaneous electroweak baryogenesis associated with axion-like-particle walls. In the absence of entropy dilution, this coincidence mechanism generically points to dark matter in the eV-keV mass range. Its momentum distribution can nevertheless be cold if thermal production is dominated by Bose-enhanced stimulated emission. Alternatively, entropy dilution after both the dark-matter and baryon yields are fixed allows masses up to the MeV scale.

</details>


### [85] [Simulations and flavour-scheme studies for Higgs-boson production in association with charm quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26863)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 文章对H+c和H+b过程进行NLO+PS级别的完整研究，系统对比重味数方案（massive scheme）与轻味数方案（massless scheme），量化标度不确定性（scale uncertainty）与味数方案不确定性（flavour-scheme uncertainty），并评估干涉效应（interference）与圈诱导贡献（loop-induced contributions）的影响。针对粲夸克汤川融合（charm Yukawa fusion）产生的c̄cH通道，将NLO+PS与四味数方案下的NNLO+PS计算在13.6 TeV能标下进行对比。

Result: 文章系统地量化了不同味数方案之间的预言差异及其不确定性来源，并针对c̄cH产生给出了NLO+PS与NNLO+PS计算之间的对比结果。基于这些比较，首次提出了面向LHC实验分析的c̄cH产生模拟实用建议。

Conclusion: 作者基于系统性的数值比较，首次为LHC分析中c̄cH产生的模拟提供了具体推荐的方案选择与实践指南，这有助于提高未来实验测量中对希格斯-粲夸克汤川耦合（Hcc Yukawa coupling）约束的可靠性。

Abstract: We present a detailed NLO+PS study of Higgs-boson production in association with charm and bottom quarks, with particular focus on the modelling of the H+c final state. We systematically compare predictions in massive and massless flavour schemes, quantify scale and flavour-scheme uncertainties, and assess the impact of interference and loop-induced contributions. Special emphasis is placed on the Higgs production via charm Yukawa fusion, for which we compare NLO+PS predictions with a NNLO+PS calculation of $c\bar{c}H$ production in the four-flavour scheme at 13.6 TeV. Based on these results, we provide the first practical recommendations for the simulation of $c\bar{c}H$ production in LHC analyses.

### [86] [Exploring $Z/γ$-mediated heavy FCNCs at the FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27183)
*Abhik Sarkar,Subhajit Kala,Amir Subba,Yu Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，利用偶极子与希格斯流算符，研究由中性规范玻色子（Z/γ）介导的第三代与第一、二代之间的味破缺跃迁，并通过最优可观测量技术（OOT）评估未来正负电子对撞机FCC-ee在不同质心能量下的探测灵敏度，同时结合低能味观测量和重费米子衰变道给出互补约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（SM）的味结构是粒子物理中悬而未决的核心问题之一，第三代费米子因其显著更大的质量与较不精确测量的性质而尤为引人关注，这使得第三代味跃迁成为探寻超越标准模型（BSM）物理的重要窗口。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下，利用偶极子算符与希格斯流算符构建味破缺相互作用；采用最优可观测量技术（OOT）计算FCC-ee在不同质心能量下的最优灵敏度；从低能味破缺观测量与重费米子衰变道推导互补约束。

Result: 研究发现偶极子贡献之间存在特征性干涉图样，其依赖底层味跃迁类型，并在Z极点与更高能量FCC-ee阶段表现出不同行为；投影结果显示FCC-ee对若干相互作用具有改进的灵敏度，对若干其他相互作用则与现有味约束相当。

Conclusion: FCC-ee能够提供对电弱能标味破缺相互作用的直接且互补的探测手段，系统性评估其不同能量阶段有助于全面认识其探索味破缺现象的潜力，并凸显其与低能味实验之间的互补性。

Abstract: The flavor structure of the Standard Model (SM) remains one of the most compelling questions in particle physics, with the third generation being particularly intriguing due to its significantly larger masses and comparatively less precisely measured properties. These features make third-generation flavor transitions particularly interesting in context of search for physics beyond the SM. In this work, we investigate flavor-violating transitions between the third and the first two generations, mediated by the neutral gauge bosons ($Z/γ$), within the framework of the SM Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), using dipole and Higgs-current operators. We determine the optimal sensitivities using the optimal observable technique (OOT) at different center-of-mass energies of the upcoming Future Circular Collider in the $e^+e^-$ mode (FCC-ee). We further derive complementary constraints on the relevant SMEFT operators from low-energy flavor-violating observables and heavy fermion decay channels. Our analysis also reveals characteristic interference patterns among the dipole contributions, which depend on the underlying flavor transition and exhibit distinct behavior between the $Z$ pole and higher-energy FCC-ee stages. The FCC-ee provides a complementary and direct probe of flavor-violating interactions at the electroweak scale, with the projections showing improved sensitivity for several interactions and comparable sensitivity to existing flavor constraints for several others. This highlights the importance of a systematic assessment across the different FCC-ee energy stages, which provides a comprehensive picture of its potential to explore flavor-violating phenomena and its complementarity with low-energy flavor experiments.

</details>

Method: 本文采用历史综述（historical survey）的方式，以主题演讲（keynote lecture）形式回顾粒子物理学从J/ψ发现之前的前史阶段到1980年代初期的关键发展。

Abstract: The 4th International Symposium on the History of Particle Physics spanned two decades, from approximately 1980 to 2000. In this opening keynote lecture, I survey developments in particle physics from the prehistory of the $J/ψ$ discovery to the early nineteen-eighties. I offer context for the contributions that follow in the symposium program.

### [88] [Fermi gas of domain-wall Skyrmions in QCD in a strong magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26608)
*Patrick Copinger,Minoru Eto,Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁化背景下手征孤子晶格（chiral soliton lattice）中产生的费米子域壁斯格朗子（Fermionic domain-wall Skyrmion）的电磁屏蔽特性，发现零温下斯格朗子密度在垂直于磁场的圆盘上饱和至费米动量，并将其与量子色动力学（QCD）中的重子及有限温度屏蔽效应联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解域壁斯格朗子的电磁屏蔽特性有助于揭示磁场与化学势相图中重子密度的分布规律，为连接低能手征有效理论与基础量子色动力学之间的物理提供线索。

Method: 采用模有效理论（moduli effective theory）分析零温下的斯格朗子密度；将手征微扰理论（chiral perturbation theory）与量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics）耦合以研究有限温度屏蔽，并利用德拜质量/长度（Debye mass/length）进行预言。

Result: 零温下斯格朗子密度在垂直于磁场的孤子晶格圆盘上饱和至费米动量；有限温度下预测了静态费米子域壁斯格朗子的屏蔽效应，其由德拜质量/长度综合描述。

Conclusion: 域壁斯格朗子作为手征孤子晶格基态与QCD重子相关联，其电磁屏蔽特性为磁场-化学势相图上的重子密度提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Fermionic domain-wall Skyrmions arise in the chiral soliton lattice of two flavor chiral perturbation theory in a magnetic field as the ground state and are associated with baryons of the underlying theory of quantum chromodynamics. We analyze the electromagnetic screening characteristics of domain-wall Skyrmions that give insight into baryon density on the magnetic field and chemical potential phase diagram. At zero temperature, using the moduli effective theory, it is found Skyrmion density is saturated up to the Fermi momenta on the disk of the chiral soliton lattice orthogonal to the magnetic field. We further study chiral perturbation theory coupled to quantum electrodynamics leading to finite temperature screening, wherein a static fermionic domain-wall Skyrmion screening is predicted through a combined Debye mass/length.

</details>


### [89] [Advances in the holographic description of hot and dense QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27023)
*Nicolas Kovensky,Andreas Schmitt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾并扩展了规范-引力对偶（gauge-gravity duality）框架下，Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型对非零温度与化学势下类QCD（QCD-like）相结构的研究，首次构建了介于BEC与BCS区域之间的π介子相的全息模型，并指出该相为亚稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD在非零温度与化学势下的相图是强相互作用物理的核心问题，但传统格点方法在有限密度区域面临符号问题（sign problem），需要借助全息方法从弦论出发获取定性和半定量的洞察。

Method: 基于Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型（一种自弦论出发的top-down方法），统一处理介子（meson）、重子（baryon）和夸克（quark）自由度，通过规范-引力对偶将强相互作用的相结构映射为引力侧的计算。

Result: 在相图中定位了π介子凝聚（pion condensation）、重子物质（baryonic matter）和夸克离子物质（quarkyonic matter）等相；首次构建了介于BEC与BCS区域之间的π介子相的全息构造，但该相被发现是亚稳态（metastable）的。

Conclusion: 作者评估了该全息方法的预测能力及其弱点与局限性，并讨论了在现有模型框架及更广泛背景下全息QCD（holographic QCD）的未来研究方向。

Abstract: We review and extend recent progress on QCD-like phases at nonzero temperature and chemical potentials within the gauge-gravity duality. We focus on the Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto model, a top-down approach based on string theory which allows for a unified treatment of mesons, baryons and quarks. Phases such as pion condensation, baryonic and quarkyonic matter are located in the phase diagram, and we discuss predictions as well as weaknesses and limitations of this holographic approach. One of the novelties included in this review is the first holographic construction of a pionic phase between BEC and BCS regimes, which however is found to be metastable. We also discuss future directions in holographic QCD, within the model used here and in a wider context.

</details>


### [90] [Chiral soliton lattice in inhomogeneous magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27319)
*Tomas Brauner,Ramkumar Radhakrishnan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子色动力学（QCD）在非均匀外部磁场下的基态性质，探索是否仍然存在由中性π介子凝聚形成的空间调制结构——手征孤子晶格（CSL）。通过低能有效场论分析，作者在零π介子质量极限下完整刻画了支持CSL类基态的磁场构型，并在数值方面给出了有限质量情形的初步结果，表明即使磁场弯曲削弱了凝聚能量增益，CSL型基态仍然可能存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究已表明，均匀强磁场下QCD基态可支持手征孤子晶格（chiral soliton lattice, CSL）这种空间调制的中性π介子凝聚态。本文旨在探究在实际物理场景（如重离子碰撞和中子星）中磁场往往是非均匀的情形下，是否存在类似的序化基态。

Method: 采用QCD的低能有效场论方法，在强磁场下仅保留中性π介子作为唯一的低能自由度。在π介子质量为零的极限下，对支持CSL类基态的磁场进行完整分类；对一类简单但无穷族的磁场构型，解析求解对应的CSL态。对非零π介子质量情形，则对能量泛函执行全数值最小化，给出样本数值结果。

Result: 在零质量极限下，实现了支持CSL类基态磁场的完备刻画，并对简单无穷族磁场解析求出对应的CSL态。数值结果揭示了非均匀磁场与均匀磁场及零π介子质量情形之间的定性差异。主要结论为：尽管弯曲磁场通常降低中性π介子凝聚带来的能量增益，但CSL型基态仍然可能出现。此外，作为副产品，论文还在均匀磁场与有限体积下绘制了QCD相图中CSL相的位置。

Conclusion: 非均匀外部磁场下CSL型基态是存在的，磁场弯曲对凝聚有抑制作用但不会完全消除CSL序。该结论为在重离子碰撞和中子星等现实物理环境中探测手征孤子晶格提供了理论依据。

Abstract: It has been known that in sufficiently strong uniform magnetic fields, the ground state of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) supports a spatially modulated condensate of neutral pions, dubbed chiral soliton lattice (CSL). In this paper, we investigate whether a similar ordered ground state might exist when the external magnetic field is nonuniform, as appropriate for potential phenomenological applications, including heavy-ion collisions and neutron stars. To that end, we use the low-energy effective field theory of QCD, restricted to the neutral pions as the sole low-energy degrees of freedom in strong magnetic fields. In the limit of vanishing pion mass, we achieve a complete characterization of magnetic fields supporting a CSL-like ground state. Moreover, for a simple but infinite family of magnetic fields, we find the corresponding CSL state analytically. Going away from the massless limit requires full numerical minimization of the energy functional. Here we provide some sample numerical results, focusing on the qualitative differences as compared to the situations with a uniform magnetic field or a vanishing pion mass. The main conclusion remains unchanged: while bending the magnetic field typically reduces the energy gain due to the neutral pion condensation, a CSL-type ground state is still possible. As a byproduct of our work, we map the location of the CSL phase in the phase diagram of QCD in a uniform magnetic field and finite volume.

</details>

### [91] [Packora: Systematic Design for Generative Molecular Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26962)
*Nayoung Kim,Kiyoung Seong,Sungsoo Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Packora，一个基于流的生成模型（flow-based generative model），用于分子晶体结构预测（crystal structure prediction, CSP）。该模型从分子图（molecular graph）出发联合预测原子坐标和晶格，支持多组分和有机金属晶体，并可在单模型中条件化任意子集的构象、立体化学标签和空间群信息。Packora在六个生成基准和排序基准上均优于基线，实现了最佳匹配预算覆盖率、更高的实验形式恢复率、更低的实验形式排名和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体结构预测在制药、农化和有机电子等领域至关重要，因为分子构象和堆积的细微差异会显著影响材料性质。现有方法在生成质量和端到端排序性能上仍有不足，需要更强大的生成模型来同时预测原子坐标和晶格。

Method: Packora采用基于流的生成模型（flow-based generative model）架构，从分子图联合预测原子坐标和晶格。模型支持多组分和有机金属晶体，并允许在单个模型内对分子构象子集、立体化学标签和空间群信息进行条件化。作者系统研究了架构、训练、条件化、推理和扩展策略，提出了一种基于可缓存成对推理的有效设计方案，涉及训练目标、数值求解器选择、条件化丢弃（conditioning dropout）以及成对与单表示之间的平衡扩展。

Result: Packora在结构生成和排序（ranking）两个基准上均优于基线模型，在全部六个生成基准上取得最佳匹配预算覆盖率（matched-budget coverage）。在排序方面，Packora获得更高的实验形式恢复率（experimental-form recovery）、更低的实验形式排名以及更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: Packora展示了基于流的生成模型在分子晶体结构预测中的有效性和通用性，通过分离评估生成质量与端到端排序性能（参考CCDC CSP盲测设计），并在系统性消融研究中确定了关键设计选择，为后续CSP模型研究提供了可参考的架构和训练策略。

Abstract: Molecular crystal structure prediction (CSP) is important in pharmaceuticals, agrochemicals, and organic electronics, where subtle differences in molecular conformation and packing can strongly affect material properties. We present Packora, a flow-based generative model for molecular CSP that jointly predicts atomic coordinates and the lattice from molecular graphs. Packora supports multi-component and organometallic crystals and can condition on any subset of molecular conformers, stereochemical labels, and space-group information within a single model. Inspired by the CCDC CSP blind test, we evaluate generation and ranking separately, using generation to isolate generator quality and ranking to measure end-to-end performance under a common relaxation and ranking pipeline. We also systematically study architecture, training, conditioning, inference, and scaling, identifying an effective design based on cacheable pairwise reasoning, training objective and numerical solver choices, conditioning dropout, and balanced scaling of pairwise and single representations. Packora outperforms the baselines on both structure generation and ranking benchmarks, achieving the best matched-budget coverage across all six generation benchmarks, as well as higher experimental-form recovery, lower experimental-form ranks, and faster convergence in ranking.

</details>

### [92] [Critical Properties and Glass Transitions in Randomly Coupled Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26279)
*Amer Al-Hiyasat*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由N个标量场经淬火随机相互作用耦合构成的体系，相当于将自旋玻璃（spin glass）中的自旋提升为d维场。当N较大时，随机耦合矩阵产生一族高斯普适类（Gaussian universality classes），其临界指数由谱边缘附近的特征值密度行为决定。对于Wigner矩阵，关联长度在临界点发散但磁化率保持有限，单圈复制分析支持上临界维度为一。在可精确求解的球面变体中，d>1时热容在相变处跳跃，且整个玻璃相中关联保持临界形式，产生无Goldstone模的普适标度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在探索自旋玻璃与d维场论之间的联系，理解当自旋被推广为场时随机耦合系统所展现的临界行为及其普适类。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：对一般Wigner随机矩阵情形进行单圈复制分析（one-loop replica analysis）；对可精确求解的球面变体（spherical variant）模型进行严格解析。

Result: Wigner矩阵情形下，关联长度在临界点发散但磁化率有限，且单圈复制分析表明上临界维度为一；球面变体中，d>1时热容在相变处出现跳跃，玻璃相内关联保持临界形式，呈现无Goldstone模的普适标度不变性。

Conclusion: 随机耦合标量场体系在N较大时由谱边特征值密度决定临界指数，且存在一类无需Goldstone模即呈现标度不变性的临界相。

Abstract: We study an assembly of $N$ scalar fields coupled via a quenched random interaction, equivalent to a spin glass whose spins are promoted to $d$-dimensional fields. When $N$ is large, the random coupling matrix produces a family of Gaussian universality classes whose critical exponents are determined by the behavior of the eigenvalue density near the spectral edge. For a Wigner matrix, the correlation length diverges at criticality but the susceptibility remains finite, and one-loop replica analysis supports an upper critical dimension of unity. In an exactly solvable spherical variant, the heat capacity jumps at the transition in $d>1$, and correlations remain pinned to their critical form throughout the glass phase, giving generic scale invariance despite the absence of a Goldstone mode.

</details>


### [93] [Information-theoretic formulation of the Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26339)
*Enrico Maria Fenoaltea,Riccardo Piombo,Aurelio Patelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率与信息论框架的旅行商问题（Traveling Salesman Problem, TSP）求解方法，通过最大熵（maximum entropy）分布结合可微分解环（cycle）约束的均值场（mean-field）近似，将硬性全局约束转化为可处理的软约束，在多项式时间内获得与已知最优解接近的候选回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行商问题要求以最短路线遍历所有城市一次，其难点在于局部规则（每个城市恰好访问一次）与全局硬性约束（所有城市构成单一回路）的共存。作者指出，在概率和信息论框架下，局部规则可通过顶点级约束实现，而全局约束无法由独立边概率捕获，因此需要新的概率建模方法来弥合这一结构性障碍。

Method: 作者在图上定义了一个最大熵概率分布，其中边成本和度数（degree）约束生成类似于分配（assignment）问题的系综，同时引入描述硬性全局约束的项将该系综向哈密顿回路（Hamiltonian cycle）方向倾斜。为使构造可计算，作者推导了以边占用率为变量的均值场近似，并实现了可微分的回路惩罚项以抑制子回路（sub-tours），最终形成自洽的数值迭代过程。

Result: 该方法在合成数据集合TSPLIB实例上进行了验证。算法以多项式时间收敛到连通的回路，在大部分实例中可与已知最优解匹配，其余情形仅存在较小的相对差距。

Conclusion: 除具有竞争力的求解性能外，该框架提供了一个处理硬约束的通用途径，可将困难的组合优化问题（combinatorial optimization problems）约化为更简单的问题，同时在输出候选回路之外还编码了竞争边和简并解的概率矩阵，为理解和求解此类问题提供了信息论视角。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) asks for the shortest route to visit a set of cities exactly once. It combines a simple local rule - each city must be visited once - with a hard, global constraint- all cities must be traversed within a single cycle. We cast the problem within a probabilistic, information-theoretic framework. The coexistence of local and global constraints is precisely what makes the problem difficult to address in this framework: the local rule can be enforced through vertex-level constraints, whereas the global constraint cannot be captured by independent edge probabilities. We show that this obstacle can be overcome by defining a maximum-entropy probability distribution over graphs, in which edge costs and degree constraints generate an assignment-like ensemble, and a global term, describing the hard constraint, tilts this ensemble toward Hamiltonian cycles. To make the construction tractable, we derive a mean-field approximation in terms of edge occupancies and implement a differentiable cycle penalty that suppresses sub-tours. This leads to a self-consistent numerical procedure whose output is not only a candidate tour but also a probability matrix encoding competing edges and degenerate solutions. We test the method on synthetic ensembles and on TSPLIB instances. The algorithm converges to connected tours in polynomial time, matching the best-known solution in the majority of instances and remaining within a small relative gap otherwise. Beyond its competitive performance, the proposed framework offers a general approach for handling hard constraints while reducing hard combinatorial optimization problems to simpler ones.

</details>


### [94] [When dissipative steady states admit thermodynamic occupation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26621)
*Tetsu Ichitsubo*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一种扇区分割几何（sector-separated geometry），在不消除耗散的条件下克服了非平衡稳态（NESS）中有限定态环流与全局精确速率比场不能共存的不相容性，从而为耗散NESS建立热力学占据定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡稳态（non-equilibrium steady states, NESSs）通常缺乏热力学占据律，因为对同一马尔可夫生成元（Markov generator），有限的定态环流（stationary circulation）和全局精确的速率比场（exact rate-ratio field）无法同时存在。作者旨在构造一种几何结构以克服这一固有不相容性，同时不抑制耗散。

Method: 作者引入熵产生暴露-分离（exposure-and-separation）机制，将熵产生态0从条件占据流形中排除但保留在耗散全图中；在强偏置/快速重置（SR）极限下物理回路变为有效马尔可夫过程。对热力学完备的条件流形，采用自主再分布（AR）消除残余空转环流，使速率比一形式精确；通过热力学校准得到占据概率表达，并证明全图概率可精确分解。

Result: 在SR极限下动力学因子趋近于1，占据概率p_α趋近于玻尔兹曼型分布e^{X_α}/Z，而耗散保持有限。在AR附近，可积性以残余环流电流的线性阶丢失，而耗散以二次阶开始。在二元零环秩（zero-cycle-rank）极限下，占据分布在维持Δμ偏置下自主再分布，得到反费米-狄拉克律（inverted Fermi-Dirac law），并应用于量子点激光器的热模糊效应。

Conclusion: 该框架为耗散NESS中的热力学占据律提供了建设性的获取条件与失败诊断，表明即使存在持续耗散，仍可构造出具有热力学形式和普适占据分布的稳态系统。

Abstract: Non-equilibrium steady states (NESSs) generally lack thermodynamic occupation laws because finite stationary circulation and a globally exact rate-ratio field cannot coexist for the same Markov generator. Here we construct a sector-separated geometry that overcomes this incompatibility without arresting dissipation. Entropy-production exposure-and-separation excludes the entropy-producing state~$0$ from the conditional occupation manifold while retaining it in the dissipative full graph; physical returns $i\to0\to0^\ast$ become effectively Markovian in the strong-bias/rapid-reset (SR) limit. For a thermodynamically complete conditional manifold, autonomous redistribution (AR) eliminates residual futile circulation, making the rate-ratio one-form exact. Thermodynamic calibration gives $X_i=β(Δμ- \mathcal F_i^{\mathrm{cost}})$ and $p_i=e^{X_i}/Z_\mathcal{C}$, with $Z_\mathcal{C}=1+\sum_i e^{X_i}$. Full-graph probabilities factorize exactly as $P_α=(1-P_0)p_α$. In the SR limit, the kinetic factor tends to unity while $p_α\to e^{X_α}/Z_\mathcal C$, yielding $P_α\to p_α$ while finite dissipation persists. Near AR, integrability is lost linearly in residual cycle current whereas dissipation begins quadratically. In the binary zero-cycle-rank limit, occupation redistributes autonomously under maintained $Δμ$ bias, yielding the inverted Fermi--Dirac law, which is applied to thermal smearing in quantum-dot lasers. The framework provides constructive acquisition conditions and failure diagnostics for thermodynamic occupation laws in dissipative NESSs.

</details>


### [95] [Brownian yet non-Gaussian diffusion through equilibrium nonlinear friction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26773)
*Jakob Mihatsch,Andreas M. Menzel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出，在正确的随机平衡条件下应用非线性摩擦（nonlinear friction），可以解释布朗但非高斯扩散（BnGD）现象在均匀环境中的产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布朗但非高斯扩散（BnGD）现象中，均方位移随时间线性增长，但位移统计在中间时间尺度呈现非高斯分布，直到长时间极限才过渡到高斯分布，这一现象的微观机制此前尚未被充分解释，尤其是在均匀环境（homogeneous environments）中的情形。

Method: 作者论证，在正确施加的随机平衡条件（stochastic equilibrium conditions）下，非线性摩擦可以解释BnGD现象，无需引入异质性环境假设。

Result: 研究表明，非线性摩擦配合正确的随机平衡条件即可在均匀环境中产生中间时间尺度的非高斯位移统计，并在长时间极限下恢复高斯分布，从而再现BnGD的典型特征。

Conclusion: 非线性摩擦机制的引入为均匀环境下的布朗但非高斯扩散现象提供了理论解释，拓展了对该现象产生条件的理解。

Abstract: In Brownian yet non-Gaussian diffusion (BnGD) the mean squared displacement grows linearly in time. However, the displacement statistics do not follow a normal distribution throughout. Typically, they are non-Gaussian at intermediate times, before they cross over to Gaussian in the long-time regime. We demonstrate that nonlinear friction under correctly applied stochastic equilibrium conditions provides an explanation of this phenomenon also for homogeneous environments.

</details>


### [96] [Quenched activity induces nonuniversal scaling in nonreciprocal XY Models and surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26919)
*Sudip Mukherjee,Abhik Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文证明淬火无序（quenched disorder）非互易随机键二维XY模型与受淬火速度切向平流的反演对称活性表面在流体动力学理论（hydrodynamic theory）上完全等价。该理论预测短程无序下XY模型存在次对数（sub-logarithmic）相位序，活性表面存在次对数或超对数（super-logarithmic）位置序，且指数随无序方差横向度连续变化；论文还论证了该XY模型可通过涡旋增殖（vortex proliferation）实现无序化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动XY模型（active XY model）与活性表面（active surface）是两个微观起源不同的典型非平衡（nonequilibrium）系统，揭示二者之间潜在的深层联系有助于统一理解非平衡系统中的无序和有序行为。

Method: 通过对比推导二者各自的流体动力学理论，并证明它们在数学结构上的完全等同性。随后基于该等效理论，对短程无序情形下的序参量（order parameter）衰减行为和相位有序化机制进行分析。

Result: 论文得到两个主要结果：其一，该等效理论预测XY模型在短程无序下呈次对数相位序，活性表面呈次对数或超对数位置序，且无序指数随无序方差横向度（transversality）连续变化，体现出非普适性（nonuniversal）特征；其二，论证了非互易随机键XY模型可以通过涡旋增殖而非仅依赖相位涨落走向无序态。

Conclusion: 非互易随机键XY模型与活性表面共享同一流体动力学框架，说明二者属于同一普适类；其中无序诱导的次对数序及非普适指数为理解非平衡无序系统提供了新的物理图像，涡旋增殖机制则揭示了该系统无序化的可能新途径。

Abstract: Active XY models and active surfaces are two paradigmatic nonequilibrium systems with distinct microscopic origins. We show that the hydrodynamic theories for a quenched-disordered nonreciprocal random bond two-dimensional XY model and an inversion-symmetric active surface tangentially advected by quenched velocities are identical. This theory predicts sub-logarithmic phase order in the XY model and sub- or super-logarithmic positional order in the surface for short-range disorder with nonuniversal exponents, which vary continuously with the degree of transversality of the disorder variance. We argue that the nonreciprocal random bond XY model can disorder through vortex proliferation.

</details>


### [97] [Localization Delocalization Transition in Diffusion with Adaptive Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27090)
*Tommer D. Keidar,Shlomi Reuveni*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文建立了自适应随机重置（adaptive resetting，即重置率依赖位置的随机重置）下扩散过程的普适分类。研究表明，重置率渐近标度 r(x)~|x|^λ 存在 λ=-2 的尖锐阈值：λ>-2 时稳态局域化并呈拉伸指数尾，λ<-2 时重置不足以局域化粒子，而恰在 λ=-2 的边际标度下，稳态出现温标依赖指数的幂律尾及有限温度退局域化转变，构成了平衡态对数势所驱动温度退局域化的非平衡对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置（stochastic resetting）能够局域化扩散并产生非平衡稳态，但空间依赖重置在何种条件下产生局域化尚不清楚。本文旨在建立该问题的完整分类框架。

Method: 对重置率依赖于空间位置 r(x) 的自适应重置扩散过程进行理论分析，考察重置率的渐近标度 r(x)~|x|^λ，并在该标度下系统分析稳态的局域化性质与尾部行为。

Result: 在 λ=-2 处发现尖锐阈值：λ>-2 时稳态局域化且具有拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）尾；λ<-2 时重置渐近过弱，无法局域化粒子。恰在 r(x)~|x|^{-2} 的边际标度下，稳态出现具有温度依赖指数的幂律尾，并发生有限温度退局域化转变。

Conclusion: 平方反比重置在非平衡体系中扮演平衡态对数势（logarithmic potential）的角色，建立了温度驱动退局域化的非平衡对应机制。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting can localize diffusion and generate nonequilibrium steady states, but the conditions under which spatially dependent resetting produces localization remain unclear. Here, we establish a general classification for diffusion under adaptive resetting, where the resetting rate $r(x)$ depends on position. For rates with the asymptotic scaling $r(x)\sim |x|^λ$, we identify a sharp threshold at $λ=-2$. For $λ>-2$, the steady state is localized and exhibits stretched-exponential tails, whereas for $λ<-2$, resetting is asymptotically too weak to localize the particle. Precisely at the marginal scaling $r(x)\sim |x|^{-2}$, a qualitatively new regime emerges: the steady state develops power-law tails with a temperature-dependent exponent and exhibits a finite-temperature delocalization transition. Thus, inverse-square resetting plays the role of the logarithmic potential in equilibrium, establishing a nonequilibrium counterpart of the temperature-driven delocalization seen there.

</details>


### [98] [Long-time Dynamics of Many-body Open Quantum Systems using Quantum Generating Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27237)
*Katha Ganguly,Dario Poletti,Bijay Kumar Agarwalla*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子生成函数（quantum generating function, QGF）框架，用于高效研究开放多体量子系统的长时间动力学，包括系综平均的马尔可夫量子主方程动力学和含量子跃迁与量子态扩散的轨迹分辨动力学，并通过可积开放XXZ链与不可积开放次近邻XXZ自旋链验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放量子系统中相干幺正演化与环境诱导耗散相互作用可产生丰富的非平衡动力学，但现有用于模拟多体开放量子系统的方法难以访问长时间动力学过程，这一技术瓶颈亟需突破。

Method: 该方法发展了一种量子生成函数形式体系，通过直接计算高阶矩和涨落统计而不显式演化量子态，从而高效且可扩展地研究开放多体量子系统。该形式体系同时覆盖系综平均动力学与轨迹分辨动力学，后者涵盖量子跃迁（quantum jumps）和量子态扩散（quantum state diffusion）。

Result: 将该形式体系应用于可积开放XXZ链和不可积开放次近邻XXZ自旋链，揭示了依赖于初始状态的长时间输运区间。

Conclusion: 量子生成函数为研究开放量子多体系统的长时间动力学提供了一种高效且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: The interplay between coherent unitary evolution and environment-induced dissipation can give rise to a wide range of non-equilibrium dynamics in open quantum systems, ranging from interesting transport phenomena to dynamical phase transitions. However, accessing such long-time dynamics remains challenging for the existing methods developed for the simulation of many-body open quantum systems. We address this problem by developing a quantum generating function (QGF) formalism for open many-body systems for both ensemble-averaged dynamics governed by a Markovian quantum master equation and trajectory-resolved dynamics described by the quantum trajectory formalism, including quantum jumps, and quantum state diffusion. Our approach computes the dynamics of higher-order moments and fluctuation statistics without explicitly evolving the quantum state, thereby providing an efficient and scalable approach for investigating many-body open quantum systems. We demonstrate the versatility of the formalism by applying it to both the integrable open XXZ chain and the nonintegrable open next-nearest-neighbor XXZ spin chain, where it uncovers distinct initial state dependent long-time transport regimes. Our work establishes quantum generating functions as an efficient and scalable framework for investigating long-time dynamics in open quantum many-body systems.

</details>

### [99] [Emptiness formation in the Lieb-Liniger gas: hydrodynamic instantons and a conjectured rate function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26640)
*Boris A. Khanikati,Alexander G. Abanov*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一维排斥性Lieb-Liniger玻色气体基态中的空位形成概率（emptiness formation probability, EFP）。对于长度为2R的宏观空区间，其主导渐近行为由速率函数f(γ₀)描述。作者提出了一个无参数积分方程来描述f(γ₀)，该猜想在Tonks-Girardeau极限和弱耦合极限下均与已知解析结果吻合，并与数值最小化流体力学作用量的结果在数个数量级内具有百分位一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位形成概率是表征量子多体系统中大尺度涨落的重要可观测量，但对其精确解析描述长期以来存在困难。作者旨在为Lieb-Liniger玻色气体基态的空位形成概率提供统一的、无参数的速率函数描述，以弥补已有工作中仅能处理特定极限的不足。

Method: 作者从EFP的精确双场Fredholm行列式表示出发，通过形式推导提出关于速率函数f(γ₀)的无参数积分方程猜想，并识别了严格的均匀渐近陈述（uniform asymptotic statement）以待严格证明。通过与两个独立极限（Tonks-Girardeau强耦合极限与弱耦合极限）中的精确结果及其一阶修正对比，以及数值上最小化Lieb-Liniger流体力学作用量（hydrodynamic action）来验证猜想。

Result: 所提出的积分方程复现了Tonks-Girardeau极限和弱耦合极限及其各自独立获得的一阶修正。数值计算在与耦合强度覆盖超过四个数量级的范围内与猜想结果在百分之几误差水平内吻合，并给出了相应的空位瞬子（emptiness instanton）及其星形（astroid-like）真空区域结构。

Conclusion: 该猜想为Lieb-Liniger玻色气体空位形成概率的速率函数提供了统一且数值上高度一致的无参数积分方程描述，为未来严格的数学推导指明了方向；数值结果同时揭示了空位形成的瞬子几何结构。

Abstract: We study the emptiness formation probability (EFP) in the ground state of the repulsive one-dimensional Lieb-Liniger Bose gas. For a macroscopic empty interval of length $2R$, its leading asymptotic behavior is described by a rate function $f(γ_0)$, defined by $-\log P(R)\sim(ρ_0R)^2f(γ_0)$, where $ρ_0$ is the mean density and $γ_0$ is the dimensionless interaction strength. We propose a parameter-free integral equation for $f(γ_0)$. Starting from the exact dual-field Fredholm-determinant representation of the EFP, we show how the conjectured kernel arises formally and identify the uniform asymptotic statement that remains to be proven for a rigorous derivation. The conjecture reproduces the Tonks-Girardeau and weak-coupling limits, as well as the first correction obtained independently in both limits. It also agrees at the few-percent level with numerical minimization of the Lieb-Liniger hydrodynamic action over more than four orders of magnitude in coupling. The numerical calculation yields the corresponding emptiness instantons and their astroid-like vacuum regions.

</details>


### [100] [Vortex lattices in coupled one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensates with a synthetic magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26966)
*Holly A. J. Middleton-Spencer,Rose Davies,David G. Reid,Anna Dalmasso,Theodore Kazanas,Hannah M. Price*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究受合成磁场作用的耦合一维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体的平均场基态。通过具有连续与离散两个空间方向的耦合线（coupled-wire）模型，研究从少腿梯子（few-leg ladders）到扩展涡旋晶格（extended vortex lattices）的物理过渡，重点分析有限尺寸效应与边界条件对涡旋涌现和空间组织的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图理解在连续-离散量子流体中，有限系统尺寸与边界条件如何决定涡旋（vortex）的涌现及其空间排列方式，尤其是从少线系统向扩展涡旋晶格过渡过程中的基态行为，为未来隧道耦合原子线或合成维度实验提供理论指导。

Method: 采用平均场方法（mean-field approach）数值求解耦合一维玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体在合成磁场下的基态方程，构建同时包含一个连续和一个离散空间方向相互作用的耦合线模型，分别考察两线系统以研究不同基态相之间的转变，并逐步增加线数以追踪向扩展涡旋晶格的演化，同时对比周期边界与开放边界条件的影响。

Result: 数值结果表明，在两根导线系统中存在涡旋类（vortex-like）、偏密度（biased-density）和类迈斯纳（Meissner-like）态三种基态，且系统纵向有限尺寸会改变这些态之间的转变行为。增加导线数量后，基态朝扩展涡旋晶格演化；在离散方向上使用周期边界条件时，会形成类似阿布里科索夫晶格（Abrikosov lattice）的类涡旋交错排列，而在小尺寸系统的开放边界条件下，涡旋被限制在合成方向中心附近的若干行内。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸效应和边界条件是控制连续-离散量子流体中涡旋涌现与空间组织的关键因素，该发现与未来隧道耦合原子线或合成维度实验密切相关。

Abstract: We investigate the mean-field ground states of coupled one-dimensional Bose-Einstein condensates subject to a synthetic magnetic field. The resulting interacting coupled-wire model has one continuous and one discrete spatial direction, providing a controlled way to interpolate between the physics of few-leg ladders and extended vortex lattices. For two wires, we study the vortex-like, biased-density, and Meissner-like states, exploring how the finite longitudinal size of the system modifies the transitions between them. Increasing the number of wires, the ground state evolves towards an extended vortex lattice. We find numerically that periodic boundary conditions in the discrete direction can favour staggered arrays of like-signed vortices resembling an Abrikosov lattice, while open boundaries in small finite-size systems confine the vortices into rows near the centre of the synthetic direction. Our results explore how finite size effects and boundary conditions govern the emergence and spatial organisation of vortices in continuous-discrete quantum fluids, with relevance to future experiments in tunnel-coupled atomic wires or with synthetic dimensions.

</details>


### [101] [Crossing the Rotational Sound Barrier in a Quantum Solvent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27030)
*Baptiste Coquinot,Giacomo Bighin,Mikhail Lemeshko,Ragheed Alhyder*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了嵌入超流体（superfluid）环境中受驱动的旋转分子（driven molecule）的动力学行为，涵盖氦和玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）体系，在转速与浴动力学（bath dynamics）相当的条件下，证明转子在特征谐振频率以下保持局域化，并揭示了一个耗散旋转声障（dissipative rotation sound barrier）——分子在此处共振发射浴激发并发生强角动量交换，最终建立了驱动转子在量子溶剂（quantum solvent）中的动力学相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超流体中的分子为研究杂质物理（impurity physics）提供了实验可控的平台，但在快速旋转驱动条件下，分子与超流体浴之间的耦合动力学尚未被充分理解，特别是在转速接近浴激发能量尺度的情形下。

Method: 采用光学离心机（optical centrifuge）这一实验相关平台对旋转分子进行驱动，并在共转参考系（co-rotating frame）中分析浴激发所经历的旋转多普勒位移（rotational Doppler shift），从而推导出角动量分辨的共振条件。

Result: 转子在某个特征谐振频率以下保持局域化，该频率可超过浴的激发能量，从而实现对超快旋转杂质（ultrafast rotating impurities）的访问；浴激发在共转系中经历旋转多普勒位移，产生平衡态角动量理论（angulon theory）中不存在的角动量分辨共振；并识别出一个耗散旋转声障，在此处分子共振发射浴激发并发生强角动量交换。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了驱动转子在量子溶剂中的动力学相图（dynamical phase diagram），并引入了一个研究量子多体体系中快速驱动旋转杂质的通用平台。

Abstract: Molecules embedded in superfluids provide an experimentally controllable platform for investigating impurity physics. Here, we investigate a driven molecule rotating in a superfluid environment, including helium and Bose--Einstein condensates, at rotation frequencies similar to the bath dynamics. Within the experimentally relevant platform of an optical centrifuge, we show that the rotor remains localized up to a characteristic harmonic frequency that can realistically exceed the excitation energies of the bath, enabling access to ultrafast rotating impurities. In the co-rotating frame, the bath excitations experience a rotational Doppler shift, generating angular-momentum-resolved resonances absent in equilibrium angulon theory. We identify a dissipative rotational sound barrier at which the molecule resonantly emits bath excitations and undergoes strong angular momentum exchange with the surrounding medium. Overall, we establish the dynamical phase diagram of the driven rotor in a quantum solvent and introduce a generic platform for investigating fast driven rotating impurities in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [102] [The heavy Fermi polaron I: the Lithium-Cesium experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27322)
*Michael Rautenberg,Tobias Krom,Eleonora Lippi,Lauriane Chomaz,Matthias Weidemüller*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于在量子气体混合物中研究费米极化子（Fermi polaron）的实验平台。该平台包含约一千个$^{133}$Cs玻色杂质，浸没在约$2\times10^5$个$^6$Li原子的深度简并费米气体中（$T/T_F\sim0.2$），通过Feshbach共振调节种间相互作用，并利用无相对动量转移的光学拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）进行注入光谱学来产生费米极化子。由于两种原子间巨大的质量失配，该系统可探索此前未触及的费米极化子新物理区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米极化子研究需要能够灵活调控量子气体参数的实验平台，以探索不同参数区间下的极化子物理。现有平台在质量失配和相互作用调控方面存在局限，难以触及某些此前未探索的物理区域，因此需要开发新的实验装置。

Method: 该平台由约一千个$^{133}$Cs玻色杂质和约$2\times10^5$个处于单一超精细态的$^6$Li费米子构成，通过Feshbach共振（Feshbach resonance）实现种间相互作用可调。采用不涉及相对动量转移的光学拉曼光谱进行注入光谱学（injection spectroscopy）操作，从而制备费米极化子。

Result: 该实验平台实现了费米极化子的制备，且凭借$^{133}$Cs与$^6$Li之间极大的质量失配，该系统能够进入此前未被实验探索的费米极化子物理新区域。

Conclusion: 该实验平台为研究费米极化子提供了新的实验途径，特别是在大质量失配这一此前难以实现的条件下的极化子行为，有望推进对费米极化子本征物理的理解。

Abstract: We present details of an experimental platform for studying Fermi polarons in a quantum-gas mixture. The system consists of about a thousand bosonic $^{133}$Cs impurities immersed in a deeply degenerate Fermi gas ($T/T_F \sim 0.2$) of approximately $2\times 10^5$ $^6$Li atoms in a single hyperfine state, with interspecies interactions tunable via a Feshbach resonance. Using optical Raman spectroscopy without relative momentum transfer, we perform injection spectroscopy and thereby create the Fermi polaron. Owing to the large mass imbalance between the two species, the setup provides access to previously unexplored regimes of Fermi polarons.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

### [103] [Quantifying the effects of nickel on Earth's inner-core nucleation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27139)
*Jiahui Zhai,Liangrui Wei,Chen Gao,Yang Sun*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本研究通过原子模拟（atomistic simulation）量化镍（Ni）对地球内核成核过程的影响，发现Fe-Ni合金中体心立方（bcc）相具有更小的临界核和更低的成核势垒，镍富集、bcc成核和化学不均匀性共同显著缩小了内核成核悖论，使Fe80Ni20合金的bcc成核过冷度达到约250 K，接近地球物理约束值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球固态内核的形成是深部地球热演化和化学演化的关键转折点，但其起源存在悖论：初始成核似乎需要在液态外核中产生不切实际的大过冷度（undercooling）。因此亟需阐明在真实内核条件下，何种因素可以降低成核屏障。

Method: 采用原子模拟（atomistic simulations）方法，在约15 GPa和5000 K的内核条件下，系统量化镍含量对Fe-Ni合金中hcp与bcc相竞争成核行为的影响，包括临界核尺寸、成核势垒、成核等待时间以及局部化学波动（chemical fluctuation）对宏观成核速率的作用。

Result: Fe-Ni合金中hcp与bcc相保持强烈的热力学竞争，但bcc相始终形成更小的临界核并具有更低的成核势垒；增加熔体中Ni含量可进一步降低成核势垒并缩短成核等待时间；局部化学波动显著影响宏观成核速率。综合效应下，Fe80Ni20中bcc成核的过冷度达到约250 K，接近地球物理约束。

Conclusion: 镍富集、bcc成核路径以及化学不均匀性三方面因素的共同作用可大幅缩小内核成核悖论，为理解地球内核的形成机制提供了新的定量认识。

Abstract: The formation of Earth's solid inner core marks a major transition in the thermal and chemical evolution of the deep Earth, yet its origin remains paradoxical, as initial nucleation appears to require unrealistically large undercooling in the outer core. Here, we use atomistic simulations to quantify how Ni affects this process under inner-core conditions. While Fe-Ni alloys preserve strong thermodynamic competition between the hcp and bcc phases, the bcc phase consistently forms smaller critical nuclei and has lower nucleation barriers than hcp. Increasing Ni content in the melts further lowers the nucleation barrier and shortens the nucleation waiting time. Local chemical fluctuations also strongly affect the macroscopic nucleation rate. Combining these effects, bcc nucleation in Fe80Ni20 reaches about 250 K of undercooling, approaching geophysical constraints. We demonstrate that Ni enrichment, bcc nucleation, and chemical heterogeneity substantially narrow the inner-core nucleation paradox.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Homogenization procedure in the Cauchy problem for the elastic composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26439)
*Sergey Sergeev,Reinaldo Rodriguez-Ramos*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了弹性力学方程柯西问题（Cauchy problem）的均匀化（homogenization）问题。复合材料假设为沿选定轴以单位胞胞（unit cell）周期性重复构成，包含两种不同材料。均匀化过程基于算子变量分离（operator separation of variables）方法，为弹性力学方程的均匀化提供了新思路，并提出了一种构造均匀化方程的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弹性力学方程柯西问题的均匀化问题，旨在为复合材料的宏观行为建模提供更有效的数学方法。

Method: 假设复合材料为沿选定轴的单位胞胞周期性重复排列，包含两种不同材料；均匀化过程基于算子变量分离方法，以此推导均匀化方程。

Result: 基于算子变量分离给出了弹性力学方程均匀化的新见解，并提供了一种新的均匀化方法及构建均匀化方程的途径。

Conclusion: 算子变量分离方法为弹性力学方程的均匀化提供了新的理论视角和构造方案。

Abstract: In the present work we consider the problem of the homogenization of the Cauchy problem for the equations of the elasticity. We assume the composite material is constructed as a periodic repetition along the chosen axis of the unit cell which models to different materials. The homogenization procedure is based on the operator separation of variables, which gives the new incites for the homogenization of the elasticity equations and provides new way of the homogenization and constructing the homogenized equation.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Front-induced transitions control THz waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2102.02879)
*Aidan W. Schiff-Kearn,Lauren Gingras,Simon Bernier,Nima Chamanara,Kartiek Agarwal,Jean-Michel Ménard,David G. Cooke*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 SLIPSTREAM 的技术，通过在半导体填充的平面波导中光激发移动电荷载流子产生相对论性前端，实现对太赫兹（THz）光脉冲的新型操控，包括在亚光速前端下产生准静态场波形，以及在超光速前端下实现时间反演操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论性移动介电扰动能够在空间和时间上同时调制光波，产生非互易性和时间反演等传统非线性光学无法实现的奇异效应。作者旨在开发一种可操控太赫兹光谱波段内光的平台，以实现在亚周期（sub-cycle）水平上对光的直接和先进控制。

Method: 该方法通过光激发半导体填充平面波导中的移动电荷载流子来产生相对论性前端（relativistic front），利用绝热（adiabatic）相互作用使波矢和频率同时发生前端诱导转变，从而实现太赫兹光脉冲的操控。

Result: 在亚光速前端速度区域生成了时间拉伸的太赫兹波形，其准静态场可持续数个皮秒且可通过前端相互作用距离调谐；在超光速区域，载流子前端超越太赫兹脉冲，通过前端诱导的带内跃迁实现了时间反演操作。

Conclusion: 作者预期该平台将成为太赫兹光谱波段的通用工具，适用于需要在亚周期水平上对光进行直接和先进控制的应用场景。

Abstract: Relativistically moving dielectric perturbations can be used to manipulate light in new and exciting ways beyond the capabilities of traditional nonlinear optics. Adiabatic interaction with the moving front modulates the wave simultaneously in both space and time, and manifests a front-induced transition in both wave vector and frequency yielding exotic effects including non-reciprocity and time-reversal. Here, we introduce a technique called SLIPSTREAM, Spacetime Light-Induced Photonic STRucturEs for Advanced Manipulation. The technique is based on the creation of relativistic fronts in a semiconductor-filled planar waveguide by photoexcitation of mobile charge carriers. Here we demonstrate the capabilities of SLIPSTREAM for novel manipulation of THz light pulses through relativistic front-induced transitions. In the sub-luminal front velocity regime, we generate temporally stretched THz waveforms, with a quasi-static field lasting for several picoseconds tunable with the front interaction distance. In the super-luminal regime, the carrier front outpaces the THz pulse and a time-reversal operation is performed via a front-induced intra-band transition. We anticipate our platform will be a versatile tool for future applications in the THz spectral band requiring direct and advanced control of light at the sub-cycle level.

</details>


### [106] [Intrinsic temporal and spectral mixing in time-resolved terahertz spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16713)
*Benjamin J. Dringoli,David G. Cooke*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文通过实验、有限差分时域（FDTD）模拟和非平衡响应函数理论扩展，揭示了超快光泵-太赫兹探测光谱中因光致材料响应在比太赫兹脉冲宽度更短的时间尺度上发生调制而产生的伪特征，并澄清了这些特征不应被误认为光激发相干响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超快光泵-太赫兹探测（optical-pump terahertz-probe）测量中，当光致响应被调制的时标远短于太赫兹脉冲自身时，测得的时频响应会偏离简单的线性（linear response）行为，产生容易被误判为光激发相干响应的复杂特征，因此需要系统性厘清其来源。

Method: 研究以光激发SnSe为实验对象，测量其二维时频太赫兹透射图谱，并通过有限差分时域（finite-difference time-domain, FDTD）仿真以及对非平衡响应函数理论（non-equilibrium response function theory）的扩展来复现实验特征，验证其系统性来源。

Result: 实验在光激发SnSe的早期泵浦-探测延迟时刻观察到意外的时频特征；这些特征在FDTD数值模拟和扩展的非平衡响应函数理论中均得以复现，证明其源于测量本身的系统性效应而非光激发相干响应。

Conclusion: 该工作增进了对高时间分辨率光泵-太赫兹探测光谱中系统性效应的理解，并探讨了这些假象特征最可能出现的条件，为该类光谱学实验结果的正确解读提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In an ultrafast optical-pump terahertz-probe measurement, the photoinduced material response can be modulated on a timescale shorter than the extent of the THz pulse. In this situation, the measured time-frequency response deviates from a simple time-dependent linear response. When full two-dimensional time-frequency maps are measured, this yields complex features that can be incorrectly assigned to a photoexcited coherent response. We investigate this experimentally via the measured response of photoexcited SnSe, whereby photoinduced phase change dynamics lead to ultrafast changes of the charge carrier and lattice optical conductivity response. Two-dimensional time-frequency THz transmission maps subsequently show unexpected time-frequency features at early pump-probe delay times. These features are reproduced in both finite-difference time-domain simulations of the THz experiment and in an extension of non-equilibrium response function theory, demonstrating their systematic origin. This work improves the understanding of systematic effects in high time resolution optical-pump THz-probe spectroscopy, and explores the conditions in which they are likely to appear.

</details>


### [107] [Optically Induced Thermal Runaway in Phase-Change VO2 Nanostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26401)
*Jiří Kabát,Peter Kepič,Andrea Konečná,Filip Ligmajer*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个耦合全波电磁动力学与热传导的迭代多物理场框架，首次在亚波长相变光子学中发现二氧化钒（VO2）纳米结构中的光致热失控（optically induced thermal runaway）现象，并揭示了其阈值对波长、环境温度和衬底的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热失控现象在多个物理学科中普遍存在，但亚波长相变光子学中该现象尚未被探索，原因在于现有热光模型在尖锐光学跃迁附近失效。

Method: 提出一种迭代多物理场框架，将全波电动力学（full-wave electrodynamics）与热传导（heat transfer）耦合，用于模拟VO2纳米结构中的光热转变过程。

Result: 在VO2纳米结构中观察到光致热失控现象；失控开关阈值取决于照明波长、环境温度和底层衬底；外部热偏置可显著降低触发热失控所需的光学强度。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了尖锐光热转变的建模能力，并为有源超表面、神经形态光子器件及纳米级热管理提供了关键的设计原则。

Abstract: While thermal runaway occurs across diverse physical disciplines, its presence in subwavelength phase-change photonics remains unexplored because existing thermo-optical models break down near sharp optical transitions. Here, we introduce an iterative multiphysics framework coupling full-wave electrodynamics with heat transfer, and we discover optically induced thermal runaway in vanadium dioxide (VO2) nanostructures. The runaway switching threshold depends on the illumination wavelength, ambient temperature, and underlying substrate, with external thermal biasing significantly reducing the optical intensity required for runaway ignition. Our results push forward the modeling of sharp photothermal transitions and provide essential design principles for active metasurfaces, neuromorphic photonic devices, and nanoscale thermal management.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond geometric symmetry: Broadband linear relations in wave scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26562)
*Malte Röntgen,Sucui Luo,Xuelong Chen,Chenyu Zhang,Ruotao Ye,Tianshu Jiang,Wenlong Gao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的频率无关（frequency-independent）的线性关系，用于描述波散射装置中反射与透射参数之间的约束，超越了经典的互易性（reciprocity）和几何对称性所诱导的关系，并通过波导网络实验在0-1 GHz频段内得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波散射装置的设计与控制至关重要，其反射和透射参数随频率变化，但存在某些频率无关的线性关系。互易性与几何对称性是经典的已知例子，本文旨在发现并研究无法由这些经典机制诱导的新一类关系。

Method: 以波导网络（waveguide networks）为研究载体，提出并讨论新一类频率无关关系的存在条件和后果，设计示例设置进行展示，并利用同轴电缆进行实验测量。

Result: 实验在0至1 GHz的宽频范围内与理论预测高度吻合，验证了新提出的关系类别。该关系无法由互易性或几何对称性单独诱导产生。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对波导网络动力学的理论理解，并为宽带信号处理（broadband signal processing）、量子信息（quantum information）和集成光子学（integrated photonics）等应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The design and control of wave scattering, that is, of the reflection and transmission parameters of a device, is of ubiquitous importance. These parameters generally change with varying frequency, though certain \emph{frequency-independent} linear relations may exist between them. Reciprocity and geometric symmetry (reflections, rotations, etc.) are classic and well-known examples that are present in many devices and significantly ease their design. In this work, we go beyond these and introduce a new class of relations that cannot be induced by reciprocity or geometric symmetry. Choosing networks of waveguides as our workhorse, we discuss the conditions and consequences of such novel behaviour and showcase suitable example setups. We further experimentally test our predictions using coaxial cables and find excellent agreement in the broad frequency range between 0 and 1 GHz. Our work not only deepens the theoretical understanding of waveguide network dynamics, but also opens new avenues for applications in broadband signal processing, quantum information, and integrated photonics.

</details>


### [109] [Circular Dichroism Spectroscopy of Single Objects: Problems, Artifacts, and Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26717)
*Stefan Goppelt,Lisa M. Günther,Jürgen Köhler*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于单物体圆二色光谱学（single-object circular dichroism spectroscopy）的系统理论和实验框架，用于理解、量化并控制由偏振不完美产生的伪信号，并通过实验验证了在690–785 nm范围内从单个分子聚集体获取可靠CD谱的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子聚集体是众多功能系统的基础，但其结构组织因内在异质性而难以解析。将圆二色（circular dichroism, CD）测量从系综扩展到单一物体为探测异质、不可结晶系统中的超分子结构提供了独特途径，但缺乏空间和取向平均时，偏振不平衡和线性二色等效应会产生虚假CD信号，构成主要障碍。

Method: 作者建立了描述主要伪影机制（artifact mechanisms）的数学模型，识别了在实际实现中（包括基于泡克尔斯盒（Pockels cell）的偏振调制）这些伪影的起源，并提出了最小化策略。同时构建了一套实验装置，能够在690–785 nm波长范围内获取单个分子聚集体的CD光谱。

Result: 实验验证表明，当偏振相关伪影被严格表征和控制时，可以实现可靠的单物体CD测量。该框架提供了统一的原理性指导和实际操作指南，适用于单粒子手性、超分子光子学和生物光子学等应用。

Conclusion: 本文作为一篇教程性综述（Tutorial Review），为单物体CD光谱学提供了统一的理论框架和实用指南，证实了在严格控制偏振伪影条件下单物体CD测量的可靠性，为该技术在异质和非结晶系统中的超分子结构探测铺平了道路。

Abstract: Molecular aggregates are at the basis of a broad range of functional systems, including photosynthetic light-harvesting complexes, organic photovoltaics, optoelectronic devices, and molecular sensors. Their structural organization, however, is often difficult to resolve because intrinsic heterogeneity. In this context, circular dichroism (CD) spectroscopy is particularly powerful because of its sensitivity to the three-dimensional organization of molecular building blocks. Extending CD measurements from ensembles to individual objects therefore offers a unique approach to probing supramolecular architecture in heterogeneous, non-crystallizable systems. However, without spatial and orientational averaging, measurements are highly sensitive to imbalances between left- and right-circularly polarized excitation, deviations from ideal circular polarization, and polarization imperfections coupled to linear dichroism, which creates spurious CD signals. Here, we present a systematic theoretical and experimental framework for understanding, quantifying, and controlling artifacts in single-object CD spectroscopy. We develop a mathematical description of the principal artifact mechanisms, identify their origins in practical implementations, including Pockels-cell-based polarization modulation, and establish strategies for their minimization. We validate this framework using an experimental implementation capable of acquiring CD spectra from individual molecular aggregates over 690-785 nm. Our results demonstrate that reliable single-object CD measurements are achievable when polarization-related artifacts are rigorously characterized and controlled. This Tutorial Review provides a unified theoretical framework and practical guide for single-object CD spectroscopy and its application to single-particle chirality, supramolecular photonics, and biophotonics.

</details>

### [110] [Diamond quantum-sensing platform with integrated boron-doped diamond microwave antenna and thermometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27160)
*Masahiro Ohkuma,Ryo Matsumoto,Shintaro Adachi,Shinobu Onoda,Kensei Terashima,Takao Watanabe,Yoshihiko Takano,Keigo Arai*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种集成的金刚石量子传感平台，将氮-空位（NV）色心系综与硼掺杂金刚石（BDD）微波天线和温度计集成在同一金刚石衬底上，实现了低温宽场磁成像中的微波激发与局部测温功能，并成功对铜氧化物超导体的迈斯纳响应进行了温度依赖性成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温宽场氮-空位（NV）磁成像需要微波激发以及对传感区域附近温度的可靠监测，现有方法难以同时满足这两个需求，因此需要一种集成化的解决方案。

Method: 在同一个金刚石衬底上集成了NV色心系综、BDD微波天线（用于光探测磁共振测量中的微波激发）和BDD温度计（用于监测NV传感区域附近的热环境）。

Result: BDD温度计能够探测到激光引起的局部加热，而这种加热无法被安装在载物台上的温度计清晰分辨；利用该平台，作者在记录温度的同时对多个铜氧化物超导体的温度依赖性迈斯纳响应进行了成像。

Conclusion: 该集成BDD–NV平台为低温宽场磁成像提供了一种实用方案，兼具集成微波传输和局部测温能力。

Abstract: Wide-field nitrogen-vacancy (NV) magnetic imaging at cryogenic temperatures requires microwave excitation and reliable knowledge of the temperature near the sensing region. Here, we report an integrated diamond quantum-sensing platform combining an ensemble of NV centers with a boron-doped diamond (BDD) microwave antenna and thermometer formed on the same diamond substrate. The BDD antenna provides microwave excitation for optically detected magnetic resonance measurements, and the BDD thermometer monitors the thermal environment near the NV sensing region. The BDD thermometer detected laser-induced local heating that was not clearly resolved by a stage-mounted thermometer. Using this platform, we imaged the temperature-dependent Meissner response of multiple cuprate superconductors while recording the temperature. These results demonstrate that the integrated BDD--NV platform provides a practical approach for cryogenic wide-field magnetic imaging with integrated microwave delivery and local thermometry.

</details>
