### [111] [Crudely Estimated Maximum Mass for a Cosmological Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26203)
*Don N. Page*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 基于Milosavljevic和Merritt的$N$体模拟，超质量黑洞双星在近圆轨道上的内旋（inspiral）使$(v/c)^2$以约210 Gyr的时标近似线性增长；结合引力辐射后，等质量黑洞双星可在约$1.4\times10^{14}M_\odot$以下合并，该值可能构成当前宇宙黑洞质量的上限，并且合并产生的引力波应变在周期大于10万年时随周期增大而减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨超质量黑洞双星（supermassive black hole binaries）在没有引力波主导阶段的近圆轨道上，其内旋速率与时间的关系，并进一步推断引力辐射对双星合并的贡献以及可能存在的宇宙中黑洞质量上限。

Method: 依据M. Milosavljevic和D. Merritt的$N$体数值模拟结果进行分析，考察轨道相对速度平方$(v/c)^2$随时间的变化率，并在此基础上叠加引力波辐射（gravitational radiation）效应评估合并条件。

Result: $(v/c)^2$以近似210 Gyr^{-1}的速率近乎线性增长（约为当前宇宙年龄的15倍），且该速率几乎与双星质量无关；单独的内旋不足以在当今时代导致合并，但结合引力辐射后，等质量黑洞双星可在质量上限约$1.4\times10^{14}M_\odot$以内实现合并。此外，合并产生的引力波应变在波周期大于约10万年时随周期增加而减小。

Conclusion: 该结果可推测出当前宇宙中黑洞质量的一个粗略上限约$1.4\times10^{14}M_\odot$，并预言超质量黑洞双星合并产生的引力波应变在长周期（大于约10万年）区间呈下降趋势。

Abstract: $N$-body simulations by M.~Milosavljevic and D.~Merritt \cite{Milosavljevic:2001vi} suggest that for supermassive black hole binaries in their expected nearly circular orbits, before gravitational wave emission dominates, the inspiral rate causes the dimensionless orbital relative velocity squared, $(v/c)^2$, to increase nearly linearly with time at a rate that is approximately the inverse of 210 Gyr (about 15 times the present age of the universe), nearly independently of the mass of the binary. This by itself would not lead to coalescence by the present time, but when it is augmented by gravitational radiation, it can lead to coalescence for equal-mass black hole binaries up to about $1.4\times 10^{14} M_\odot$, which might then be conjectured to be a very crude upper limit to the mass of black holes in our universe at the present time. This also suggests that the gravitational wave strain from coalescing supermassive black hole binaries should decrease for wave periods greater than about 100\,000 years.

</details>


### [112] [Validating Timing-Model Accuracy for Continuous Gravitational Waves: A Comparison of LALSuite and PINT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26271)
*Kartikey Sharma,Reinhard Prix,Maria Alessandra Papa,Curt Cutler*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对LALSuite连续引力波计时模型与高精度脉冲星计时软件PINT进行了系统性验证，推导并验证了定时误差导致的信号功率损失一阶关系μ≈(2πf)²σ²_δt，并比较了两者在太阳系和双星分量上的差异。改进后的Einstein延迟实现使一年内的总定时误差降至约31纳秒以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续引力波的精确探测与表征依赖于对信号相位的精确跟踪，而精度不足的定时模型会直接导致信号功率损失和探测灵敏度下降。由于需要对LALSuite计时模型进行系统性验证以量化其精度，并与现代高精度脉冲星计时软件PINT进行对比，从而明确其误差来源和适用范围。

Method: 推导并验证了定时误差方差σ²_δt与信号功率分数损失μ之间的一阶关系式μ≈(2πf)²σ²_δt；将LALSuite中太阳系和双星分量与PINT对应模型逐一比较；使用474个编目双星脉冲星的轨道参数测试双星延迟，并验证LALSuite源时间导数的自洽性；推导了引力波信号穿过太阳内部时的Shapiro延迟处理。

Result: 使用原始Einstein延迟实现时，总不一致性以该分量为主导，σ_δt≈2.3微秒（对应f=1000 Hz时μ≈0.02%）；采用更新的Einstein延迟实现后，一年内的总不一致性降至σ_δt≲31纳秒（f=1000 Hz时μ≲4×10⁻⁸），此时主要误差来源为LALSuite近似的自转地球模型导致的台站Römer延迟贡献。

Conclusion: LALSuite在采用新的Einstein延迟实现后可达到与PINT相当的定时精度，但太阳系台站Römer延迟的地球自转近似模型仍是主要误差来源；此外，LALSuite对穿越太阳内部的信号提供了Shapiro延迟的处理方案，该情形仅对引力波观测相关。

Abstract: We present results of a systematic validation of the \lalsuite{} timing model for continuous gravitational waves against \pint{}, a modern high-accuracy pulsar-timing package. An accurate timing model is essential for tracking the signal phase, and hence for detecting and accurately characterizing continuous gravitational waves. In order to quantify the impact of timing inaccuracies, we derive and validate the leading-order relation $μ\approx (2πf)^2\stddtau^2$, where $μ$ is the fractional loss of signal power, $f$ is the signal frequency, and $\stddtau^2$ is the variance of the timing errors. We then compare the solar-system and binary components of the \lalsuite{} timing model against the corresponding models in \pint{}. With the original \lalsuite{} Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement is dominated by that component and has $\stddtau\simeq\SI{2.3}{\micro\second}$ (corresponding to $μ\simeq\SI{0.02}{\percent}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$). With the newer Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement (over one year) drops to $\stddtau\lesssim\SI{31}{\nano\second}$ (or $μ\lesssim\num{4e-8}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$) and is dominated by the observatory contribution to the \Romer{} delay, owing to the approximate Earth-rotation model used by \lalsuite{}. We additionally test binary delays using orbital parameters from \num{474} catalogued binary pulsars and verify the self-consistency of the \lalsuite{} source-time derivatives. Finally, we derive and discuss the \lalsuite{} Shapiro delay for signals passing through the solar interior, a case only relevant to gravitational waves.

</details>


### [113] [Effects of the interaction of dark matter and neutron-star matter on extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26274)
*Benjamin A. Wade,Julian Heeck,David A. Nichols*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在致密暗物质分布中，极端及中等质量比旋进系统（extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals）在引入暗物质自湮灭及其与普通物质相互作用后，对这些双星系统轨道动力学和引力波信号的影响，发现多种暗物质模型可产生独特的引力波特征，部分情形下空间引力波探测器可加以区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明空间引力波探测器可测量暗物质对引力波的影响，但此前假设暗物质仅有引力相互作用；本文旨在探索引入暗物质自湮灭及其与普通物质（重子或轻子）相互作用后，引力波信号是否呈现可区分的特征。

Method: 以中子星作为伴星的致密旋进系统为研究对象，考虑多类暗物质模型：改变暗物质分布的模型（密度尖峰spike 或湮灭平台annihilation plateau）、允许伴星吸积的模型，以及旋进过程中中子星质量递增或恒定的情形，并将纯粹引力效应（动力学摩擦dynamical friction）作为基准进行对照分析。

Result: 研究发现这些相互作用和自湮灭效应会产生独特的引力波特征，且在部分场景下效应足够显著，空间探测器可凭借引力波观测区分不同暗物质模型。

Conclusion: 暗物质与自身或普通物质的相互作用会在中子星伴星的旋进系统中留下可辨识的引力波印记，为未来空间引力波探测器探测暗物质性质提供了新的可检验途径。

Abstract: Extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals in a dense dark-matter distribution have the effects of the dark matter imprinted on the orbital dynamics of and the emitted gravitational waves from these systems. Prior work has shown that space-based gravitational-wave detectors can measure the dark-matter-induced effects on the gravitational waves, which would give evidence for the presence of dark matter around the massive black hole. In this earlier work, the dark matter has been assumed to have only gravitational interactions (namely, no dark-matter self-annihilation or interactions between dark matter and ordinary baryonic or leptonic matter). In this article, we investigate the gravitational-wave effects of introducing such interactions of dark matter with itself or with ordinary matter in binaries with a neutron-star secondary. We consider broad classes of dark-matter models that change the distribution of dark matter (spikes or annihilation plateaus) and which permit accretion onto the secondary, as well as an increasing or static mass of the neutron star during the inspiral (in addition to the purely gravitational effect of dynamical friction). We find distinctive gravitational-wave signatures of these interactions and of self-annihilation, which in some of the scenarios could be sufficiently large for space-based detectors to distinguish them using gravitational-wave observations of these systems.

</details>


### [114] [Born Geometry, Metaparticles, and an Effective Geometric Realization of the Modular Black-Hole Remnant: A Phase-Space Resolution of the Schwarzschild Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26413)
*Paul-Robert Chouha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Born几何（Born geometry）、模时空（modular spacetime）与元粒子（metaparticle）动力学框架下，构造了模残余（modular remnant）的有效几何实现，通过Born对偶施瓦西几何将经典奇点替换为有限模核心（modular core），并揭示了该结构如何规避Hawking-Penrose奇点定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于元粒子黑洞热力学的工作暗示蒸发终点存在有限模残余，但其对应的时空几何此前未知。本文旨在为这一热力学推断提供具体的几何实现，并解答经典施瓦西奇点（Schwarzschild singularity）在该框架下的命运问题。

Method: 从坐标由对偶坐标（dual coordinates）补充的双倍相空间（doubled phase-space）描述出发，推导出由Born不变径向距离控制的Born对偶施瓦西几何；引入模不确定性关系以禁止相空间原点处的任意局域化，从而生成有限模核心；并入元粒子对偶约束，产生有效保形形变和非平庸的有效应力-能量张量（effective stress-energy tensor）。

Result: 所得内部区域发展出以张力主导径向扇区为特征的有有限各向异性源；在模核心附近自然出现对径向零能量条件（Null Energy Condition）与强能量条件（Strong Energy Condition）的局域违反，从而规避了Hawking-Penrose奇点定理中聚焦假设（focusing assumptions）的适用前提。

Conclusion: 施瓦西奇点被有限Born几何相空间结构所替代，该结构的紫外（ultraviolet）行为由对偶的非几何自由度（non-geometric degrees of freedom）支配。这为元粒子黑洞热力学推断的模残余提供了有效的几何对应物。

Abstract: Recent work on metaparticle black-hole thermodynamics suggested the existence of a finite evaporation endpoint characterized by a minimal horizon area, a maximal Hawking temperature, and a stable cold modular remnant. However, the corresponding spacetime geometry remained unknown. In this work we construct an effective geometric realization of the modular remnant within the framework of Born geometry, modular spacetime, and metaparticle dynamics. Starting from a doubled phase-space description in which spacetime coordinates are supplemented by dual coordinates, we derive a Born-doubled Schwarzschild geometry governed by a Born-invariant radial distance. The associated modular uncertainty relation prevents arbitrary localization at the phase-space origin, rendering the classical Schwarzschild singularity physically inaccessible and replacing it with a finite modular core. We then incorporate the metaparticle duality constraint, which induces an effective conformal deformation of the Born-geometric background and generates a nontrivial effective stress-energy tensor. The resulting interior region develops a finite anisotropic source characterized by a tension-dominated radial sector. Localized violations of the radial Null Energy Condition and the Strong Energy Condition arise naturally near the modular core, providing a mechanism through which the focusing assumptions underlying the Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems are evaded. These results establish an effective geometric counterpart of the modular remnant inferred from metaparticle black-hole thermodynamics and suggest that the Schwarzschild singularity is replaced by a finite Born-geometric phase-space structure whose ultraviolet behavior is governed by dual, non-geometric degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [115] [Gödel-type universes in Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond gravity with Ricci- and Riemann-tensor nonminimal couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26472)
*Fernando M. Belchior*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种在局域洛伦兹对称性（local Lorentz symmetry）被Kalb-Ramond二形式真空期望值自发破缺的引力模型中的齐次Gödel型几何，分析了含Ricci张量与Riemann张量两类非最小曲率耦合的场方程，给出了各向不等效真空中二形式取向下的代数解系统，并重点讨论了类磁场背景B12=b下因果性的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gödel型时空允许闭合类时曲线（closed timelike curves），研究其因果性破缺边界在一般洛伦兹破缺引力框架下的表现，可检验非最小曲率耦合对时空因果结构的影响，并补充现有bumblebee模型结果。

Method: 在Einstein-Hilbert作用量上补充两类独立的非最小曲率耦合项（Ricci张量耦合与Riemann张量耦合），在Kalb-Ramond场强与对称性破缺势一阶导数消失的齐次真空扇区中，将场方程约化为Gödel型度规四脚标架（tetrad frame）下的闭合代数方程组，并分别推导多种不可等效真空二形式取向下的曲率张量、约化度规方程与Kalb-Ramond自洽方程。

Result: 对伪磁场背景B12=b，Kalb-Ramond方程给出质量与角速度比值的约束关系m²/ω²=2(ξ₁+3ξ₂)/(ξ₁+2ξ₂)。仅含Ricci耦合时恢复非因果Gödel值m²=2ω²；引入Riemann耦合可改变时序界限，当ξ₂=-ξ₁时可达到因果临界值m²=4ω²。此外Riemann耦合能移动闭合类时曲线的临界半径，但普通正能物质会严重限制完全因果解的存在。

Conclusion: 非最小Riemann曲率耦合能显著调节Gödel型时空的因果结构，允许达到临界因果值，但正能条件的约束使得完全因果的物理解在实际物质源下难以实现。论文还讨论了理想流体、标量场和电磁场作为源的扩展情形。

Abstract: This paper studies homogeneous \Godel-type geometries in a gravitational model where local Lorentz symmetry is spontaneously broken by the vacuum expectation value of an antisymmetric Kalb-Ramond two-form. We consider the Einstein-Hilbert action supplemented by two independent nonminimal curvature couplings: a Ricci-tensor coupling of the form $B_{a}{}^{c}B_{bc}R^{ab}$ and a Riemann-tensor coupling of the form $B^{ab}B^{cd}R_{abcd}$. In the homogeneous vacuum sector, where the Kalb-Ramond field strength and the first derivatives of the symmetry-breaking potential vanish, the field equations reduce to a closed algebraic system in the tetrad frame of the \Godel-type metric. We derive the curvature tensors, the reduced metric equations, and the Kalb-Ramond consistency equations for several inequivalent orientations of the vacuum two-form. Particular attention is given to the pseudo-magnetic background $B_{12}=b$, because it preserves the symmetry of the rotation plane and yields a transparent deformation of the usual bumblebee result. For this sector the Kalb-Ramond equation fixes $\frac{m^2}{ω^2}=\frac{2(ξ_1+3ξ_2)}{ξ_1+2ξ_2}$, where $ξ_1$ and $ξ_2$ are the Ricci and Riemann couplings. The Ricci coupling alone reproduces the noncausal \Godel value $m^2=2ω^2$, while the Riemann coupling shifts the chronology bound and allows the critical causal value $m^2=4ω^2$ for $ξ_2=-ξ_1$. We also show that the Riemann coupling can move the critical radius for closed timelike curves, but ordinary positive-energy matter severely restricts completely causal solutions. Finally, we discuss perfect fluid, scalar field, and electromagnetic sources.

</details>


### [116] [Comparison of Two-Atom Cross Spectra in de Sitter Spacetime, Uniformly Accelerated Minkowski Vacuum, and a Thermal Bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26554)
*Zhiming Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个二能级原子分别与德西特（de Sitter）时空、闵可夫斯基真空中横向分离的匀加速原子、以及闵可夫斯基热浴三种物理配置下四维无质量共形耦合标量场的弱耦合，比较了相同局域温度但物理起源不同的三种设置。当参数满足特定匹配条件时，三种配置的局域Wightman核和局域谱完全一致，但有限的分离距离下热浴产生sinc空间因子，而另两者产生双曲几何因子，且在长距离极限下关联衰减律不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一比较具有相同局域温度但物理起源不同的三种设置（德西特时空、匀加速真空和热浴）中两原子关联谱的异同，以深入理解Unruh效应与热浴之间的等价性与差异。

Method: 首先推导三种设置下两原子交叉谱（cross spectra），然后通过取零分离极限（zero-separation limit）统一得到单原子局域谱；在参数匹配条件 $a=H$ 和 $\beta=2\pi/H$ 下比较三者的完整局域Wightman核与局域谱。

Result: 在 $a=H$、$\beta=2\pi/H$ 下三种配置的局域Wightman核和局域谱完全相同；有限分离时热浴产生 $\operatorname{sinc}(\Omega L)$ 空间因子，而德西特时空和横切加速真空产生双曲几何因子；长距离极限下德西特和加速真空交叉关联按 $L^{-2}$ 衰减，热浴仅按 $L^{-1}$ 衰减；德西特与横切加速真空交叉谱在 $a=H$ 且分离瞬时匹配时重合。

Conclusion: 德西特时空中的共动原子与闵可夫斯基横切加速原子在特定参数下共享相同的局域谱，但两者的交叉关联均与真实热浴不同，体现在空间因子形式和长距离衰减律的差异上；此外，德西特空间中物理分离随宇宙膨胀演化，而另两种闵可夫斯基配置中的交叉关联是平稳的。

Abstract: We study the weak coupling of two identical two-level atoms to a four-dimensional massless conformally coupled scalar field and compare three settings with the same local temperature but different physical origins: comoving atoms in the Bunch--Davies vacuum of de Sitter spacetime, transversely separated uniformly accelerated atoms in the Minkowski vacuum, and static atoms in a Minkowski thermal bath. We first derive the two-atom cross spectra in the three settings and then obtain the single-atom local spectra uniformly from the zero-separation limit. When $a=H$ and $β=2π/H$, the complete local Wightman kernels and local spectra are identical in all three cases. At finite separation, a genuine thermal bath produces a $\sinc(ΩL)$ spatial factor, whereas de Sitter spacetime and the transversely accelerated vacuum produce a hyperbolic geometric factor. In the long-distance limit, the cross correlations in de Sitter spacetime and the transversely accelerated vacuum decay as $L^{-2}$, whereas those in the Minkowski thermal bath decay only as $L^{-1}$. The de Sitter and transversely accelerated vacuum cross spectra coincide when $a=H$ and the separations are instantaneously matched. For a fixed experimental setup, the physical separation between the two comoving atoms in de Sitter space evolves with cosmic expansion, whereas the pulled-back cross correlations in the uniformly accelerated and thermal Minkowski configurations are stationary.

</details>


### [117] [Relativistic Modeling for Solid Earth Tide Estimation via Space-to-Ground Clock Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26672)
*Qin Li,Wei-Hang Sun,Yu-Jie Tan,Cheng-Gang Qin,Jun Ke,Xiang-Pei Liu,Han-Ning Dai,Tong Liu,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究将地球引力势、日月潮汐势和固体地球潮汐效应纳入空间—地面钟比较的相对论性框架，采用三链路多普勒消除配置隔离目标信号，并针对倾斜地球同步轨道卫星进行数值模拟，证明在30天连续观测窗口内能稳定估计Love数与k2组合的有效参数，且提取精度受限于钟稳定性而非径向精密定轨误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代原子钟技术的超高精度使其从计时工具升级为引力势传感器，催生了相对论大地测量学（Relativistic Geodesy）这一高度交叉学科。鉴于高精度钟网络有望探测周期性引力变化，必须评估其在复杂观测噪声环境下通过空间—地面链路反演固体地球潮汐参数的能力。

Method: 将地球引力势、直接日月潮汐势和固体地球潮汐效应整合进高精度相对论框架，用于空间—地面钟比较；采用三链路多普勒消除（three-link Doppler cancellation）构型以隔离目标信号，并对倾斜地球同步轨道（inclined geosynchronous orbit）卫星进行数值模拟，分析钟不稳定性与有色精密定轨误差对参数提取的影响。

Result: 高轨道高度导致单个Love数之间存在严重共线性，但由h2与k2 Love数组合的有效参数在30天连续观测窗口内能成功收敛到稳定估计值；灵敏度分析表明当前的提取精度主要由钟稳定性限制，而非径向精密定轨误差。

Conclusion: 高精度钟网络具备通过空间—地面链路反演固体地球潮汐参数的潜力，但需注意高轨构型下Love数可辨识性（identifiability）受共线性限制的实际问题，且未来精度提升的关键在于改善钟稳定性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of modern atomic clock technology, their unprecedented precision elevates them from timekeeping tools to gravitational potential sensors, thereby fostering the highly interdisciplinary field of Relativistic Geodesy. Given the potential for high-precision clock networks to detect periodic gravitational variations, it is imperative to assess their capability to invert solid Earth tide parameters via space-to-ground links in the presence of complex observational noise. To this end, we incorporate Earth's gravitational potential, direct lunisolar tidal potentials, and solid Earth tide effects into a high-precision relativistic framework for space-to-ground clock comparisons. By employing a three-link Doppler cancellation configuration to isolate the target signal, we perform numerical simulations for an inclined geosynchronous orbit satellite to analyze the effects of clock instability and colored precise orbit determination errors on parameter extraction. Our findings reveal that while high orbital altitudes cause severe collinearity between individual Love numbers, an effective parameter combining the $h_2$ and $k_2$ Love numbers successfully converges to a stable estimate within a 30-day continuous observation window. Furthermore, sensitivity analysis demonstrates that extraction accuracy is currently limited by clock stability rather than radial precise orbit determination errors.

</details>


### [118] [Near-Horizon BMS Symmetry and Implications on Black Hole Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26725)
*Nihar Ranjan Ghosh,Malay K. Nandy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究动力学位移坡林-范德比尔特-梅茨纳-萨克斯（BMS）型近视界对称性对动态Schwarzschild黑洞热力学描述的影响。作者构建了保持视界结构的微分同胚类，将超平移参数提升为视界对称性破缺产生的类Goldstone模式，并证明该模式对表面引力有非零贡献，导致黑洞熵在贝克斯坦-霍金（Bekenstein-Hawking）面积律之外出现依赖于超平移扇区及黑洞动力学演化的次领头阶修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近视界对称性（near-horizon symmetries）在黑洞物理学中的热力学意义仍是一个重要的开放问题，特别是在黑洞蒸发与信息恢复的背景下，本文旨在阐明视界适应型BMS类超平移在动态Schwarzschild黑洞热力学描述中所扮演的角色。

Method: 在近视界Rindler坐标系中构造一类保持视界结构不变的微分同胚（diffeomorphisms），并将超平移参数提升为由视界对称性破缺产生的类Goldstone模式；通过围绕背景几何展开爱因斯坦-希尔伯特（Einstein-Hilbert）作用量获得该Goldstone模式的等效作用量，并从作用量的表面项识别守恒的视界荷（horizon charge）；视界定义于未来外捕获得视界（future outer trapping horizon），表面引力采用适用于动态时空的Kodama矢量构造计算。

Result: 视界超平移模式对表面引力有非平凡贡献，并超出平稳极限地修正了黑洞的热力学描述。通过关联的Noether荷推导视界BMS变换几何的熵，发现领头阶恢复贝克斯坦-霍金面积律（Bekenstein-Hawking area law），而次领头阶修正明确依赖于超平移扇区及黑洞的动力学演化。

Conclusion: 视界超平移模式能够超越平稳极限地修正动态黑洞的热力学描述，表明近视界对称性在黑洞熵与热力学性质中承载着超越面积律的动力学信息。

Abstract: Thermodynamic significance of near-horizon symmetries remains an important open question in black hole physics, particularly in the context of black hole evaporation and information recovery. In this work, we investigate the role of horizon-adapted Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs (BMS)-like supertranslations in the thermodynamic description of a dynamical Schwarzschild black hole. Working in a near-horizon Rindler coordinate system, we construct a class of diffeomorphisms that preserve the horizon structure and promote the associated supertranslation parameter to a Goldstone-like mode arising from the breaking of horizon symmetry. By expanding the Einstein-Hilbert action around the background geometry, we obtain the effective action for the Goldstone mode and identify the corresponding conserved horizon charge from the surface contribution of the action. The relevant horizon is defined at the future outer trapping horizon, while the surface gravity is computed using the Kodama-vector construction appropriate for dynamical spacetimes. We show that the horizon supertranslation mode contributes non-trivially to the surface gravity and modifies the thermodynamic description of the black hole beyond the stationary limit. Using the associated Noether charge, we derive the entropy of the horizon-BMS transformed geometry and find that the Bekenstein-Hawking area law is recovered at leading order, while subleading corrections depend explicitly on the supertranslation sector and the dynamical evolution of the black hole.

</details>


### [119] [Gravitational Lensing of Hayward Black Holes with EFT-Corrected Photon Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26785)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在考虑有效场论（EFT）对光子传播的修正时，能否通过强引力透镜（strong gravitational lensing）在观测上区分Hayward正则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞。作者推导了非最小耦合导致的光子传播修正定律，并使用强偏转极限计算了光子球和偏转角的对数发散部分的修正。结果发现，EFT修正会在强透镜可观测量的特征上留下印记，且当Hayward参数g³接近临界值时，EFT修正的贡献更加显著，表明强引力透镜可能为区分这两类黑洞提供手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在回答一个观测问题：当考虑有效场论对光子传播的修正时，Hayward正则黑洞能否通过强引力透镜效应在观测上与Schwarzschild黑洞区分开来。

Method: 研究从电磁场与时空曲率之间的非最小耦合导出修正后的光子传播定律，并在Hayward时空中分析相应的光子轨迹。利用强偏转极限（strong deflection limit）推导光子球以及偏转角中对数发散部分的修正，并对代表性的Hayward参数值评估强偏转可观测量，与Schwarzschild情形进行比较。

Result: EFT对光子传播的修正可以在强透镜可观测量的特征上留下印记；随着Hayward参数g³趋近其临界值，EFT修正的贡献变得更加显著，表明在近临界区域曲率依赖的光子传播修正尤为重要。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，强引力透镜可能为区分Hayward正则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞提供一种可行的观测途径，尤其在Hayward参数接近临界值时。

Abstract: We investigate whether a Hayward regular black hole can be observationally distinguished from a Schwarzschild black hole through strong gravitational lensing when effective field theory (EFT) corrections to photon propagation are taken into account. We derive the modified photon propagation law induced by non-minimal couplings between the electromagnetic field and spacetime curvature, and analyze the resulting photon trajectories in the Hayward spacetime. Using the strong deflection limit, we derive the corrections to the photon sphere and the logarithmically divergent part of the deflection angle. Although the EFT corrections are parametrically small, their effects can be enhanced near the critical propagation region, where the deflection angle exhibits a logarithmic divergence. We evaluate the strong-deflection observables for representative values of the Hayward parameter and compare them with the Schwarzschild case. We find that EFT corrections to photon propagation can leave characteristic imprints on strong-lensing observables. Furthermore, the contribution of the EFT corrections becomes more pronounced as the Hayward parameter $g^3$ approaches its critical value, indicating that curvature-dependent corrections to photon propagation can become particularly relevant in the near-critical regime. These results suggest that strong gravitational lensing may provide a means of distinguishing Hayward regular black holes from Schwarzschild black holes.

</details>


### [120] [Perturbations of black holes with primary hair: time evolutions, quasinormal modes and greybody factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26984)
*Georgios Antoniou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有平移对称性的超出Horndeski引力（beyond-Horndeski gravity）中携带主要标量毛发（primary scalar hair）的黑洞的引力微扰。通过聚焦于轴向（axial）扇区，作者在物理度规（physical metric）和有效度规（effective metric）两种描述下进行了时域演化、频域拟正则模（QNM）谱分析、灰体因子（greybody factors）与吸收截面计算，并利用有效势的局域形变检验了拟正则频率和灰体因子可观测量（observables）的相对稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要在有效度规近似下计算具有标量毛发的黑洞的拟正则模谱，但缺乏物理度规框架下的验证与拓展。本文旨在系统量化不同毛发参数对微扰响应特性的影响，并评估相关观测量的稳定性。

Method: 作者聚焦于轴向扇区的引力微扰方程，在物理度规与有效度规两种描述中分别进行时域演化以比较响应的差异；随后在频域中于物理度规框架下求解拟正则模谱，并计算灰体因子与吸收截面；最后通过局域形变有效势来诊断拟正则频率与灰体因子观测量的相对稳定性。

Result: 在物理度规框架下获得了拟正则模谱，验证并拓展了现有基于有效度规方法的近似计算结果；得到了对应的灰体因子和吸收截面，并揭示了不同毛发参数下响应特性的差异。

Conclusion: 物理度规框架下的拟正则模计算能够验证并超越有效度规近似的结果；有效势的局域形变可作为衡量拟正则频率与灰体因子观测量的相对稳定性的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: In this work we study gravitational perturbations of black holes carrying primary scalar hair in shift-symmetric beyond-Horndeski gravity. By focusing on the axial sector, we study the geometric structure of the perturbation equations and perform time-domain evolutions both in the physical and in the effective metric descriptions, in order to quantify differences in the response characteristics of solutions with different hair. In the frequency domain, we then explore the QNM spectrum in the physical-metric formulation, thus verifying and extending existing approximate calculations derived in the effective-metric approach. We also compute the corresponding greybody factors and absorption cross-sections. Finally, we use localized deformations of the effective potential as a diagnostic of the relative stability of quasinormal frequencies and greybody-factor observables.

</details>


### [121] [Conformal Cyclic Cosmology from Varying Fundamental Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27014)
*Konrad Marosek,Adam Balcerzak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个双度量宇宙学模型（bimetric cosmological model），其中引力扇区与物质扇区由通过时间依赖函数 α(t) 关联的不同度规描述，导致引力参数随时间动态变化（G∝α^{-3}，c_grav∝α^{-1}）。在 α→∞ 的有限宇宙时刻，系统出现强奇点（strong singularity），引力相互作用变弱可能导致束缚结构解体与黑洞视界缩小，从而推动系统走向有效辐射主导的终态，这可能与共形循环宇宙学（Conformal Cyclic Cosmology）相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力常数与引力传播速度随时间变化的动力学机制，并研究由此产生的终态奇点（final singularity）性质及其宇宙学后果，为共形循环宇宙学提供可能的物理基础。

Method: 采用双度量框架（bimetric framework），其中引力与物质度规通过时间依赖函数 α(t) 关联，推导出动态引力参数 G(t) 与 c_grav(t)。针对 α→∞ 的可解析解，利用 Tipler 与 Królak 判据评估奇点强度，并分析其共形结构。

Result: 有限共形时间（conformal time）处发生终态奇点，其共形结构与连续宇宙学循环之间的过渡兼容；Tipler 和 Królak 判据表明该奇点为强奇点。引力减弱会导致引力束缚结构解体及黑洞视界收缩，系统趋于有效辐射主导的终态。

Conclusion: 该模型为共形循环宇宙学提供了一个可能的动力学实现路径，并表明需要将分析推广到更一般的标量-张量引力理论（scalar-tensor theories of gravity）以进一步验证其可行性。

Abstract: We investigate a bimetric cosmological model in which the gravitational and matter sectors are described by distinct metrics related through a time-dependent function $α(t)$. This relation leads to dynamical gravitational parameters, with $G(t)\proptoα^{-3}$ and $c_{\rm grav}(t)\proptoα^{-1}$, where $c_{\rm grav}$ denotes the propagation speed of gravitational interactions. We consider a class of solutions for which $α\rightarrow\infty$ at a finite cosmic time, resulting in $G\rightarrow0$ and $c_{\rm grav}\rightarrow0$. For an analytically tractable solution, we show that the final singularity occurs at finite conformal time and possesses a conformal structure compatible with a possible transition between successive cosmological cycles. The Tipler and Królak criteria indicate that the singularity is strong. We further discuss the physical consequences of the weakening gravitational interaction, including the dissolution of gravitationally bound structures and the shrinking of black-hole horizons. These effects suggest a possible mechanism leading toward an effectively radiation-dominated final state, which may be relevant for Conformal Cyclic Cosmology. The results provide a motivation for extending the analysis to more general scalar--tensor theories of gravity.

</details>


### [122] [Electric and magnetic Penrose processes, charged-particle collisions and superradiance around a Lorentz-violating dyonic black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27293)
*Fernando M. Belchior,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz-violating）的双荷Kalb-Ramond黑洞中的电磁能量提取、带电粒子碰撞、带电场超辐射（superradiance）及视界稳定性。该几何通过修正的渐近归一化和包含电、磁扇区的有效电荷与双荷Reissner-Nordström解区分。研究涉及电/磁Penrose过程、负能量态来源、逃逸条件及极端极限下的Bañados-Silk-West发散行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨洛伦兹破缺的双荷Kalb-Ramond黑洞能否通过电磁过程（如Penrose过程、超辐射和粒子碰撞）提取能量，并检验在此修改引力理论背景下视界的稳定性与经典黑洞特性的延续或改变。

Method: 在洛伦兹破缺的双荷Kalb-Ramond黑洞背景下，推导电性和磁性探针粒子的动力学方程，包括单极诱导的圆锥运动；构造电性和磁性Penrose过程作为衰变或碰撞机制；分析负能量态的来源（电磁正则能量而非几何能层）；推导出射产物的逃逸条件、过充电/过磁化可能性及质心能量。

Result: 电、磁通道分别由Φ_H=Q/[(1-ℓ)r_+]和Ψ_H=p/[(1-2ℓ)r_+]控制；非极端同向碰撞的质心能量有限，而迎头或近临界构型可产生大能量；在极端极限下，电或磁临界粒子在满足径向可达性条件时产生Bañados-Silk-West发散。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ显著影响电磁能量提取效率与碰撞能量行为。负能量态源自电磁正则能量而非几何能层，表明这类黑洞的能量提取机制与标准Reissner-Nordström情形在物理来源上有本质差异。极端极限下临界粒子碰撞可达到无穷大的质心能量，为高能粒子物理提供了潜在的研究平台。

Abstract: This paper is aimed at investigating electromagnetic energy extraction, charged-particle collisions, charged-field superradiance, and horizon stability in a Lorentz-violating dyonic Kalb-Ramond black hole. The geometry differs from the dyonic Reissner-Nordström solution through a modified asymptotic normalization and an effective charge combining electric and magnetic sectors. We derive the dynamics of electrically and magnetically charged probes, including monopole-induced conical motion, and formulate electric and magnetic Penrose processes as decay or collision mechanisms in which a negative-energy fragment falls into the horizon while another escapes with enhanced energy. The electric and magnetic channels are controlled by \(Φ_H=Q/[(1-\ell)r_+]\) and \(Ψ_H=p/[(1-2\ell)r_+]\), respectively. Negative-energy states arise from electromagnetic canonical energy rather than from a geometrical ergoregion. We also obtain escape conditions for the outgoing products, test the possibility of overcharging or overmagnetizing the black hole, and analyze the center-of-mass energy of charged-particle collisions. Nonextremal same-direction collisions remain finite, whereas head-on or near-critical configurations can become large. In the extremal limit, electrically or magnetically critical particles may generate the Bañados-Silk-West divergence when the radial reachability condition is fulfilled.

</details>


### [123] [Emergent vacua and stability constraints on black hole solutions in higher-dimensional $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27297)
*Nicolás Trullols Sandino,Andrei Galiautdinov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维 $f(R)$ 引力中的静态球对称真空解。以五维 Starobinsky 模型为起点，通过无鬼（ghost-free）稳定性判据 $f'(R)>0$ 证明纯 $R^2$ 几何修正无法动态产生稳定的有效宇宙学常数；推广到任意维度 $D$ 和单项曲率修正 $f(R)=R+\alpha R^n$ 后得到普遍稳定性界限 $n > D/2$；进一步表明引入包含高至 $\mathcal{O}(R^3)$ 的多项式层级展开可绕过限制，在 $D\ge5$ 中仅凭高阶几何项即可动态生成精确、全局无鬼的真空时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维 $f(R)$ 引力中能否仅通过纯几何修正（即无裸宇宙学常数）动态生成稳定真空解，并刻画由此产生的参数空间边界。

Method: 从五维 Starobinsky 模型 $f(R)=R+\alpha R^2-2\Lambda$ 出发，对常曲率时空施加无鬼稳定性判据 $f'(R)>0$；随后将分析推广到一般维度 $D$ 与单项修正 $f(R)=R+\alpha R^n$ 情形，推导普遍稳定性界限；最后扩展至包含高至 $\mathcal{O}(R^3)$ 的多项式层级引力作用量，并建立全局稳定性（$f'(R)>0$ 对所有 $R$ 成立）的精确参数空间边界。

Result: 五维情况下，纯 $R^2$ 修正无法在无裸宇宙学常数时产生稳定真空，即稳定有效宇宙学常数的存在必然依赖裸宇宙学常数；对于单项修正，稳定涌现真空存在的必要条件为 $n > D/2$；而引入多term多项式层级（包含至 $R^3$ 修正）后，扩展的几何自由度可同时满足迹约束与稳定性判据，在 $D\ge 5$ 中实现仅靠高阶几何项动态生成精确、全局无鬼的真空时空。

Conclusion: 高阶 $f(R)$ 修正中稳定涌现真空（emergent vacuum）的存在性与引力作用量的多项式结构密切相关：单项修正存在维度依赖的界限 $n>D/2$，多term层级修正则可在 $D\ge 5$ 中绕过此限制实现全局无鬼稳定真空。标度子（scalaron）质量条件 $m_s^2>0$ 未在本工作中完全施加，其对各模型的影响留待后续研究。

Abstract: We investigate static spherically symmetric vacuum solutions in higher-dimensional $f(R)$ gravity, beginning with the five-dimensional Starobinsky model governed by the action $f(R) = R + αR^2 - 2Λ$. By enforcing the ghost-free stability criterion $f'(R) > 0$ on constant scalar curvature spacetimes, we show that a stable effective cosmological constant cannot be dynamically generated from pure $R^2$ geometric corrections in five dimensions; its existence is inextricably tied to a bare cosmological constant. Generalizing this analysis to arbitrary dimensions $D$ and single-term curvature corrections $f(R) = R + αR^n$ with a vanishing bare cosmological constant, we derive a universal stability bound, $n > D/2$, required for the existence of stable emergent vacua. Finally, we demonstrate that expanding the gravitational action to a multi-term polynomial hierarchy circumvents this strict limitation. By including curvature corrections up to $\mathcal{O}(R^3)$, the extended geometric degrees of freedom simultaneously satisfy the trace constraint and the stability criterion. Furthermore, we establish the exact parameter space boundaries that ensure not only asymptotic vacuum stability but strict global stability ($f'(R) > 0$ for all $R$), allowing for the dynamical generation of exact, globally ghost-free vacuum spacetimes in $D \ge 5$ purely from higher-order geometric terms. The scalaron mass requirement, $m_s^2>0$ is not imposed in full in our analysis. A detailed investigation of its implications on the models considered in this work are left for future study.

</details>

### [124] [Statistics as a local phase: crystalline order and quench dynamics of emergent dimers in Ising gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26264)
*Umberto Borla,Riccardo Cioli,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究2+1维Z2格点规范理论（lattice gauge theory）强耦合极限下，底层玻色子或费米子统计如何传递给束缚态二聚体（dimer）复合粒子的机制。研究发现物质粒子的统计信息完全编码在一个局域跳跃相位中，并利用张量网络方法绘制了完整的基态相图，发现了二聚体对结晶成共振plaquette有序图案的新型带隙相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心物理问题在于理解微观粒子的玻色/费米统计在受限束缚形成复合粒子后如何存活并影响复合系统的行为，这涉及到涌现玻色子复合物（emergent bosonic composites）的本质统计来源问题。

Method: 研究在2+1维Z2格点规范理论的强耦合极限下进行，通过将受限电荷建模为有效二聚体模型，将统计信息编码为连续的局部跳跃相位（从0到π连续插值），并利用张量网络方法（tensor-network methods）求解基态相图；最后提出淬火协议（quench protocol）作为动力学探测二聚体统计性质的工具。

Result: 发现底层物质的统计完全由单一局部跳跃相位ϕ决定（玻色为0，费米为π），而相互作用与统计无关。该相位本身可驱动二聚体超流（dimer-superfluid）与二聚体电荷密度波（dimer charge density wave）之间的相变，磁性耦合则促使相邻二聚体结合为共振对，与二聚体间排斥竞争，最终确定了一个新的带隙相，其中二聚体对结晶为共振plaquette的有序排列。

Conclusion: 统计信息在复合粒子中并非被抹除，而是嵌入在复合粒子的动力学跳跃相位中；通过淬火协议可以区分统计性质并探测有序相，该方案可利用天然实现玻色自由度的量子模拟器（simulator）在实时演化信号中进行验证。

Abstract: How does the Bose or Fermi statistics of microscopic particles survive when confinement binds them into emergent bosonic composites? We address this question in the strong-coupling limit of a $2+1$D $\mathbb{Z}_2$ lattice gauge theory, where charges are confined into tightly bound pairs that can be described by an effective dimer model. We find that the statistics of the underlying matter is encoded entirely in a single local hopping phase $\varphi$ -$0$ for bosons, $π$ for fermions- while interactions remain statistics-independent. Treating $\varphi$ as a continuous parameter that interpolates between the two, we map the ground-state phase diagram with the help of tensor-network methods. The angle $\varphi$ itself drives a transition between a dimer-superfluid and dimer charge density wave state, while the magnetic coupling binds neighboring dimers into resonating pairs, in competition with the inter-dimer repulsion. We identify a novel gapped phase in which dimer pairs crystallize into an ordered pattern of resonating plaquettes. Finally, we propose a quench protocol under which identical dimer configurations evolve in markedly different ways depending on the statistics of their constituents. This provides a dynamical probe of the internal structure of dimers, and detects ordered phases through real time signatures, within reach of simulators that natively realize bosonic degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [125] [Time and Momentum Resolved Tunneling Spectroscopy of Floquet dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26270)
*Lucas Q. Silveira,Adrian E. Feiguin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间和动量分辨的隧穿谱（tunneling spectroscopy）协议，用于探测Floquet驱动系统在超越高频极限条件下的瞬时能谱，可捕捉涌现效应（emergent effects）和非绝热现象（non-adiabatic phenomena），而无需显式重构完整的时间依赖格林函数（Green's function）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动量子系统为设计新奇物态提供了强大途径，但大多数现有处理方法通常简化在高频极限（high-frequency limit）下，未考虑驱动开启过程中瞬态阶段发生的物理过程。因此需要一种能够直接探测瞬时能谱的方法来弥补这一不足。

Method: 作者引入一种具有时间和动量分辨率的隧穿谱协议，可直接探测Floquet驱动系统的瞬时能量谱。该方法首先在非相互作用费米子模型（non-interacting fermionic models）上进行基准测试，随后借助时间依赖密度矩阵重整化群（time-dependent density matrix renormalization group）技术推广至强关联系统。

Result: 该方法能够捕捉超越高频近似框架下的涌现效应与非绝热现象，并对共振驱动下带内态（in-gap states）的出现给出了微观描述，同时初步探索了其有限温度情形的对应行为。

Conclusion: 该隧穿谱协议提供了一种无需重构完整时间依赖格林函数即可探测Floquet系统瞬时能谱的有效工具，为研究和理解驱动系统中的瞬态过程及强关联效应提供了新途径。

Abstract: Periodically driven quantum systems provide a powerful route to engineer novel states of matter by controlling their effective Hamiltonians through external fields. However, most treatments are usually simplified by considering the high-frequency limit, and do not account for the processes taking place in the transient regime during the onset of the drive. In this work, we introduce a time and momentum resolved tunneling spectroscopy protocol to probe the instantaneous energy spectrum of Floquet-driven systems beyond the high-frequency regime, capturing both emergent effects and non-adiabatic phenomena without requiring explicit reconstruction of the full time-dependent Green's function. We benchmark the method on driven non-interacting fermionic models, and then generalize the approach to strongly correlated systems with the aid of time-dependent density matrix renormalization group techniques. We also provide a microscopic description to the emergence of in-gap states under resonant driving, and briefly explore its finite temperature analog.

</details>


### [126] [Dynamical signatures of deconfined spinons in dimerized sawtooth chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26347)
*Nishan Ranabhat,Brandon B. Le,Seung-Hun Lee,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过U(1)对称的密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）和含时变分原理（time-dependent variational principle, TDVP）方法，系统研究了锯齿链（sawtooth chain）上海森伯模型的基态性质与动力学响应，跨越二聚化相（dimerized phase）、精确可解的价键固体（valence-bond-solid, VBS）点及相边界。研究发现动力学结构因子（dynamical structure factor, DSF）始终表现为以低能亮带为主导的宽连续谱，并被识别为两个自旋子（spinon）退禁闭的解闭连续谱。研究为二聚化锯齿链中的分数化激发提供了微观图像，并适用于Ti³⁺氟化钾钒酸盐材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解锯齿链上海森伯模型中分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）的微观机制，特别是在精确可解VBS点之外更广泛参数区间内的行为。该研究对实验室合成材料——具有强各向异性交换相互作用的Ti³⁺氟化钾钒酸盐具有直接相关性。

Method: 采用U(1)对称的DMRG方法计算平衡态诊断量，并使用TDVP方法计算零温动力学结构因子S^zz(q,ω)。研究范围覆盖二聚化相内的三个区域：靠近连续下相边界、精确VBS点以及靠近一级上相边界。此外，在约束希尔伯特空间中进行了显式的有限分离双kink计算以复现强度分布。

Result: 在所有参数区中，DSF均为宽连续谱，由低边缘处的亮带主导，单triplon能量ω≃J_AB处的强度被抑制。谱被识别为两个简并单重态覆盖之间kink与反kink畴壁的解闭双自旋子连续谱。闭合形式的自旋子色散关系可以确定连续谱边缘并追踪各区域的主导带，显式的有限分离双kink计算成功复现了测量的强度分布。

Conclusion: 研究确立了锯齿链二聚化相中激发谱的分数化自旋子图像，为理解VBS点之外的动力学行为提供了统一框架。该微观图像对Ti³⁺氟化钾钒酸盐材料中的锯齿链构建单元具有直接实验可检验的预测意义。

Abstract: We study the ground-state properties and dynamical response of the Heisenberg model on the sawtooth chain in and away from the exactly solvable valence-bond-solid (VBS) point. We employ U(1)-symmetric density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) methods to compute equilibrium diagnostics and the zero-temperature dynamical structure factor (DSF) $S^{zz}(q,ω)$ across the dimerized phase, a valence-bond-ordered state in which the two symmetry-equivalent apex--base bonds of each triangle develop unequal spin correlations, probing the approach to the continuous lower phase boundary, the exact VBS point, and the approach to the first-order upper boundary. In every regime, the DSF is a broad continuum dominated by a bright band at its lower edge, with the intensity at the one-triplon energy $ω\simeq J_{AB}$ suppressed. We identify the spectrum as a deconfined two-spinon continuum of kink and antikink domain walls between the two degenerate singlet coverings. Closed-form spinon dispersions fix the continuum edges and track the dominant band across the zone in all three regimes, while an explicit finite-separation two-kink calculation in a constrained Hilbert space reproduces the measured intensity distribution. Our results provide a microscopic picture of fractionalized excitations in the dimerized sawtooth chain and are relevant to the recently discovered Ti$^{3+}$ kagome fluorides, where strongly anisotropic exchange interactions can generate sawtooth-chain building blocks.

</details>


### [127] [Competition between Geometrical Frustration and the Kondo Effect in CePdAl Revealed by High-Resolution Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26534)
*Yusei Shimizu,Shota Nakamura,Yoichi Ikeda,Yohei Kono,Shunichiro Kittaka,Toshiro Sakakibara,Yosikazu Isikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用高灵敏度磁力计在80 mK下观测到CePdAl中清晰的一级亚磁转变（first-order metamagnetic transition），且不伴随磁化平台，表明磁场通过抑制近藤效应（Kondo effect）和有序磁矩自旋翻转来解除几何阻挫；4.3 T处非线性磁化率的异常暗示极化顺磁态中存在非偶极关联，且高场区未发现非费米液体行为，为理解场致自旋液体态提供了关键热力学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CePdAl是一种具有准kagome结构的重费米子（heavy-fermion）化合物，其中几何阻挫（geometrical frustration）与近藤效应相互竞争，理解二者在磁场下的演变对于揭示其场致自旋液体态（spin-liquid state）的本质至关重要。

Method: 采用高灵敏度磁力计（high-sensitivity magnetometer），在极低温（80 mK）下对CePdAl进行磁场扫描，测量其磁化行为及非线性磁化率（nonlinear magnetic susceptibility）作为温度与磁场的函数。

Result: 在80 mK下观察到清晰的一级亚磁转变但不出现磁化平台，表明磁场抑制了近藤效应并导致有序磁矩自旋翻转，从而解除阻挫；4.3 T处非线性磁化率出现异常，暗示极化顺磁态（polarized paramagnetic state）中存在非偶极关联；高场区（反铁磁序完全被抑制后）未发现非费米液体行为（non-Fermi-liquid behavior）的证据。

Conclusion: 这些发现为理解CePdAl中场致自旋液体态建立了必要的热力学约束条件，明确了磁场调控近藤效应与几何阻挫竞争的宏观磁性表现。

Abstract: CePdAl is a heavy-fermion compound with a quasi-kagome structure, where geometrical frustration competes with the Kondo effect. Using a high-sensitivity magnetometer, we observe clear first-order metamagnetic transitions without magnetization plateaus in CePdAl at 80 mK, indicating a lifting of frustration through the suppression of the Kondo effect and spin flips of ordered moments under fields. An anomaly in the nonlinear magnetic susceptibility at 4.3 T suggests nondipolar correlations in the polarized paramagnetic state. Furthermore, we found no evidence of non-Fermi-liquid behavior in the high-field region, where the antiferromagnetic order is completely suppressed. These findings establish the essential thermodynamic constraints for understanding the field-induced spin-liquid state in CePdAl.

</details>


### [128] [Field-amplified readouts of weak altermagnetic exchange in MnF$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26540)
*Guowen Jiang,Feilong Wang,Yunhua Wang,Fawei Zheng,Bin Xi,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过过量程（overdetermined）第一性原理总能量映射方法，确定了教科书级双子晶格反铁磁体（two-sublattice antiferromagnet）MnF₂ 中符号反对称交换相互作用（sublattice-odd exchange）的强度为第七近邻不平衡 δJ₇ ≈ +8 μeV，并证明纵向磁场 B∥c 可将这一隐藏尺度线性放大，提供两个带符号的场线性读出通道，分别通过补偿场和固定场分裂对比来确认排斥体磁体的身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnF₂ 作为典型的排斥体磁体（altermagnet），其定义性符号反对称交换尚存争议，因为该交换只与偶极-偶极相互作用（dipole–dipole interaction）以平方形式耦合进入磁振子劈裂，导致其幅度被抑制且符号被抹去。因此需要一种方法来确定该微小能量尺度的大小与符号。

Method: 采用过量程的第一性原理总能量映射（overdetermined first-principles total-energy mapping）来解析该尺度，得到第七近邻不平衡 δJ₇ ≈ +8 μeV；随后将偶极-偶极相互作用显式纳入哈密顿量构建模型。利用纵向磁场 B∥c 作为线性放大器，通过计算补偿场 B*(Q) 以及固定场下伙伴动量处劈裂对比度两个观测量的场依赖行为来提取符号与幅度。

Result: 得到的哈密顿量成功复现了低能隙和有限动量处的可见劈裂。补偿场 B* ≃ 0.34 T，其相对于零场的移动本身即为有限不平衡存在的证据，方向决定符号，幅度决定尺度；在 1 T 磁场下，伙伴劈裂对比度约为 0.14 meV，是零场时的六倍。两个读出量在 0.12 meV 能量分辨率下均保持有效。

Conclusion: 该方案可推广到任意易轴共线排斥体磁体，使 μeV 量级的排斥体交换相互作用可在现有仪器分辨率范围内被实验探测，从而解决了 MnF₂ 中符号反对称交换的尺度与符号问题。

Abstract: MnF$_2$, the textbook two-sublattice antiferromagnet, has reemerged as a prototypical altermagnet, yet the sublattice-odd exchange that defines this identity remains under active debate: it enters the magnon splitting only in quadrature with the dipole--dipole interaction, its magnitude suppressed and its sign erased. An overdetermined first-principles total-energy mapping resolves this scale as a seventh-neighbor imbalance $δ{J_7}\simeq+8~μ$eV. The resulting Hamiltonian, with the dipole--dipole interaction included explicitly, reproduces the low-energy gap and the visible finite-momentum splitting. A longitudinal field $B\parallel c$ then acts as a linear amplifier of the hidden scale, opening two signed, field-linear readouts. The first is the compensation field $B^\ast(\mathbf Q)$, the position of minimum splitting, which is equal and opposite at the rotation-related partner momenta: a shift from zero field is itself evidence of a finite imbalance, its side gives the sign, and its magnitude, $|B^\ast|\simeq0.34$~T here, gives the scale. The second is the fixed-field contrast of the partner splittings, $\simeq0.14$~meV at $1$~T, six times the zero-field excess: a sign check from just two spectra. Both readouts survive a $0.12$~meV energy resolution, and the construction carries over to any easy-axis collinear altermagnet, bringing $μ$eV altermagnetic exchange within present instrumental reach.

</details>


### [129] [Thermally quenched metastability in metal-insulator transitions via elemental substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26609)
*Hiroshi Oike,Yasunori Takahashi,Keisuke Shibuya,Masaki Nakano,Tatsuki Hanada,Motoaki Hirayama,Hiroko Tokoro,Fumitaka Kagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过超过10^9 K s^-1的快速冷却过程在钨取代的VO2中动力学地规避了金属-绝缘体相变，稳定了一种长寿命的亚稳态金属相，从而首次在金属-绝缘体相变体系中实现了热猝灭（thermal quenching）诱导的亚稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热猝灭过程中系统无法向热力学基态平衡，从而揭示亚稳态相（如结构玻璃和淬火合金）。然而，由于金属-绝缘体相变由集体电子重组而非原子扩散所主导，在金属-绝缘体相变中能否实现热猝灭亚稳态一直悬而未决。

Method: 对钨取代的VO2晶体施加超过10^9 K s^-1的超快冷却速率，以动力学方式逃避金属-绝缘体相变；通过随温度变化的弛豫测量表征亚稳态金属相的稳定性与微观动力学机制。

Result: 超快冷却策略成功稳定了长寿命的亚稳态金属相；温度依赖的弛豫行为揭示了以成核为主导的动力学机制，并表明钨取代引入了热活化势垒。

Conclusion: 元素取代为在金属-绝缘体相变中实现热猝灭亚稳态提供了一条有效路径，将亚稳态的控制范围拓展至电子相。

Abstract: Thermal quenching inhibits equilibration toward the thermodynamic ground state during phase transitions, revealing metastable phases such as structural glasses and quenched alloys. Whether such thermally quenched metastability can be realized in metal-insulator transitions has remained an open question because these transformations are governed by collective electronic reorganization rather than atomic diffusion. We demonstrate that rapid cooling exceeding 10$^9$ K s$^{-1}$ kinetically avoids the metal-insulator transition, stabilizing a long-lived metastable metallic phase in tungsten-substituted VO$_2$. Temperature-dependent relaxation reveals nucleation-dominated kinetics with a thermal activation barrier introduced by tungsten substitution. Our results establish elemental substitution as a route to thermally quenched metastability in metal-insulator transitions, expanding metastable phase control to electronic phases.

</details>


### [130] [Metallogenic quantum criticality: Fermi surface nucleation at transitions between gapped phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27453)
*Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个违反传统范式的量子相变类型：在分隔两个全带隙绝缘体的量子临界点上，可以涌现出完整的巡游费米面（gapless Fermi surface），称为"产金属量子临界点"（metallogenic quantum critical point, MGQCP）。作者构造了控制性的有效场论框架，并提出周期性调制的量子霍尔双层作为最小微观实现，其临界理论展现出共形场论标度与非费米液体行为的独特混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相变理论基于伊辛模型等典范例子的组织原则认为，有能隙相之间相变的低能物理由尺度不变的量子场论支配，其在动量空间中仅在孤立点上具有无能隙自由度。本文旨在检验并挑战这一普遍原则，探索是否存在更广泛的新型量子临界现象。

Method: 作者构造了一个可控的有效场论家族（family of effective field theories）来描述MGQCP，用于描述不同分数量子霍尔绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators）之间的量子临界点。同时提出具有周期性调制的量子霍尔双层系统作为最小的微观实现平台。

Result: 临界理论展示了独特的物理信号混合：输运和可压缩性行为遵循与共形场论（conformal field theory）一致的标度律，而电子谱函数在费米动量处发展出功率律非解析性，这是非费米液体金属（non-Fermi-liquid metals）的特征。

Conclusion: MGQCP概念可能为具有可调Chern能带的莫尔材料（moiré materials）中的临界现象提供新视角。更一般地，本文结果指向一类更广泛的量子临界现象，其中动量空间中的扩展无能隙激发流形（extended manifolds of gapless excitations）可以涌现，尽管它们在两侧相邻相中均不存在。

Abstract: Continuous quantum phase transitions in interacting many-body systems exhibit universal phenomena that are largely independent of microscopic details. An organizing principle distilled from canonical examples such as the magnetic transition in Ising models is that the low-energy physics of transitions between gapped phases is governed by scale-invariant quantum field theories with gapless degrees of freedom at isolated points in momentum space. In this paper, we show that this principle can fail in a radical way: an entire gapless Fermi surface can emerge at a quantum critical point separating two fully gapped insulators. These exotic phase transitions are dubbed \textit{metallogenic quantum critical points} (MGQCP). We construct a controlled family of effective field theories for MGQCPs between distinct fractional Chern insulators and propose a periodically modulated quantum Hall bilayer as a minimal microscopic setting. The critical theory exhibits an unusual mixture of physical signatures: while transport and compressibility follow scaling laws compatible with a conformal field theory, the electron spectral function develops a power-law non-analyticity at the Fermi momentum, characteristic of non-Fermi-liquid metals. We discuss how the concept of MGQCP may shed new light on critical phenomena in moire materials with tunable Chern bands. More generally, our results point to a broader class of quantum critical phenomena in which extended manifolds of gapless excitations emerge in momentum space despite being absent in both neighboring phases.

</details>


### [131] [Interlayer-engineering of Charge Order Wave Vector in Kagome Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26751)
*Muntafa M. Mahi,Quazi D. M. Khosru,M. Zahid Hasan,Mahbub Alam,Md Shafayat Hossain*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算，调控CsV₃Sb₅层间距从单层极限到体相极限，发现层间耦合是连接竞争性4×1和2×2电荷序（charge order）倾向的控制参数，为理解kagome金属中多种电荷序波矢共存与竞争提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome金属AV₃Sb₅中电荷序与超导、向列序（nematicity）及时间反演对称破缺信号共存，但哪些电荷序波矢是内禀的、哪些由维度和晶格耦合选择仍不明确，体相2×2电荷序与表面探针观测的4×1调制之间的微观关系也尚未解决。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），通过调控CsV₃Sb₅的层间距从单层极限连续变化到体相极限，系统研究层间耦合对声子谱（phonon spectrum）稳定性的影响。

Result: 单层AV₃Sb₅中声子谱在M点无失稳，主导晶格不稳定性位于q=(1/4,0,0)，对应4×1调制；当层间耦合增强时，M点声子逐渐软化，在c≈12.24 Å处已变得不稳定，演化为体相特征性的强2×2失稳。

Conclusion: 层间耦合在固定化学计量比下作为控制参数，将竞争性的4×1与2×2电荷序倾向联系起来，为解释kagome金属中多种电荷序波矢共存与竞争提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Charge orders in the kagome metals AV$_3$Sb$_5$ sit at the center of a rich phase diagram that also includes superconductivity, nematicity, and signatures of time-reversal-symmetry breaking. Yet even the basic question of which charge ordering wave vectors are intrinsic, and which are selected by dimensionality and lattice coupling, remains unsettled. Importantly, the microscopic origin of different charge orders and, in particular, the relationship between the robust bulk $2 \times 2$ charge order and the controversial $4 \times 1$ modulation, which is primarily resolved by surface probes, remains unresolved. Here, we use first-principles calculations to study the role of interlayer coupling in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ by tuning the interlayer separation from the monolayer limit to the bulk limit. In the monolayer AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A = Rb, Cs), the phonon spectrum exhibits no instability at the M point; instead, the dominant lattice instability occurs at q = (1/4, 0, 0), consistent with a $4 \times 1$ modulation. As interlayer coupling increases in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$, an M-point phonon progressively softens and becomes unstable already near c = 12.24 Å, evolving into the strong $2 \times 2$ instability characteristic of the bulk. These results identify interlayer coupling as a control parameter at a fixed stoichiometry that links competing $4 \times 1$ and $2 \times 2$ tendencies, providing a unified framework for understanding why multiple charge-order wave vectors coexist and compete in kagome metals.

</details>


### [132] [3D- (H-theta-phi) magnetic phase diagram of antiferromagnetic metal GdB6 with electron and lattice instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27134)
*A. N. Azarevich,A. V. Bogach,T. F. Garipova,V. V. Voronov,M. Rajnak,S. Gabani,K. Flachbart,N. B. Bolotina,O. N. Khrykina,V. M. Gridchina,A. Yu. Tsvetkov,S. Yu. Gavrilkin,N. E. Sluchanko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高精度低温X射线衍射测量、磁阻与磁化强度的角度依赖研究，系统研究了立方晶格反铁磁金属GdB6中电荷输运与磁化各向异性的起源，发现静态Jahn-Teller畸变和动态电荷条纹（dynamic charge stripes）两种电子不稳定性，并构建了包含两种主要磁相AF(I)和AF(II)的磁场-温度相图以及H-phi平面中的叶轮型（impeller-type）磁相图，指出条纹电荷涨落抑制RKKY间接交换作用，进而产生独特的各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GdB6是一种具有立方晶格和Gd S型磁性离子的反铁磁金属，其电荷输运和磁化各向异性的微观起源尚不明确，需要借助精密实验表征来揭示关键的电子与晶格不稳定性及其与磁结构之间的关联。

Method: 采用高精度低温X射线衍射测量探测晶格畸变与动态电荷条纹；通过系统测量磁阻（magnetoresistance）和磁化强度（magnetization）的角度phi依赖性，构建H-T相图和(110)、(111)面内的H-phi磁相图。

Result: 低温X射线衍射揭示出小的静态Jahn-Teller畸变与纳米尺度的动态电荷条纹；构建了包含AF(I)和AF(II)两个主要磁相的H-T相图；在氦温度下于(110)和(111)面内获得了由径向和圆形边界分隔的叶轮型H-phi磁相图，其中AF(I)和AF(II)相被清晰区分。

Conclusion: 结果表明5d自旋密度波（spin density wave）分量在AF(II)态的磁结构中起重要作用；条纹中的电荷涨落抑制了沿<100>和<110>方向第一、第二近邻Gd3+离子间的RKKY间接交换，动态电荷条纹与振动耦合的Gd-Gd对共同导致了GdB6中异常的电荷散射各向异性和叶轮型相图。

Abstract: The origin of charge transport and magnetization anisotropy was studied in GdB6, an antiferromagnetic (AF) metal (Néel temperature TN ~ 15.5 K) with cubic lattice and Gd S-type magnetic ions. Both small static Jahn-Teller distortions and nanoscale electronic instabilities (dynamic charge stripes) were found in precise low temperature X-ray diffraction measurements. The detailed magnetic field (H) vs temperature (T) phase diagrams were constructed with two main magnetic phases AF(I) and AF(II). Using the angular phi-dependences of magnetoresistance and magnetization, impeller-type patterns of the H-phi magnetic phase diagrams in the (110) and (111) planes were found at helium temperatures, which included the AF phases I and II separated from each other by radial and circular boundaries. The results argue in favor of the important role of the spin density wave 5d- component in the magnetic structure of AF(II) state. Charge fluctuations in stripes are proposed to be responsible for the suppression of the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yoshida (RKKY) indirect exchange between the first and second neighboring Gd3+ ions located along the <100> and <110> directions. These dynamic charge stripes and vibrationally coupled Gd-Gd pairs produce unusual anisotropy of charge scattering and the impeller-type diagrams in GdB6 with S-type magnetic ion.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-orbital physics in inverse Lieb lattice altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27200)
*Mercè Roig,Jannik Gondolf,Andreas Kreisel,Brian M. Andersen,Daniel F. Agterberg*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文针对逆Lieb晶格（inverse Lieb lattice）上的d波交替磁性材料（altermagnetism），构建了包含子格和轨道自由度的对称性基微观哈密顿量，超越了仅含子格自由度的最小模型。通过研究钒氧硫族化合物（vanadium oxychalcogenide）家族多轨道电子关联物理，证明xy轨道对稳定单层V₂O中的交替磁序至关重要，而xz/yz轨道中的交替磁序通过Hund耦合诱导产生；这些多轨道模型还揭示了具有天然轨道选择性的拓扑边界态区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆Lieb晶格近期被视为交替磁性（altermagnetism）的有前景平台，多种该结构的材料被提出为d波交替磁性候选体；然而现有研究大多依赖仅含子格自由度的最小模型，缺乏对轨道自由度及其对电子关联和拓扑性质影响的系统考察。

Method: 构建基于对称性的微观哈密顿量，同时纳入子格与轨道（orbital）自由度；将该模型应用于钒氧硫族化合物家族，以研究多轨道电子关联物理，尤其关注费米能级附近主导的xy与xz/yz轨道特征。

Result: 证明xy轨道对稳定单层V₂O中观测到的交替磁态起关键作用，xz/yz轨道中的交替磁序则由Hund耦合诱导；多轨道模型进一步揭示出拓扑区域，其中拓扑边界态具有天然的轨道选择性。

Conclusion: 多轨道对称性建模揭示出单轨道子格模型无法捕获的轨道分辨物理，为理解d波交替磁性材料的关联电子行为和轨道选择性拓扑性质提供了新框架。

Abstract: The inverse Lieb lattice has recently emerged as a promising platform for altermagnetism, with several materials with this structure proposed as $d$-wave altermagnetic candidates. Here, we develop a symmetry-based microscopic Hamiltonian for these materials that includes both sublattice and orbital degrees of freedom, going beyond the sublattice-only minimal models that have been extensively used to study such altermagnets. We apply these models to examine multi-orbital electron correlation physics in the vanadium oxychalcogenide family altermagnets, which contain dominant $xy$ and $xz/yz$ orbitals character at the Fermi level in the altermagnetic state. We demonstrate that $xy$ orbitals are crucial to stabilize the altermagnetic state observed within a single V$_2$O layer, and altermagnetic order in the $xz/yz$ orbitals is induced through Hund's coupling. Additionally, we show that these multi-orbital models reveal topological regimes in which topological edge states are naturally orbital selective.

</details>


### [134] [A continuous confinement-deconfinement transition in a triangular quantum magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27411)
*Suguru Hosoi,Sejun Park,Michihiro Hirata,Minseong Lee,Adam P Dioguardi,Joe D Thompson,Filip Ronning,Allen O Scheie,Kumpei Imamura,Kenichiro Hashimoto,Takasada Shibauchi,Bishnu P Belbase,Arjun Unnikrishnan,Johannes Knolle,Arnab Banerjee,Yuji Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究在三角晶格反铁磁体TlYbS₂中观察到了具有不同激发类型（玻色子磁振子与费米子自旋子）的两相之间的连续相变，该相变类似于量子色动力学中的禁闭—解禁闭转变，表现为超出Ginzburg-Landau范式的分数化激发主导的连续转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子磁性领域，寻找连接具有不同激发性质的相（玻色子磁振子相与费米子自旋子相）的连续相变长期以来是重要目标，其与量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics）中的禁闭—解禁闭转变（confinement–deconfinement transition）具有类比关系。

Method: 研究者通过测量热电导（thermal conductivity）、磁化率（susceptibility）和核磁共振（NMR）Knight位移等实验手段，系统研究三角晶格反铁磁体TlYbS₂在c轴磁场作用下的磁性质与输运行为。

Result: 实验显示反铁磁序在T_N≈0.53 K以下建立；c轴磁场在μ₀H_c≈3 T处抑制该序，驱动体系进入具有自旋子费米面（spinon Fermi surface）的无能隙量子自旋液体（gapless quantum spin liquid）。在该相中观测到有限的热导率的线性剩余项、类Pauli磁化率（Pauli-like susceptibility）以及与温度无关的NMR Knight位移。接近H_c时，巡游激发的散射率显著增强而态密度无临界增强，这与常规磁量子临界性（magnetic quantum criticality）行为不同。

Conclusion: 这些结果指向一种由分数化激发（fractionalized excitations）支配的连续禁闭—解禁闭转变，其超越Ginzburg–Landau范式（Ginzburg–Landau paradigm）的描述框架。

Abstract: A continuous transition between phases hosting distinct excitations---bosonic magnons versus fermionic spinons---is a long-sought phenomenon in quantum magnetism, analogous to the confinement--deconfinement transition in quantum chromodynamics. We report evidence for such a transition in the triangular-lattice antiferromagnet TlYbS$_2$. Antiferromagnetic order develops below $T_\mathrm{N} \approx 0.53\,\mathrm{K}$. A $c$-axis field suppresses this order, driving the system into a gapless quantum spin liquid with a spinon Fermi surface above $μ_0 H_\mathrm{c} \approx 3\,\mathrm{T}$, evidenced by a finite residual linear term in thermal conductivity, a Pauli-like susceptibility, and a temperature-independent NMR Knight shift. Approaching $H_\mathrm{c}$ from above, the scattering rate of itinerant excitations is strongly enhanced while their density of states shows no critical enhancement, atypical of conventional magnetic quantum criticality. These results point to a continuous confinement--deconfinement transition governed by fractionalized excitations beyond the Ginzburg--Landau paradigm.

</details>


### [135] [Exact Stiffness and Dynamical Responses from Fock-Space Fragmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27446)
*Jonah Herzog-Arbeitman,Eslam Khalaf,Zhaoyu Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明量子几何嵌套（QGN）模型由于其Fock空间碎裂（Fock-space fragmentation）结构而具有异常强的可解性，并由此严格证明自发破缺连续对称性的刚度等于其在Gaussian流形上的变分值，验证了量子多体自举（quantum many-body bootstrap）中的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确可解的量子多体模型极为罕见，即便谱具有代数结构，动力学响应通常因涉及大量激发态而难以求解。作者旨在利用QGN模型的特殊结构实现超越谱层面的严格可解性。

Method: 利用Fock空间碎裂结构：在精确的无阻挫（frustration-free）基态上的激发可解耦为具有固定粒子-空穴算子数目的Krylov子空间，从而在动力学上保持不变。在此框架下分析无穷小相位扭转与基态的耦合方式。

Result: 证明了QGN模型中自发破缺连续对称性的刚度严格等于其在Gaussian流形上的变分值；证明无穷小相位扭转仅将基态耦合到单粒子-单空穴碎片，该碎片与变分流形中基态的切空间重合，因此变分曲率是精确的。更一般地，限制在固定Fock碎片内的扰动，其响应函数由相应的少体扇区精确确定，可实现静态磁化率、光学电导率、动力学结构因子和单粒子格林函数的精确求解。

Conclusion: QGN模型提供了一类具有Fock空间碎裂结构的精确可解多体系统，其动力学响应和静态响应均可通过少数体扇区严格获得，从而为量子多体可解性提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Exactly solvable quantum many-body models are rare, and even when their spectra are algebraically organized, dynamical responses generally remain difficult to obtain because they probe an extensive number of excited states. Here we show that quantum geometric nesting (QGN) models admit an unusually strong form of solvability rooted in \emph{Fock-space fragmentation}: excitations on top of the exact frustration-free ground states decouple into Krylov subspaces with a fixed number of particle and hole operators, and hence remain dynamically invariant. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the stiffness of the spontaneously broken continuous symmetry in QGN models is exactly equal to its variational value in the Gaussian manifold, confirming a conjecture from quantum many-body bootstrap~\cite{GaoHanKhalaf2026}. The proof shows that an infinitesimal phase twist couples the ground state only to the one-particle, one-hole fragment, which coincides with the tangent space of the ground state within the variational manifold, thereby making the variational curvature exact. More generally, perturbations whose action remains within a fixed Fock-space fragment have response functions determined exactly by the corresponding few-body sector, enabling exact access to quantities including static susceptibility, optical conductivity, dynamical structure factors, and single-particle Green's functions.

</details>
