### [136] [The Role of Geometric Analysis in Interferometer Design and Optimization for Gravitational Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26420)
*Alireza Maleki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个大质量粒子间量子纠缠作为探测引力量子性质的途径，指出以往干涉仪分析中忽略竖直轨迹段相位贡献的近似可能导致错误预测，甚至在某些参数区域产生伪零结果。作者推导了包含完整粒子轨迹的精确解，证明竖直臂会显著影响累积相位，并发现简化处理会遗漏纠缠完全消失的构型，因此完整考虑干涉仪几何构型对于准确评估引力诱导纠缠至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论旨在统一广义相对论与量子力学，但普朗克能标（Planck scale）下的极高能量使得直接实验证据难以获取。近期提案指出两个大质量粒子间的量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）可能为探测引力的量子本质提供途径，因此有必要审视这些提案中常用的近似假设是否可靠。

Method: 作者系统考察了提案中的干涉仪（interferometer）几何构型，重点分析忽略粒子轨迹竖直段相位贡献这一常用近似的有效性。研究推导了包含完整粒子轨迹的精确解，并与简化近似结果进行对比，系统扫描了相关参数区域以识别差异。

Result: 分析表明，忽略竖直段相位贡献的近似可导致错误预测，并在特定参数区间产生本应存在纠缠（entanglement）时的零结果。精确解显示竖直臂可显著影响累积相位，且存在诱导纠缠完全消失的构型，这是简化处理无法捕捉的特征。

Conclusion: 完整考虑干涉仪几何构型并非仅仅是一种精细化处理，而是准确评估引力诱导纠缠的必要条件。研究结果对基于纠缠探测的量子引力实验设计具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: The pursuit of a quantum theory of gravity, aiming to unify general relativity and quantum mechanics, remains one of the most enduring challenges in physics. Because of the extreme energy scales associated with the Planck regime, direct experimental evidence for quantum gravity remains elusive. However, recent proposals suggest that quantum entanglement between two massive particles may provide a pathway to probe the quantum nature of gravity. In this study, we examine the interferometer geometries proposed in these works, with particular attention to the commonly used approximation that neglects phase contributions from the vertical segments of the particle trajectories. Our analysis shows that this approximation can lead to incorrect predictions and, in certain parameter regimes, to null results where entanglement would otherwise be expected. We derive exact solutions that incorporate the full particle trajectories and demonstrate that the vertical arms can significantly affect the accumulated phase. Crucially, we identify configurations in which the induced entanglement vanishes entirely, a feature missed by simplified treatments. These findings show that accounting for the full interferometer geometry is not merely a refinement, but is essential for accurately assessing gravity-induced entanglement.

</details>


### [137] [Fast GHZ encoding with $1-o(1)$ fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26241)
*Laura Shou,T. C. Mooney,Twesh Upadhyaya,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在拥有全连接2-局部哈密顿量（all-to-all 2-local Hamiltonians）且2量子比特相互作用项有界的条件下，可在时间O(log N/N)内实现保真度为(1-o(1))的N量子比特GHZ态编码，该时间尺度饱和了理论下界Ω(log N/N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在哈密顿量框架下生成大规模GHZ纠缠态的最快时间下界问题，探讨是否可以通过精心设计的哈密顿量动力学突破此前已知的实现速度限制。

Method: 将自旋压缩动力学（spin-squeezing dynamics）与量子信号处理（quantum signal processing）相结合，构造特定协议的编码方案；同时通过时间重缩放推导功率律相互作用强度1/r^γ（γ<d，维度d）下哈密顿量的信令时间下界。

Result: 实现了O(log N/N)时间内的(1-o(1))保真度N比特GHZ编码，饱和理论下界Ω(log N/N)；该结果经重缩放后同样饱和功率律相互作用哈密顿量的信令时间下界。

Conclusion: 在全连接2-局部哈密顿量框架下，GHZ态编码的速度下限已被达到，表明此类纠缠制备任务在时间效率上已达到物理最优；并推广到了更一般的功率律相互作用场景。

Abstract: We prove that $(1-o(1))$-fidelity $N$-qubit Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) encoding can be performed in time $O(\log N/N)$ using all-to-all 2-local Hamiltonians with bounded 2-qubit interaction terms. This saturates the theoretical lower bound $Ω(\log N/N)$, and by rescaling, also saturates the lower bound on signaling time for Hamiltonians with power-law interaction strengths $1/r^γ$, $γ<d$ in dimension $d$. The protocol uses spin-squeezing dynamics combined with quantum signal processing.

</details>


### [138] [Quantizing the guiding center: do quantization and coarse graining commute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26277)
*Rodrigo Andrade e Silva,Ted Jacobson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种基于群论的（group-theoretic）非微扰量子化方案，应用于二维磁场中带电粒子漂移的有效导向中心理论（effective guiding center theory），并与底层微观理论的粗粒化结果进行比较。研究发现量子化有效理论的一般预测与微观理论一致，但在闭合等磁通轮廓上会出现微观理论所不具备的"半径"量子化，表明有效理论的量子化可能产生虚假的非微扰预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效理论（effective theory）的量子化是否总能忠实反映底层微观物理，是一个基础且重要的问题。本文旨在探索导向中心有效理论的非微扰量子化及其与微观理论的对应关系，以检验有效理论量子化可能引入的伪影效应。

Method: 采用非微扰的群论量子化方案对经典的导向中心有效理论进行量子化，并建立微观理论与有效量子理论之间的匹配（matching）规则，通过比较两者的预测来检验有效理论的可靠性。该有效理论的约化相空间（reduced phase space）即物理空间本身，量子化导致空间坐标非对易（noncommuting），系统仅保留面积结构（area structure）而丧失度量结构（metric structure）。

Result: 通过匹配规则的构建，量子化有效理论的预测在一般情况下与微观理论一致。然而，对于闭合等磁通轮廓（closed isomagnetic contours），有效理论预测出"半径"的量子化，即一种微观理论中不存在空间离散性。这一虚假预测表明有效理论的量子化可能产生非微扰的伪预言。

Conclusion: 即使一个量子化的有效理论在内部不显现任何崩溃的迹象，它仍可能产生虚假的非微扰预测。因此，有效理论的量子化并不总能忠实反映微观物理，需要谨慎对待其非微扰预言。

Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative, group-theoretic quantization of the effective guiding center theory of a charged particle drifting in a magnetic field in two spatial dimensions, and compare the resulting quantum theory with a corresponding coarse graining of the underlying microscopic theory. In the classical effective theory, the small gyro motion is not resolved, while the motion of the center of the gyro orbit remains observable. The reduced phase space is the physical space itself, so quantization leads to noncommuting spatial coordinates, and the effective theory loses access to the metric structure of physical space, retaining only its area structure. By formulating a prescription to match between the microscopic and effective quantum theories, we find that the predictions of the quantized effective theory are generally consistent with those of the microscopic theory. However, for closed isomagnetic contours the effective theory predicts a quantization of ``radius'', a spatial discreteness absent from the microscopic theory. This illustrates that quantization of an effective theory may yield spurious nonperturbative predictions, even if that quantum theory shows no internal signs of breakdown.

</details>


### [139] [Lieb-Schultz-Mattis Constraints for Quantum Channels: A Spacetime-Duality View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26266)
*Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Yu-Jie Liu,Tsung-Cheng Lu,Frank Pollmann,Yizhi You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过时空对偶（spacetime duality）将带有混合反常（mixed anomaly）的重复量子通道（repeated quantum channel）的刘维尔算符（Liouvillian）映射为高维混合态对称保护拓扑（mSPT）相，揭示了LSM约束在开放量子系统动力学中的体现，并引入扭转载荷Renyi-N关联函数（TRNC）作为时序关联探针，证明其与mSPT奇怪关联函数对偶，从而为长程时间序提供体—边界诊断路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子反常（quantum anomaly）对多体系统的行为施加了强约束，经典的Lieb-Schultz-Mattis（LSM）定理将紫外对称性与填充约束关联到红外能谱特征。但已有研究主要聚焦于平衡态谱学，尚未系统回答LSM约束如何塑造开放量子系统中的动力学特征与时序关联（temporal correlations）。

Method: 作者采用时空对偶映射，将d维重复量子通道（具有强S对称性与弱G对称性之间的混合反常）的刘维尔算符映射到一个(d+1)维的混合态对称保护拓扑（mSPT）相。在该对应关系中，通道的初态与稳态被视为体投影存在下的高维mSPT边界态。此外，作者引入扭转载荷Renyi-N关联函数（TRNC）作为通道时序关联的探针，证明其对偶于mSPT奇怪关联函数（strange correlator）。最后，作者提出刘维尔奇异谱（Liouvillian singular spectrum）作为量子反常的更基本诊断量，并证明其对偶于mSPT的算子纠缠谱（operator entanglement spectrum）。

Result: 研究表明，重复量子通道的动力学可由高维mSPT相结构完整刻画；TRNC与mSPT奇怪关联函数之间的对偶关系提供了诊断由LSM反常所蕴含的长程时间序的直接体—边界路径。文中识别出刘维尔奇异谱而非刘维尔谱本身，是量子反常的更基本诊断工具，并建立其与mSPT算子纠缠谱的对偶关系。

Conclusion: 本工作将LSM定理的约束扩展到开放量子系统的动力学领域，通过体—边界对偶建立了重复量子通道时序关联与高维mSPT相结构之间的桥梁，为量子反常的动力学诊断提供了新的理论框架和具体探针。

Abstract: Quantum anomalies strongly constrain the possible behavior of many-body systems. A prime example is the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis (LSM) theorem, which relates UV symmetry and filling constraints to IR features of the energy spectrum and ground-state structure. Here, we ask how LSM constraints shape dynamical signatures and temporal correlations in open quantum systems. Using a spacetime duality, we show that the Liouvillian of a $d$-dimensional repeated quantum channel with a mixed anomaly between strong $S$ and weak $G$ symmetry can be mapped to a $(d{+}1)$-dimensional mixed-state symmetry-protected topological (mSPT) phase. Under this correspondence, the initial and steady states of the channel are identified with boundary states of the higher-dimensional mSPT in the presence of bulk projection. We further introduce the twisted Renyi-$N$ correlator (TRNC) as a probe of temporal correlations in the channel and demonstrate that it is dual to the mSPT strange correlator, providing a direct bulk-boundary route to diagnose long-range temporal order implied by the LSM anomaly. Finally, we identify the \textit{Liouvillian singular spectrum}, rather than the Liouvillian spectrum itself, as a more fundamental diagnostic of quantum anomalies, and show that it is dual to the operator entanglement spectrum of the mSPT.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Chaos and Quantum Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27350)
*Jordan Cotler,Felipe Hernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用量子最优输运理论（quantum optimal transport）为量子混沌（quantum chaos）建立了严格的数学基础，定义了无发散问题的量子李雅普诺夫指数（quantum Lyapunov exponents），在准经典极限下可恢复经典全局扩张率，并阐明了out-of-time-order相关子的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典系统的混沌性可由李雅普诺夫指数刻画，但直接将该框架推广到量子力学面临持续性挑战：量子态的波动本质和量子相空间的非交换几何阻碍了经典混沌理论的直接泛化。

Method: 作者借助量子最优输运理论构建度量扩展量子分布间距离的几何基础，据此定义量子李雅普诺夫指数，以避免直接推广经典指数时出现的发散问题。

Result: 该框架表明在准经典极限下，量子李雅普诺夫指数可恢复到经典的全局扩张率；当两者一致时即为通常的最大李雅普诺夫指数，并进一步阐明了out-of-time-order相关子作为量子混沌诊断工具的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了量子最优输运作为量子混沌理论的统一数学基础，为从简单少体模型到复杂多体系统提供刻画量子动力学行为的全新工具。

Abstract: Chaos in classical systems can be characterized by Lyapunov exponents that measure the exponential divergence of nearby trajectories, but directly extending this framework to quantum mechanics has been a persistent challenge. The wavelike nature of quantum states and the non-commutative geometry of quantum phase space obstruct a straightforward generalization of classical chaos theory. Here we develop a rigorous approach to quantum chaos by leveraging quantum optimal transport theory, which provides the missing geometric foundation for measuring distances between extended quantum distributions. We define quantum Lyapunov exponents that naturally avoid divergences encountered when naïvely generalizing classical exponents, and show that in the semiclassical limit they recover the classical global expansion rate, and hence the usual maximal Lyapunov exponent when they coincide. Our framework provides a tight connection between the divergence of classical trajectories and semiclassical phase space evolution, and additionally clarifies the role of out-of-time-order correlators as diagnostics of quantum chaos. These results establish quantum optimal transport as a unifying mathematical foundation for quantum chaos theory, providing new tools to characterize dynamical behavior across the full range of quantum dynamics from simple few-body models to complex many-body systems.

</details>


### [141] [On supporting affine functionals for Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27363)
*A. S. Holevo,M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造一个显式反例，证明了当量子态ρ是简并态（degenerate state）时，纠缠形成熵（entanglement of formation, EoF）的凸屋顶（convex roof）结构和子系统A、B的有限维性并不能保证全局支撑仿射泛函（supporting affine functional）的存在性，即使A和B都是最简单的qubit系统也是如此。此外，本文还描述了有限维和无限维双体量子系统中，局部和全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并据此给出了有限秩态上EoF的Lipschitz下半连续界（Lipschitz lower semicontinuity bounds）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的多篇文献假设，基于纠缠形成熵EoF的凸屋顶结构以及子系统A和B的有限维性，可以保证在系统AB的任何态ρ上，EoF都存在全局支撑仿射泛函，即在任意态σ下满足E_F(ρ)=TrΛ_ρ ρ且E_F(σ)≥TrΛ_ρ σ的厄米算子Λ_ρ。作者试图检验这一假设是否在简并态情况下依然成立，并发现即使在最简情形（A、B为qubit系统）下该假设也并非普遍正确。

Method: 作者利用支撑仿射泛函在态ρ处的存在性与EoF在该态处的Lipschitz下半连续性（Lipschitz lower semicontinuity）等价这一关键事实，借助Wootters公式（Wootters' formula）并利用工具“Claude Fable 5”构造了一个不满足该性质的双qubit系统态ρ作为显式反例。同时，作者研究了有限维和无限维双体量子系统中局部与全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并利用这些条件推导有限秩态上EoF的Lipschitz下半连续界。

Result: 作者在文章中给出了一个具体的反例，表明当ρ为简并态时，即使A和B均为qubit系统，EoF在ρ处也可能不存在支撑仿射泛函。与此同时，作者描述了支撑仿射泛函存在的充分条件，并导出了形如E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)≤C_ρ‖ρ-σ‖_1的Lipschitz下半连续界，涵盖了对支持集受限和不受限的态σ两种情形。

Conclusion: 本文推翻了以往文献中关于凸屋顶结构和子系统有限维性足以保证EoF支撑仿射泛函存在性的普遍假设，为理解EoF在简并态下的分析性质提供了新的视角，并给出了Lipschitz下半连续性的实用估计式，对有限秩态下EoF的稳定性分析具有理论意义。

Abstract: In several articles, the authors assume that the convex roof structure of the EoF and finite-dimensionality of subsystems $A$ and $B$ guarantee the existence of the (global) supporting affine functional for the EoF at any state of the system $AB$. This means that for any state $ρ$ of $AB$ there is a Hermitian operator $Λ_ρ$ on $\mathcal{H}_{AB}=\mathcal{H}_A\otimes\mathcal{H}_B$ such that $E_F(ρ)=\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρρ$ and $E_F(σ)\geq\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρσ$ for any state $σ$ of $AB$. We present an explicit example showing that, when $ρ$ is degenerate, this is not true even in the simplest case when $A$ and $B$ are qubit systems. The construction is based on the fact that the existence of a supporting affine functional for the EoF at a state $ρ$ is equivalent to the Lipschitz lower semicontinuity of the EoF at this state $ρ$. We use Wootters' formula and the help of Claude Fable 5 to find a state $ρ$ of the system $AB$ for which the latter property does not hold.
  We also describe conditions for the existence the local and global supporting affine functionals for the EoF at a given state of both finite and infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum systems. These conditions allow us to find Lipschitz lower semicontinuity bounds for the EoF at a given finite rank state $ρ$ (i.e. inequalities of the form $\,E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)\leq C_ρ\|ρ-σ\|_1$) with and without restrictions on the support of the state $σ$.

</details>


### [142] [Krylov Break Times from an Inhomogeneous Lieb--Robinson Light Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27399)
*Shunji Matsuura,Yoji Kawamura,Joseph Salfi,Satoshi Iso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Krylov截断（Krylov truncation）的有效时间尺度问题，指出截断可靠时间由Jacobi链上的因果传播决定，并证明了在非均匀输运度量下的Lieb–Robinson界，给出了截断误差的下界及break time的严格估计。对于谱局部激发的情形，截断可在任意长时间内保持精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krylov和Lanczos近似广泛应用于量子动力学、量子子空间方法和哈密顿量学习，但一个实际问题是：m维Krylov截断在多久时间内是可信的？这一时间尺度的明确定义和严格界至今尚不清楚，需要建立理论框架来回答。

Method: 将Krylov截断的可靠性问题归结为Jacobi链上的因果传播问题，引入非均匀输运度量ρ(m,n)=Σ_{j=min(m,n)}^{max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j作为相关信息距离，证明该度量下的Lieb–Robinson界；结合Duhamel公式获得截断动力学误差的下界，并通过定义break time t_*(m;ε)给出截断保真度的量化保证。

Result: 证明了Lieb–Robinson速度v_LR=2，误差在锥ρ(m,n)≃2|t|之外指数抑制；推导出break time下界t_*(m;ε)≥τ_m[1-o(1)]，其中τ_m=Σ_{j<m}1/b_j；在探针沿链传播时该下界是紧的，即t_*(m)≃τ_m；但当探针仅激发谱的局部或已被截断分辨的部分时，超过临界Krylov维数m_*后break time趋于无穷。数值实验（自旋链和随机Jacobi矩阵）支持输运受限区域中t_*(m)≃τ_m。

Conclusion: Krylov截断的可靠时间由信息在Jacobi链上的输运时间决定，其round-trip效应给出了误差的下界。在输运受限机制下break time约为τ_m，而在谱局部激发情形下截断可持久精确，这为实际量子动力学模拟中选择合适的截断维数提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Krylov and Lanczos approximations are used in quantum dynamics, quantum subspace methods, and Hamiltonian learning. A practical question is how long an $m$-dimensional Krylov truncation can be trusted. We argue that this time is fixed by causal propagation on the associated Jacobi chain. The relevant distance is not the Krylov index itself, but the inhomogeneous transport metric $ρ(m,n) = \sum_{j=\min(m,n)}^{\max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j$, where $b_j$ is the Lanczos hopping across the bond $j \leftrightarrow j+1$. We prove a Lieb--Robinson bound in this metric. Its small-weight limit gives the velocity $v_{\rm LR} = 2$, meaning that propagation is exponentially suppressed outside the cone $ρ(m,n) \simeq 2|t|$. The error of a finite Krylov approximation to the return amplitude is a round-trip effect: information has to travel from the probe to the truncation boundary and back. Combining the Lieb--Robinson bound with Duhamel's formula yields a lower bound on the error of the truncated dynamics. For a fixed tolerance $ε$, let the break time $t_\ast(m;ε)$ denote the longest time for which the $m$-dimensional truncation is guaranteed to reproduce the exact return amplitude within error $ε$. We show that $t_\ast(m;ε) \ge τ_m[1-o(1)]$, where $τ_m = ρ(0,m) = \sum_{j<m} 1/b_j$. When the probe spreads along the chain, this lower bound is also tight, so $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$. The situation is different when the probe excites only a localized part of the spectrum, or a part already resolved by the truncation. In this case, essentially no signal reaches the boundary. Beyond a state-dependent Krylov dimension $m_\ast$, the approximation can therefore remain accurate at all times, and the break time is effectively infinite. Numerical tests on spin chains and random Jacobi matrices support $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$ in the transport-limited regime.

</details>


### [143] [Dynamics of local quantum information in random unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27440)
*Ratul Thakur,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了局域随机酉电路中局域量子信息动力学的理论框架，利用信息格（information lattice）系统化各长度尺度上的信息内容，揭示了信息分布的动力学行为：该分布以Tracy-Widom形式弹道式（ballistically，∝t）向更大尺度移动，伴随~t^{1/3}的展宽。在Haar随机电路（无限局域希尔伯特空间维度）和随机Clifford电路（量子比特）两种不同设定中均观察到相同的定性行为，暗示其普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中信息打乱（information scrambling）的物理与热化和混沌涌现密切相关，但标准刻画手段（二分纠缠、算符增长）本质上是粗粒度的，无法揭示量子信息如何在系统不同区域和不同长度尺度间流动的时空解剖结构。作者旨在建立一种细粒度的理论，解析局域信息的微观结构及其动力学。

Method: 作者基于信息格框架，对每个子系统内各长度尺度的信息含量进行系统化分类，追踪信息在不同长度尺度上的分布及其随时间的演化。对于局域希尔伯特空间维度为无限的Haar随机电路，将此动力学映射为精确的经典随机过程；对于作用于量子比特的随机Clifford电路，发展了一个特定的规范下稳定子生成元（stabiliser generators）动力学的唯象马尔可夫过程（Markov process）。

Result: 研究发现，信息所在长度尺度的分布由Tracy-Widom形式描述，随时间以弹道方式（∝t）向更大尺度移动，并伴随~t^{1/3}的涨落展宽。Haar随机电路与随机Clifford电路两种相当不同的设定中均观察到相同的定性行为，表明该结果的普适性。Clifford电路的马尔可夫过程也独立确认了Tracy-Widom分布和t^{1/3}的涨落标度。

Conclusion: 该工作以长度尺度分辨（length scale-resolved）的方式确立了局域量子信息动力学的普适性质，为将信息动力学的精确微观理论与涌现的信息流流体动力学描述（hydrodynamic descriptions）之间的桥梁提供了可能的路径。

Abstract: The physics of information scrambling in quantum many-body systems is intimately related to thermalisation and emergence of chaos. However, its standard characterisation through bipartite entanglement or operator growth remains inherently coarse-grained, obscuring the spatiotemporal anatomy of how quantum information flows between different regions of the system and across various length scales. In this work, we develop a theory for the dynamics of local information in local random unitary circuits which resolves the fine-grained microscopic structures of local information flow in such systems. Using the framework of information lattice which systematises the information content at each length scale within each subsystem, we identify a distribution of length scales at which information resides and obtain the dynamics of the distribution. For Haar-random circuits with infinite local Hilbert-space dimensions, we map this dynamics onto an exact classical stochastic process which reveals that this distribution is described by a Tracy-Widom form which moves ballistically in time ($ \propto t$) toward larger scales, accompanied by a $\sim t^{1/3}$ broadening. We also find the same qualitative behaviour for random Clifford circuits acting on qubits. The similar scaling behaviour in two rather different settings hints strongly towards the universality of our results. In the case of Clifford circuits, we develop a phenomenological Markov process for the dynamics of the stabiliser generators in a specific gauge which confirms the Tracy-Widom distribution and the $t^{1/3}$ scaling of the fluctuations. Ultimately, our results establish the universal properties of the dynamics of local quantum information in a length scale-resolved fashion and provide a possible route towards bridging exact microscopic theories for information dynamics with emergent hydrodynamic descriptions of information flow.

</details>


### [144] [Spectral Fingerprints of Gauge Theories on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27457)
*Graham Van Goffrier,Debasish Banerjee,Bipasha Chakraborty,Emilie Huffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了最大混合态谱采样（maximally mixed state spectral sampling）这一无偏量子算法如何聚焦于哈密顿量谱的任意目标区域，并在(1+1)维非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论上进行了演示，实现了深度为78个双量子比特门的电路仿真，同时提出通过泡利框架优化编译受控时间演化的新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大混合态谱采样算法能够从哈密顿量提取整个允许能量范围内的有限分辨率谱分布，但如何将其聚焦到谱的特定区域（如基态附近或谱中段）以研究量子临界性和热化行为，仍需要系统的方法验证与硬件演示。

Method: 将谱采样算法聚焦于谱的任意目标区域；在(1+1)维非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论上实施，利用深度为78个双量子比特门的近端超导量子硬件电路进行仿真；利用近期硬件中新兴的动态电路能力将所需量子比特数大约减半；提出基于泡利框架优化（Pauli-frame optimization）的受控时间演化编译策略；指出该方法的量子读出误差缓解是平凡的。

Result: 成功演示了在非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论上执行该算法所需的所有步骤；展示谱采样在无序多体相变和中段谱态密度两种物理场景中的应用；提出将量子比特数约减半并简化误差缓解的具体方案。

Conclusion: 该算法能够获取模型从基态量子临界现象到中段热化行为的完整谱指纹，且适合在近端超导量子硬件上结合动态电路能力运行，为谱学量子模拟提供了一种高效且实用的方案。

Abstract: Maximally mixed state spectral sampling is an unbiased quantum algorithm that allows for extraction of a finite-resolution spectral distribution from a Hamiltonian over potentially the entire allowed range of energies. We show how it may be focused on any desired area of the spectrum in order to learn about the full \textit{fingerprint} of the model of interest: from its ground state phenomena such as quantum criticality, obtained from the lowest lying energies, to its thermalization behavior, obtained from the mid-spectrum. We demonstrate this technique specifically on a $(1+1)d$ non-Abelian $SO(3)$ gauge theory, providing a comprehensive analysis of the steps necessary for performing this algorithm, as well as what is possible in the near-term with superconducting quantum hardware, performing simulations with circuits that are $78$ two-qubit gates deep. We show how this algorithm is able to take advantage of emerging dynamical circuit capabilities in near-term hardware to roughly halve the number required qubits, as well as how quantum readout error mitigation is trivial for this method. Along the way, we propose a novel strategy for compiling the controlled-time evolutions needed for spectral sampling by means of Pauli-frame optimizations. We illustrate two physical applications of quantum spectral sampling -- disordered many-body transitions, and mid-spectrum densities of states -- and what postprocessing steps they require beyond the Fourier outputs of the algorithm.

</details>


### [145] [Least Variable Quantum Counting Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26240)
*Bita Olamaei,Florian Meier,Costantino Budroni,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad,Giuseppe Vitagliano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限内存条件下经典与量子计数过程的时间计时精度问题，推导出经典d态计数过程的有限内存方差下界（finite-memory variance bound），并通过数值优化发现量子计数过程可以打破这一经典界限，在相同内存和平均滴答时间下实现更小的首次滴答波动（first-tick fluctuations），确立了有限内存量子优势（quantum advantage）在时间精度方面的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计数过程（counting processes）广泛描述从光子探测到时钟滴答等各种随机事件，一个核心问题是在仅有有限内存资源（finite memory resources）的条件下这些事件能被多精确地计时。作者旨在回答该基本问题，探讨经典与量子机制下的时间计时极限与量子优势。

Method: 在有限维经典和量子计数过程的统一框架内进行研究。作者推导了一个严格的有限内存方差下界，该下界对每个经典d态计数过程均成立，并被一类离散步进型Erlang阶梯过程（discrete Erlang-type ladder process）所饱和（saturate）。通过数值优化（numerical optimization）寻找打破该经典界限的量子计数过程；对于量子比特（qubit）情形，在单Kraus无滴答族（single-Kraus no-tick family）内进一步推导了解析的渐近大均值界限。

Result: 数值优化识别出能够违反经典方差下界的量子计数过程，在相同内存大小和平均滴答时间下实现比任何经典过程更小的首次滴答波动。量子比特情形的解析结果进一步表明，在此类中量子优势可渐近保持（persist asymptotically）。优化后的量子过程展现出相干条件动力学（coherent conditioned dynamics），并随均值增大趋向于连续时间量子跳跃（continuous-time quantum-jump）描述。

Conclusion: 研究确立了有限内存量子优势在时间精度上的存在，并将离散时间计数过程与连续时间量子计时的机制联系起来。

Abstract: Counting processes provide a fundamental description of stochastic events ranging from photon detection to clock ticks. A central question is how accurately such events can be timed when only finite memory resources are available. Here, we investigate this problem within a general framework of finite-dimensional classical and quantum counting processes. We derive a rigorous finite-memory variance bound obeyed by every classical $d$-state counting process, which is tight and saturated by a discrete Erlang-type ladder process. Through numerical optimization, we identify quantum counting processes that violate this classical bound, achieving smaller first-tick fluctuations than any classical process with the same memory size and mean tick time. For the qubit case, we further derive an analytical large-mean bound within a single-Kraus no-tick family, showing that the quantum advantage persists asymptotically within this class. The optimized quantum processes exhibit coherent conditioned dynamics and approach a continuous-time quantum-jump description as the mean increases. Our results establish a finite-memory quantum advantage in temporal precision and connect discrete-time counting processes with continuous-time quantum timekeeping.

</details>


### [146] [Adiabatic Otto-like quantum thermodynamical cycle in the non-quasi-static regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26690)
*Salvador J. Robles-Pérez,Salvador Castillo-Rivera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种在非准静态（non-quasi-static）条件下保持时变谐振子绝热布居结构（adiabatic population structure）的有限时间奥托型量子热力学循环。通过将动力学表述在刘易斯-里森菲尔德不变量（Lewis-Riesenfeld invariant）表象中，而非修改物理哈密顿量或引入辅助反绝热驱动，作者避免了有限速率驱动导致的非绝热布居重分布和残余激发。研究还提出了在捕获离子阱中使用受激拉曼相互作用实现该方案的实验途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统能量表象中，有限速率驱动会诱发非绝热布居重分布，且在哈密顿量回到初值后留下残余激发，这限制了有限时间量子热循环的实现。作者旨在寻找一种不依赖准静态极限、无需辅助反绝热驱动的替代方法，以在有限时间内保留绝热能量结构。

Method: 作者采用刘易斯-里森菲尔德不变量表象重新表述动力学，在不修改物理哈密顿量的前提下避免非绝热布居重分布。针对参数化马蒂厄（Mathieu）协议，分析量子惯性导致的模式空间宽度与瞬态裸能量标度之间的失配。实验方案基于捕获离子泡利阱（Paul trap）中的受激拉曼相互作用，独立控制激光失谐量和光束交叉角。

Result: 研究证明，在不变量表象下，物理陷阱频率非准静态演化的同时可以保持不变量布居结构，从而实现有限时间热循环。该方案区分了绝热运行（adiabatic operation）与准静态驱动（quasi-static driving），提供了无需准静态极限即可保留绝热能量结构的有限时间量子热循环实现路径。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限时间量子热循环提供了新框架，表明绝热能量结构可在外场而非准静态变化时得以保留，对量子热机、制冷机等热力学器件的有限时间实现具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We show a finite-time Otto-like quantum thermodynamic cycle that preserves the adiabatic population structure of a time-dependent harmonic oscillator in the non-quasi-static regime. In the conventional energy representation, finite-rate driving induces non-adiabatic population redistribution and leaves residual excitations after the Hamiltonian has returned to its initial value. We show that this difficulty can be avoided by formulating the dynamics in the Lewis-Riesenfeld invariant representation, without modifying the physical Hamiltonian through auxiliary counterdiabatic driving. For a parametric Mathieu protocol, quantum inertia produces a mismatch between the spatial width of the working mode and its transient dressed energy scale. We propose an experimental implementation of this scheme in a trapped-ion Paul trap using stimulated Raman interactions, with independent control of the laser detuning and beam intersection angle. This provides a finite-time implementation in which the invariant population structure is preserved while the physical trap frequency evolves non-quasi-statically. Our results establish a clear distinction between adiabatic operation and quasi-static driving, providing a route toward finite-time quantum thermal cycles that retain the adiabatic energy structure without requiring the quasi-static limit.

</details>


### [147] [Geometric optimality of entanglement-induced fast qubit reset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26897)
*Davide Rinaldi,Mattia Moroder,Dario Gerace,Giacomo Guarnieri,Steve Campbell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过几何分析（geometric analysis）桥接了两类量子比特重置协议的研究视角：姆潘巴效应（Mpemba effect）启发的加速方案与几何最优化方法。研究表明，纠缠操作可以在多比特寄存器间重新分布局部相干性（coherence），使每个比特的约化态（reduced state）沿测地线（geodesic）路径趋向基态，即使在集体演化非最优时也能实现局域最优的快速重置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速且可靠的量子比特重置（qubit reset）对量子处理器的高效运行至关重要。现有的姆潘巴效应协议虽能加速弛豫，但对整体重置动力学的最优性缺乏深入理解；而几何方法虽能量化最优化程度，却难以直接给出实用的加速方案。本文旨在通过几何分析弥合这两种视角之间的鸿沟。

Method: 本文采用几何分析方法研究纠缠辅助（entanglement-assisted）的量子比特重置过程。核心思路是考察纠缠操作如何在多量子比特寄存器中重新分配局部相干性，并分析各量子比特约化态在密度矩阵空间中的轨迹是否沿测地线趋向基态。研究涵盖不同初始多比特量子态族，以揭示局域最优性与集体演化最优性之间的分离关系。

Result: 研究表明，纠缠操作能够重新分布多比特寄存器中的局部相干性，使每个比特的约化态沿测地线路径趋向基态，从而实现局域最优行为。值得注意的是，这种局域最优可以在集体演化于完整状态空间中并非最优的情况下依然出现。不同初始多比特态族均展示了这一相互作用机制。

Conclusion: 本文为姆潘巴启发的重置加速提供了几何视角的解释框架，并阐明了将二比特协议扩展至更大寄存器时何时能够带来额外的重置优势。这些发现有助于从最优化角度深入理解并指导实际量子比特重置协议的设计。

Abstract: Fast and reliable qubit reset is essential for the efficient operation of quantum processors. Among the proposed strategies, Mpemba-effect-based protocols offer a simple route to accelerated relaxation, but provide limited insight into the optimality of the full reset dynamics. Geometric approaches, by contrast, quantify optimality but do not generally suggest practical acceleration protocols. Here, we bridge these perspectives through a geometric analysis of entanglement-assisted qubit reset. We show that entangling operations can redistribute local coherences across a multi-qubit register, allowing the reduced state of each qubit to follow a geodesic path towards the ground state. Remarkably, this locally optimal behaviour can emerge even when the collective evolution becomes suboptimal in the full state space. We illustrate this interplay for different families of initial multi-qubit states. Our results provide a geometric perspective on Mpemba-inspired reset acceleration and clarify when extending two-qubit protocols to larger registers provides a further advantage in the reset process.

</details>


### [148] [Isotropic Nanoscale Quantum Sensor at Room-Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26942)
*Daniel T. Möller,Baha Sakar,Ekrem T. Güldeste,Dhruv Wadhwani,Olt Gashi,Daniel A. García Vaca,Mokessh K. Ciwan,Simone P. Crozier,Christian Laube,Sirswa K. Shree Ram,Matteo Slaviero,Nabeel Aslam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于金刚石中性氮空位中心（NV0）的各向同性磁力计。通过自旋-电荷动力学形成量子化轴可随外磁场动态对齐的NV0-暗自旋对，结合电荷态选择性荧光读出，实现了室温下微秒相干时间和任意场方向下的纳特斯拉灵敏度，解决了传统色心在对轴磁场中信号损失严重的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于色心的量子传感器在环境条件下具有纳米级分辨率，但其应用受到严重限制——由于量子化轴被锁定于宿主晶格，传统色心（conventional color centers）在离轴磁场中会遭受严重信号损失，亟需一种不依赖方位对准的磁传感方案。

Method: 利用金刚石中中性氮空位中心（NV0）的自旋-电荷动力学，形成NV0-暗自旋对，其量子化轴能够动态地与外磁场方向对齐；读出方式采用NV电荷态选择性荧光（NV charge-state-selective fluorescence）。

Result: 该系统在室温下展现出微秒量级的相干时间和任意磁场方向的纳特斯拉灵敏度。实验上验证了无需对准即可映射陡峭场梯度和单个顺磁微目标的能力，并实现了从随机取向纳米金刚石中的各向同性读出。

Conclusion: 该平台解决了长期存在的取向约束问题，为生命科学和量子材料领域的无限制纳米级磁测量（nanoscale magnetometry）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Color-center based quantum sensors provide nanoscale resolution under ambient conditions, yet their applicability remains limited. Because the quantization axes are locked to the host lattice, conventional color centers suffer severe signal loss in off-axis magnetic fields. To address this, we report an isotropic magnetometer enabled by the neutrally charged nitrogen-vacancy center (NV0) in diamond. Here, spin-to-charge dynamics yield an NV0-dark spin pair whose quantization axis dynamically aligns with the external field. Read out through NV charge-state-selective fluorescence, this system exhibits microsecond room-temperature coherence and nanotesla sensitivity for arbitrary field directions. We demonstrate alignment-free mapping of steep field gradients and single paramagnetic micro-targets, alongside isotropic readout from randomly oriented nanodiamonds. Resolving longstanding orientation constraints, this platform unlocks unrestricted nanoscale magnetometry across life sciences and quantum materials.

</details>


### [149] [A Superconducting Phase Transition Single-Electron Transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27045)
*Gorka Aizpurua-Iraola,Thomas H. Swift,Felix-Ekkehard von Horstig,Domenic Prete,James Kirkman,Grayson M. Noah,Fabio Olivieri,Alberto Gomez-Saiz,M. Fernando Gonzalez-Zalba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将超导相变传感原理应用于半导体自旋量子比特（spin qubits）的电荷探测，展示了一种超导相变射频单电子晶体管（PTSET），其灵敏度在大信号和小信号模式下分别较传统射频单电子晶体管（rfSET）提升一个和两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算需要快速且精确的量子比特态检测方法，而相变传感器虽然已在超导纳米线单光子探测器等量子技术中展现出增强灵敏度，但此前尚未被应用于半导体自旋量子比特。

Method: 通过将传感器电流与集成在射频匹配网络中的低临界电流、高动态电感（kinetic inductance）电感器相耦合，工程化地构建了超导态到正常态的相变，从而增强传感器的响应。

Result: 实验表明，PTSET在大信号和小信号模式下相较于传统rfSET的灵敏度分别提升了1个和2个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了相变传感作为半导体量子计算中超灵敏集成电荷传感器的可行路径，并指出其在低温光子探测（如射电天文）等更广泛领域的应用前景。

Abstract: Quantum computers require fast and accurate methods for qubit state detection. Phase-transition sensors exploit the abrupt change between two physical states of a material to achieve enhanced sensitivity and have enabled advanced detectors for quantum technologies, such as superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors. However, this sensing principle has not yet been applied to semiconductor spin qubits. Here, we demonstrate a superconducting phase-transition radio-frequency single-electron transistor (PTSET), a charge sensor for semiconductor spin qubits whose response is enhanced by a superconducting-to-normal phase transition. The transition is engineered by linking the sensor current to a low-critical-current, high-kinetic-inductance inductor integrated into the radio-frequency matching network. We demonstrate improvements in sensitivity of one and two orders of magnitude over conventional rfSETs in the large- and small-signal regimes, respectively. Our results establish phase-transition sensing as a route towards ultrasensitive, integrated charge sensors for semiconductor quantum computing and point to broader applications, including cryogenic photon detection for radio astronomy.

</details>


### [150] [Fast On-Chip Thermometry with TiN Kinetic Inductance Resonators in 22 nm Process Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27057)
*Johann Drayne,George Ridgard,Lorenzo Peri,Frederic Schlattner,Fabio Olivieri,Mathieu de Kruijf,Isaac Harris,Grayson Noah,James Kirkman,Alberto Gomez-Saiz,M. Fernando Gonzalez-Zalba,Thomas Swift*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于集成在22 nm FDSOI芯片中的氮化钛（TiN）动力学电感谐振器（kinetic inductance resonator）的时间分辨测温平台。该平台可在低至100 mK的温度下实现亚毫开尔文的温度灵敏度，并揭示了准弹道热传播速度（约3.9 mm·μs⁻¹）以及基板限制与Kapitza边界限制两种不同的热弛豫机制，展示了其对低温器件中非平衡温度动力学的快速探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能低温器件的性能受温度的时间和空间依赖性影响显著，而传统测温技术难以同时兼顾高带宽、高灵敏度与片上集成，限制了对器件内部快速热波动的测量能力，因此需要一种新型的集成化时间分辨测温平台。

Method: 利用集成在22 nm FDSOI芯片中的TiN动力学电感谐振器构建测温平台，通过跟踪谐振频率随温度变化的频移来实现温度测量；在此基础上开展时间分辨片上脉冲加热实验，并测量不同距离下的热响应以分析热传播速度与热弛豫行为。

Result: 该平台在低至100 mK温度下达到亚毫开尔文温度灵敏度；脉冲加热实验中观测到与加热器距离相关的起始延迟，对应准弹道热传播速度约3.9±0.1 mm·μs⁻¹。升高温度会增加净热导并缩短各空间间隔下的热弛豫时间，该行为表现为两种不同机制：100 mK下为基板限制机制（冷却速率随加热器距离变化），400 mK下为Kapitza边界限制机制（热弛豫趋于空间均匀）。

Conclusion: 动力学电感测温（kinetic inductance thermometry）能够在低温器件（如量子处理器）中快速探测非平衡温度动力学，为高性能低温器件的热管理研究提供了新的集成化工具。

Abstract: Understanding the temporal and spatial dependence of temperature is critical in high-performance cryogenic devices. Typical thermometry techniques struggle to simultaneously combine high bandwidth, sensitivity and on-chip integration, limiting their ability to measure fast intra-device thermal fluctuations. Here, we demonstrate a time-resolved thermometry platform based on TiN kinetic inductance resonators integrated in a 22 nm FDSOI chip. By tracking temperature-dependent shifts in the resonant frequency, we achieve sub-millikelvin temperature sensitivity down to temperatures of 100 mK. Time-resolved on-chip pulsed heating experiments as a function of distance reveal an onset delay, consistent with a quasi-ballistic heat propagation velocity of 3.9 ${\pm}$ 0.1 mm $μ$s${}^{-1}$. We also show that elevated temperatures increase net thermal conductance, shortening thermal relaxation times across all spatial separations. This behaviour manifests in two distinct regimes: a substrate-limited regime at 100 mK, where cooling rates vary with heater distance, and a Kapitza boundary-limited regime at 400 mK, where thermal relaxation becomes more spatially uniform.These measurements demonstrate kinetic inductance thermometry's ability to rapidly probe non-equilibrium temperature dynamics in cryogenic devices such as quantum processors.

</details>


### [151] [Programmable Cavity Squeezing for Distributed Sensing in a Tweezer Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27132)
*Youssef Trifa,Marco Fattori,Luca Pezzè*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用腔内光镊阵列（tweezer array in a cavity）作为分布式传感（distributed sensing）的理想实验平台，通过工程化空间分离设备间的量子关联，实现跨云（intercloud）纠缠的制备，并展示了该方案在差分拉姆齐干涉（differential Ramsey interferometry）中将相位不确定性降至标准量子极限（standard quantum limit）以下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最先进的原子干涉仪（atom interferometer）在传感应用中被限制于使用无关联设备并行运行，无法利用量子关联提升传感性能。作者希望克服这一局限，通过工程化空间分离设备之间的量子关联来实现分布传感。

Method: 作者提出腔内光镊阵列作为工程化量子态的实验平台，利用局部与腔内介导的云间交换之间的竞争，使云间耦合的符号和空间模式决定压缩模式；同时采用半经典（semiclassical）分析揭示类似于反扭转（counter-twisting）的相空间流。

Result: 对于两个系综（ensemble），正耦合产生均匀集体压缩（uniform collective squeezing），而负耦合产生强交错的非局域压缩（staggered nonlocal squeezing）；其相空间流动质上区别于标准单轴扭转（one-axis twisting）。该交错态可将相位不确定性降至标准量子极限以下，其椭圆估计器（ellipse estimator）逼近Cramèr-Rao界。

Conclusion: 可编程腔相互作用（programmable cavity interactions）为定制的分布式信号提供了一种可扩展的纠缠制备途径，且该方法可推广至更大数量的系综。

Abstract: Field sensing with state-of-the-art atom interferometers is restricted to the use of uncorrelated devices op- erating in parallel. We can overcome this limitation by using distributed sensing protocols where quantum correlations among spatially-separated devices are engineered in the spatial mode carrying the signal. We show that a tweezer array in a cavity offers an ideal testbed to engineer quantum states for distributed sensing, with the possibility to generate entanglement both within and between the clouds. The competition between local and intercloud cavity-mediated exchange allows the sign and spatial pattern of the intercloud couplings to select the squeezed mode. For two ensembles, positive coupling produces uniform collective squeezing, whereas negative coupling generates strong staggered, nonlocal squeezing. A semiclassical analysis reveals a counter-twisting- like phase-space flow, qualitatively distinct from standard one-axis twisting. The analysis and results can be further generalized to a larger number of ensembles. We apply the scheme to differential Ramsey interferometry with common phase noise spanning the full 2 πrange, the resulting staggered states reduce the phase uncertainty below the standard quantum limit, with an ellipse estimator approaching the Cramèr-Rao bound. These results establish programmable cavity interactions as a scalable route to entanglement tailored to distributed signals.

</details>


### [152] [Stochastic transport of a Goldstone mode in a self-organized atomic crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27387)
*Zhanhai Yu,Di Xiang,Xiaotian Zhang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究者在一个光学环形腔内的自组织原子晶体中，直接追踪了戈德斯通模（Goldstone mode）在时间域内的随机输运。通过测量腔输出相位来监测原子及其自生光晶格的个体轨迹，发现扩散常数按 $1/N$ 衰减，表明所有原子作为刚性整体集体运动。通过调节朗之万驱动力和腔介导的阻尼，归一化扩散常数塌缩到单一普适曲线上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性自发破缺产生的无质量戈德斯通模通常通过激发谱、模式软化或集体振荡来识别，但其在固有涨落和耗散下的时间域输运行为此前几乎未被探索。

Method: 实验采用光学环形腔内的自组织原子晶体，环形腔将序参量相位映射到涌现晶体的实空间位置。无需外部扰动，基础光子散射反冲驱动集体输运，腔耗散提供摩擦。通过测量腔输出相位，监测原子的个体轨迹及其自生光学晶格，并从轨迹中提取扩散常数。

Result: 发现扩散常数随原子数 $N$ 按 $1/N$ 变化，表明所有原子作为刚性整体集体运动而非独立运动；通过调节朗之万驱动力和腔介导阻尼，归一化扩散常数塌缩到单一普适曲线上。

Conclusion: 该工作将连续对称性破缺的研究从激发频率测量拓展到输运的实时追踪，为在驱动耗散量子物质中研究非平衡集体输运、声子动力学和缺陷形成开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Spontaneous breaking of a continuous symmetry produces a massless Goldstone mode that can evolve across a degenerate manifold at zero energy cost. Goldstone modes have been identified primarily through excitation spectra, mode softening or collective oscillations. However, their time-domain transport under intrinsic fluctuations and dissipation has remained largely unexplored. Here we directly track the stochastic transport of a Goldstone mode in a self-organized atomic crystal inside an optical ring cavity. The ring cavity maps the order-parameter phase onto the real-space position of the emergent crystal. Without any external perturbation, fundamental photon-scattering recoil drives the collective transport, while cavity dissipation generates friction. We monitor individual trajectories of the atoms and their self-generated optical lattice by measuring the cavity output phase. We find that the diffusion constant decreases as $1/N$, indicating that all atoms move collectively as a rigid object rather than independently. By tuning the Langevin driving force and cavity-mediated damping, we show that the normalized diffusion constant collapses onto a single universal curve. This work extends the study of continuous symmetry breaking from excitation-frequency measurements to real-time tracking of transport, and opens routes for studying non-equilibrium collective transport, phonon dynamics, and defect formation in driven-dissipative quantum matter.

</details>


### [153] [Detuning- and Stark-robust Rydberg gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27426)
*Elie Bataille,Gyohei Nomura,Manuel Endres*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的Rydberg纠缠门协议，通过让两个量子比特态都参与门操作，实现对光移（light-shift）诱导的失谐误差的全鲁棒或部分鲁棒性，克服了传统单态耦合方案中无法设计鲁棒门的限制，并发现该协议已在当前最先进实验中具备优势条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碱金属原子中由双光子跃迁驱动的Rydberg纠缠门受光移诱导误差的不利标度影响，而传统Rydberg门协议（只有其中一个量子比特态与Rydberg态耦合）被证明无法实现对这类失谐误差的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者在更一般的框架中让两个量子比特态都参与门操作，设计了两种门构型：一种直接完全消除误差，另一种将误差转化为可在本地纠正的单量子比特误差。论文还映射了多个参数区间（光移强度、强度不均匀性、Rydberg衰变率）以比较这些协议与时间最优Rydberg门的性能。

Result: 该协议的性能优势区间已覆盖当前最先进实验的条件范围。此外，论文证明了Rydberg"飞掠"（fly-by）纠缠门的存在，这是新兴中性原子量子计算架构中的一个重要基本操作。

Conclusion: 通过引入双量子比特态参与的更一般框架，可以突破传统Rydberg门在失谐误差鲁棒性上的理论限制，为中性原子量子计算提供更优的纠缠门方案和新的架构原语。

Abstract: Rydberg entangling gates driven by a two-photon transition in alkali atoms suffer from an adverse scaling of light-shift-induced errors. Robustness to such detuning errors is known to be impossible to achieve in the design of conventional Rydberg gate protocols, where only one of the qubit states is coupled to the Rydberg state. Here, we show that in a more general framework, in which both qubit states take part in the gate, full or partial robustness to these errors can be realized. We present two gate constructions, which either cancel the errors outright or convert them into single-qubit errors that can be corrected locally. We map the regimes -- in terms of light-shift strength, intensity inhomogeneity, and Rydberg decay rate -- in which these protocols outperform the widely used time-optimal Rydberg gate, and find that they already include the conditions of state-of-the-art experiments. Finally, we show the existence of a Rydberg `fly-by' entangling gate, an important primitive for an emerging class of neutral-atom quantum computing architectures.

</details>

### [154] [Influence of twist direction and large deformation on soft material torsional contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26616)
*Yucai Hu,Pengfei Li,Michele Ciavarella,Yue Wu,Yang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过正向-反向扭转实验结合调节固化剂-基料比例来改变PDMS球体的软度和变形程度，发现大变形会增强剪切诱导的接触面积减小效应，最终抑制初始面积增加，使足够软的PDMS在加载过程中呈现单调面积减小；实验还观察到卸载时的不可逆路径和花瓣状边缘等表面失稳现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪切诱导的接触面积减小在软接触中广泛存在，但最近的扭转实验揭示了更复杂的非单调演化——接触面积随扭转角先增加后减小；初始面积增加的机制以及大变形在整体面积演化中的作用仍不清楚，需要实验研究来阐明。

Method: 对软聚二甲基硅氧烷（PDMS）球体进行正向-反向扭转接触实验，通过系统改变固化剂-基料比例来调节材料软度和变形水平，并进行加载-卸载测试以研究扭转界面的响应。

Result: 研究发现扭转界面强烈不可逆：卸载时接触面积经历减小-增加-减小的路径而非沿加载分支返回，且出现可重复的花瓣状边缘，表明存在皱纹诱导的表面失稳。降低混合比例后，更大的变形强化了面积减小的贡献并最终抑制初始面积增加，使足够软的PDMS呈现单调面积减小；较软PDMS还表现出更低的剪切强度、更弱的扭矩振荡和更好的可重复性。

Conclusion: 实验结果为大变形可驱动剪切诱导的接触面积减小提供了实验证据，但初始面积增加的起源仍未解决；这些发现缩小了导致初始面积增加的潜在机制（如摩擦带电 triboelectrification）的范围，并为软界面的摩擦接触模型提供了严格的基准。

Abstract: Shear-induced contact area reduction is widely observed in soft contacts, yet recent torsional experiments have revealed a more complex non-monotonic evolution in which the contact area first increases and then decreases with twist angle. The mechanism responsible for this initial area increase and the role of large deformation in the overall area evolution remain unclear. In this study, we experimentally investigate the torsional contact response of soft Polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) spheres by combining forward-backward twist tests with a systematic variation of the curing-agent-to-base ratio to tune material softness and deformation level. The loading-unloading tests show that the torsional interface is strongly irreversible: during unloading, the contact area follows a decrease-increase-decrease path rather than retracing the loading branch, and repeatable petal-like edges appear, indicating a wrinkle-induced surface instability. By decreasing the mixing ratio, we find that larger deformation strengthens the area-reduction contribution and eventually suppresses the initial area increase, leading to a monotonic area decrease during loading for sufficiently soft PDMS. Softer PDMS also exhibits lower shear strength, weaker torque oscillations, and improved repeatability. The results provide experimental evidence that large deformation can drive shear-induced contact area reduction, while the origin of the initial area increase remains unresolved. These findings narrow the possible mechanisms (e.g., triboelectrification) responsible for the initial area increase and provide a stringent benchmark for frictional contact models of soft interfaces.

</details>


### [155] [Asymmetric Ions in Solution are Similar to Active Brownian Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27245)
*Setare Mostajabi Sarhangi,Dmitry V. Matyushov*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟发现，当电解质离子的电荷偏离离子几何中心的距离d超过临界值d*时，离子体坐标系中的机械平衡无法建立，体系出现从零到非零体坐标系力的尖锐交叉，源自水分子无法通过局部密度增强补偿静电拉力而产生挫折（frustration）。虽然热平衡和实验室坐标系下的净力仍然保持为零，但电荷位移跨越阈值后扩散常数显著下降（约400倍），且不同途径计算的扩散常数不再等价，均方位移得到的扩散常数远高于速度相关函数和力相关函数给出的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该项研究旨在探究电荷分布不对称的电解质离子在水溶液中的动力学行为，特别是电荷偏离几何中心时对机械平衡和输运性质（如扩散常数）的影响。该问题与活性物质（active matter）中布朗粒子自推进现象存在类比，具有基础物理意义。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟方法，对电荷偏移距离为d的电解质离子在溶液中进行模拟。通过改变电荷位移参数，系统监测体坐标系（body frame）中的力、实验室坐标系中的力以及不同途径计算的扩散常数（基于均方位移、速度相关函数和力相关函数）随电荷位移的变化。

Result: 研究发现存在一个临界电荷偏移距离d*，超过该阈值时离子体坐标系中出现非零力，呈尖锐交叉转变，源于水分子无法以局部密度增强来补偿静电拉力。离子在体坐标系中受到类似活性布朗粒子自推进的净力。热平衡和实验室坐标系净力仍保持为零，但扩散常数下降约400倍，且均方位移法得到的扩散常数显著高于速度相关函数和力相关函数法。

Conclusion: 电荷不对称溶剂化离子在超过临界偏移距离后，其动力学行为表现出类似活性物质自推进的特征，且不同统计途径给出的输运系数（transport coefficient）不再等价。这一发现挑战了对称电解质体系中不同扩散常数计算途径一致性的传统假设，对理解非对称电荷体系中的离子输运具有重要意义。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics simulations of electrolyte ions with charge shifted by the distance $d$ from the ion geometrical center have shown that mechanical equilibrium in the ion's body frame is not established beyond a critical shift distance $d^*$. A sharp crossover to a non-zero body-frame force occurs due to frustration of water molecules failing to compensate the electrostatic pull with a local density augmentation. A solution ion with asymmetric charge experiences a net body-frame force similar to self-propelled motion of active-matter Brownian particles. Thermal equilibrium and zero laboratory-frame force are still maintained, but the diffusion constant drops significantly ($\sim 400$ times) when the charge displacement crosses the threshold value $d^*$. Alternative routes to the diffusion constant become inequivalent and the diffusion constant from the mean-squared displacement is much higher than those from velocity and force correlation functions.

</details>


### [156] [Nonparametric multiscale modeling of boundary lubrication: hexadecane in highly pressurized gold asperity contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27352)
*Hannes Holey,Michael Moseler,Peter Gumbsch,Lars Pastewka*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯过程（Gaussian process, GP）回归的非参数多尺度框架，用于模拟金表面间受限十六烷的边界润滑（boundary lubrication），在高达1 GPa压力和1.4 nm间隙尺度下，无需固定形式本构方程即可将分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟数据无缝传递至连续介质薄膜求解器，重现了Codrignani等人的原子级基准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界润滑由滑动界面的分子过程主导，经典连续介质描述无法捕捉这些过程。虽然分子动力学模拟能以原子级细节解析这些过程，但当间隙接近分子尺度时，通过半经验本构定律将模拟输出整合到工程尺度模型中变得越来越困难，亟需一种绕过固定形式本构方程的替代方法。

Method: 采用基于高斯过程回归的非参数多尺度框架，以非平衡分子动力学（nonequilibrium MD）模拟的受限流体数据训练GP替代模型（surrogate model），并将GP输出的应力预测直接提供给连续介质薄膜求解器，从而规避固定形式本构定律的需求。

Result: 该框架能够自然捕捉密度分层（density layering）、黏度变化以及在高压力和小子间隙下主导摩擦响应的强非线性壁面滑移（wall slip）等分子现象，并与Codrignani等人的原子级基准数据一致。

Conclusion: 非参数替代模型为边界润滑的预测性连续介质建模提供了一条灵活且物理透明的途径，展示了多尺度耦合在分子尺度受限流动中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Boundary lubrication is governed by molecular processes at the sliding interface that are inaccessible to classical continuum descriptions. While molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can resolve these processes in atomistic detail, incorporating their output into engineering-scale models through semi-empirical constitutive laws becomes increasingly difficult as confinement approaches the molecular scale. Here, we apply a nonparametric multiscale framework based on Gaussian process (GP) regression to model boundary lubrication of hexadecane confined between gold surfaces under pressures up to 1 GPa and gap heights down to 1.4 nm. The GP surrogates are trained on nonequilibrium MD simulations of the confined fluid and directly provide stress predictions to a continuum thin-film solver, circumventing the need for fixed-form constitutive laws. The framework naturally captures molecular phenomena such as density layering, viscosity changes, and the strongly nonlinear wall slip that dominates the frictional response at high pressures and small gap heights. Our results reproduce the atomistic benchmark data of Codrignani et al., demonstrating that nonparametric surrogate models offer a flexible and physically transparent route toward predictive continuum modeling of boundary lubrication.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Deuterium Production in an Effective Field Theory Constructed from On-Shell Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23699)
*Tim M. P. Tait*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论（effective field theory）框架下计算了氘生成反应 n+p→d+γ，其中自由度仅为核态本身。利用散射矩阵（S-matrix）因子化道上的壳上三点顶点组装振幅，并由与对称性相容的接触项（contact term）补全。联合贝叶斯拟合热俘获测量和 SLEGS 光致裂解数据，将热核反应率确定至 0.22–0.24%（含系统学），理论上限不确定性为 0.25–0.27%。传播到大爆炸核合成（BBN）网络中，该反应率将预测的原始氘丰度偏移 −0.06%，并削减该反应对 D/H 不确定性的贡献，使其在实际目的上退出原始 D/H 误差预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氘的生产反应 n+p→d+γ 对原始氘丰度预测和大爆炸核合成至关重要，但需要精确的理论计算以支持高精度观测约束。传统势模型方法依赖模型依赖的短程物理，本文旨在利用有效场论提供一个系统可截断、误差可控的第一性框架。

Method: 采用自由度仅为核态本身的有效场论，振幅由壳上三点顶点跨因子化道黏合，并用与对称性一致的接触项补全。氘核通过 d-n-p 顶点进入，以实验测得的渐近归一化系数（asymptotic normalization coefficient）标定。核子对重散射用色散方法（dispersive resummation）求和，仅留下两个短距离常数——同位旋矢量磁接触项和电偶极接触项。对热俘获测量和 SLEGS 光致裂解数据做联合贝叶斯拟合，并纳入对 SLEGS 数据和 P 波重散射处理的系统学。

Result: 两个短距离常数均被拟合为自然大小；热核反应率在核合成窗口内确定为 0.22–0.24% 精度（含系统学）。截断展开单独界定的不确定性为 0.12%，其中下一阶接触项（与两个拟合常数简并）贡献 0.03%，总理论不确定性为 0.25–0.27%。传播至 BBN 网络后，预测的原始氘丰度偏移 −0.06%，且该反应对 D/H 不确定性的贡献从 0.089% 降至 0.050%。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个系统可截断的有效场论框架来精确计算氘生成反应率，其理论不确定性低于现有实验系统学。在原始 D/H 误差预算中，该反应源的不确定性贡献已降至实际可忽略水平，从而将 D/H 预算的其他部分（如观测和宇宙学参数）置于更突出的地位。

Abstract: We compute the deuterium-production reaction $n+p \to d+γ$ in an effective field theory whose degrees of freedom are the nuclear states themselves: the amplitude is assembled from on-shell three-point vertices, glued across its factorization channels, and completed by the contact terms consistent with the symmetries. The deuteron enters through the $d$-$n$-$p$ vertex, normalized to the measured asymptotic normalization coefficient. Rescattering of the nucleon pair is resummed dispersively, leaving two short-distance constants, an isovector magnetic and an electric dipole contact interaction. A joint Bayesian fit to the thermal capture measurements and the SLEGS photodisintegration data finds both of natural size and determines the thermonuclear rate to 0.22-0.24% across the nucleosynthesis window, including systematics spanning the defensible treatments of the SLEGS data and of the $P$-wave rescattering. Truncating the expansion is bounded separately at 0.12%, of which the next order of contact terms -- degenerate with the two fitted constants -- supplies 0.03%, for a total theory uncertainty of 0.25-0.27%. Propagated through a BBN network, the rate shifts the predicted primordial deuterium by -0.06% and cuts this reaction's contribution to the D/H uncertainty from 0.089% to 0.050%, retiring it from the primordial D/H error budget for practical purposes.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [The Saito determinant for extended affine Weyl discriminant strata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26817)
*Andrea Brini,Karoline van Gemst*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩展仿射Weyl群（extended affine Weyl group）反射表示在判别式（discriminant）任意分层（stratum）上的商空间中所诱导的Saito度量（Saito metric）之形式，对所有分层和Dynkin类型计算了Saito行列式（Saito determinant），并证明其可被与超平面排列（hyperplane arrangement）相关的限制根（restricted roots）的q-类比之乘积整除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统性地推广Weyl分母公式（Weyl denominator formula）中的因式分解到更高余维数的分层，并为Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan关于Coxeter群的结果提供q-类比精细化（q-analogue refinements）。

Method: 通过将扩展仿射Weyl群反射表示的商空间上的Saito度量限制到判别式的任意分层上，对所有分层和Dynkin类型显式计算Saito行列式的因式分解，并建立其与限制根q-类比乘积的整除关系。

Result: 对所有分层与Dynkin类型完成了Saito行列式的计算，并证明其可被与超平面排列相关的限制根q-类比之乘积整除，同时将Weyl分母公式的因式分解推广到任意余维数分层，并给出了Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan结果的q-类比精细化。

Conclusion: 研究结果为扩展仿射Weyl群的判别式分层上Saito度量的代数结构提供了统一刻画，同时衔接了Weyl分母公式的经典因式分解与针对Coxeter群已有的Saito行列式结论。

Abstract: We investigate the form of the Saito metric on quotients of the reflection representation of an extended affine Weyl group, restricted to an arbitrary stratum of its discriminant. We compute the Saito determinant for all strata and Dynkin types and show that it is divisible by a product of q-analogues of restricted roots for the associated hyperplane arrangement. Our results simultaneously generalise the factorisation in the Weyl denominator formula to higher codimension strata, and provide q-analogue refinements of results of Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan for Coxeter groups.

</details>

### [159] [Moderate Deviations for the Largest Eigenvalue of a Randomly Deformed Gaussian Unitary Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26745)
*Shaochen Wang,Guangyu Yang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Johansson（2007）引入的随机变形高斯酉系综（randomly deformed Gaussian unitary ensemble）中最大特征值的适中偏差（moderate deviations）。在固定耦合区间下，重标度后的最大特征值收敛于Tracy-Widom分布与源自谱边随机位移的高斯分布的卷积。作者推导了在Airy尺度增长区间上左右尾部的尖锐对数渐近式，并给出了相应的相图，揭示了左右尾具有不同的过渡尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机变形GUE模型是理解谱边随机扰动如何影响最大特征值统计行为的重要框架。由于Tracy-Widom分布与高斯位移的卷积产生非平凡的尾部行为，现有的极限定分布已无法刻画适中偏差尺度的精细渐近行为，因此需要通过推导尖锐的指数率和相图来系统描述这一过程。

Method: 作者首先建立了一个在对数窗口上对典型对角构型一致的迹范数（trace-norm）Airy近似；随后利用条件卷积（conditional convolution）论证，将所得尾部估计与谱边位移的高斯适中偏差相结合，从而得到左右尾部在增长Airy尺度上的对数渐近式与相图。

Result: 左右尾部具有不同的过渡尺度（transition scales）；在临界点（criticality）处，率函数（rate functions）是Tracy-Widom与高斯率函数的非平凡下确界卷积（infimal convolutions）。所得渐近式在对典型对角构型的对数窗口上是均匀成立的。

Conclusion: 作者完整刻画了随机变形GUE最大特征值在适中偏差尺度下的尾部指数结构，其包含Tracy-Widom与高斯适中偏差两个来源的竞争与卷积，为随机谱摄动问题中的大偏差理论提供了新的精确结果。

Abstract: We study moderate deviations for the largest eigenvalue of the randomly deformed Gaussian unitary ensemble introduced by Johansson (Probab. Theory Relat. Fields, {\bf 138}: 75--112, 2007). In the fixed-coupling regime, the rescaled largest eigenvalue converges to the convolution of the Tracy--Widom law and a Gaussian law arising from the random displacement of the spectral edge. We derive sharp logarithmic asymptotics for the right and left tails on growing Airy scales and obtain the corresponding phase diagram. The two tails have different transition scales. At criticality, the rate functions are nontrivial infimal convolutions of the Tracy--Widom and Gaussian rate functions. We establish a trace-norm Airy approximation that is uniform over typical diagonal configurations on a logarithmic window. A conditional convolution argument then combines the resulting tail estimates with the Gaussian moderate deviations of the edge displacement.

</details>


### [160] [Universality and sharp thresholds for ellipsoid fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27372)
*Frederic Koehler,Youngtak Sohn*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文建立了用椭圆体（ellipsoid）拟合随机向量的锐利相变（sharp phase transition）理论。随机向量的独立次高斯坐标均值为零、方差为一且具有共同四阶矩，向量数量与维度平方成正比。文章确定了显式的可满足性阈值，并解决了椭圆体拟合猜想，其中高斯数据情形下阈值为1/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解随机数据在高维空间中能否被一个椭圆体精确拟合的临界条件，并解决此前悬而未决的椭圆体拟合猜想（ellipsoid fitting conjecture），从而建立严格的数学判定标准。

Method: 方法涉及对随机向量坐标的次高斯分布假设建模，通过四阶矩刻画分布式，分析正定椭圆体存在性随样本数量与维度比值的阈值变化，并在不可满足区间内进一步推导最优平方拟合误差。

Result: 结果表明存在一个显式可满足性阈值：低于阈值时几乎必然存在正定椭圆体穿过所有数据点，高于阈值时不存在任何半正定拟合。最优平方拟合误差在整个不可满足区域也被确定。阈值仅依赖坐标分布的共同四阶矩，展现四阶矩普适性现象；标准高斯数据下阈值为1/4。

Conclusion: 该结果刻画了椭圆体拟合问题的完整相变行为，揭示四阶矩普适性（fourth moment universality），并最终解决了椭圆体拟合猜想。

Abstract: We establish a sharp phase transition for fitting random vectors by an ellipsoid. The random vectors have independent subgaussian coordinates with mean zero, variance one, and a common fourth moment, and the number of vectors is proportional to the square of the dimension. We identify an explicit satisfiability threshold such that, with high probability, a positive definite ellipsoid passes through every data point below the threshold, whereas no positive semidefinite fit exists above it. We also determine the optimal squared fitting error throughout the unsatisfiable regime. In particular, the threshold depends on the coordinate distributions only through their common fourth moment, revealing a fourth moment universality phenomenon. For standard Gaussian data the threshold is $1/4$, resolving the ellipsoid fitting conjecture.

</details>

### [161] [Exact pair density wave in topological moire flat bands and universal superfluid stiffness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26245)
*Zhengzhi Wu,Ming-rui Li,Hong Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在扭曲双层棋盘晶格（twisted bilayer checkerboard, TBCB）模型中构造了一个具有拓扑平带的相互作用模型，严格证明了可通过量子几何嵌套（quantum geometric nesting, QGN）诱导出精确的配对密度波（pair-density wave, PDW）基态，并推导出任何非简并QGN超导体超流体刚度的普适关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对密度波（PDW）是超越BCS范式的非传统超导态，但受各种竞争序的影响，在微观晶格模型中对其进行明确识别通常具有挑战性；尤其在与拓扑带相互作用的模型中，PDW的精确实现尚未见报道。

Method: 在具有拓扑平带的扭曲双层棋盘晶格（TBCB）上构造相互作用模型，利用其动量空间中的非近对称性（nonsymmorphic symmetry），通过量子几何嵌套机制严格推导出精确的PDW基态。

Result: 严格证明了TBCB模型中可存在精确的PDW基态；进一步证明了任何非简并QGN超导体（包括PDW和均匀超导体）的超流体刚度满足普适关系 D_s = 2N_flat ν(1−ν)/m_pair，其中N_flat为平带数、ν为平带填充率、m_pair为两粒子有效质量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了PDW序、量子几何（quantum geometry）与带拓扑（band topology）之间丰富的相互作用与关联。

Abstract: Pair-density waves (PDWs) are unconventional superconducting states beyond the BCS paradigm, but identifying them unambiguously in microscopic lattice models is generally challenging due to various competing orders. In particular, an exact realization of PDW in interacting models with topological bands remains elusive. Here we construct an interacting model on a twisted bilayer checkerboard lattice (TBCB) featuring topological flat bands, and rigorously show that an exact PDW ground state can be induced by quantum geometric nesting (QGN), owing to its momentum-space nonsymmorphic symmetry. We further prove that the superfluid stiffness of any nondegenerate QGN superconductor (including the PDW studied here and uniform superconductor) obeys the universal relation $D_s=2N_{\rm flat}ν(1-ν)m_{\rm pair}^{-1}$, where $N_{\rm flat}$, $ν$, and $m_{\rm pair}$ are the number of flat bands, flat band filling, and the two-particle effective mass, respectively. Our results reveal a rich interplay among PDW order, quantum geometry, and band topology.

</details>


### [162] [Supercurrent detection and manipulation of topological phase transitions in Shiba-Majorana hybrid systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26265)
*Debika Debnath,Ioannis Ioannidis,Paramita Dutta,Mircea Trif,Thore Posske*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过超电流（supercurrent）穿过低能模式来探测拓扑非平凡吸附原子系统中基态宇称（ground state parity）变化的非侵入式方案，并通过临界电流中的特征不连续性来识别零能能级交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes）的非阿贝尔统计特性催生了大量在拓扑超导体中探测和操控它们的方案，而基于超导体上沉积磁性吸附原子的实现因其精确原子操控和可控制无序性而备受关注。需要一种非侵入性的方法来实现对吸附原子系统基态宇称的读取与控制。

Method: 提出通过让超电流通过拓扑非平凡吸附原子系统的低能模式来检测基态宇称变化，利用零能能级交叉（zero-energy level crossings）驱动的临界电流特征不连续性作为探测信号。将该方案应用于由单个控制磁性吸附原子承载Yu-Shiba-Rusinov态来中介马约拉纳耦合的装置，并在有限温度和不同隧穿区域下测试了结果的鲁棒性。

Result: 揭示了临界电流中由零能能级交叉驱动的特征不连续性，并验证了该方案在有限温度和不同隧穿区域下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为Shiba-马约拉纳混合系统中马约拉纳态基态宇称的读取和控制提供了一种非侵入式方法。

Abstract: The non-Abelian statistics of Majorana zero modes has inspired numerous proposals for their detection and manipulation in topological superconductors. Implementations based on magnetic adatoms deposited on superconductors draw particular attention due to their capabilities for precise atomic manipulation and the control over disorder. Here, we propose a scheme for detecting changes in the ground state parity of a topologically non-trivial adatom system by passing supercurrent through their low-energy modes. We unravel characteristic discontinuities in the critical current driven by zero-energy level crossings. We apply these findings to a setup where the Majorana coupling is mediated by a single control magnetic adatom hosting a Yu-Shiba-Rusinov state, and test the robustness of our results against finite temperatures and different tunneling regimes. Our findings introduce a non-invasive approach for reading out and controlling the ground state parity of Majorana states in Shiba-Majorana hybrid systems.

</details>


### [163] [Strain-driven orbital-selective reconstruction and bicollinear-to-stripe evolution in FeTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26652)
*Zhenfeng Ouyang,Yin Chen,Yi-Heng Tian,Jia-Ming Wang,Rong-Qiang He,Kai Liu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文结合密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）与海森堡模型分析，揭示了FeTe在应变作用下从双共线（bicollinear）到条纹（stripe）反铁磁序的应变驱动演化轨迹，并展示了该过程中关联电子结构与磁性态的逐轨道（orbital-selective）重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeTe作为铁基超导体中具有代表性的母体材料，是探索轨道选择性关联、磁性与非常规超导电性之间相互作用的重要平台。然而，在应变条件下FeTe的关联电子结构与磁性尚未形成统一图景，亟需系统性阐明。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory）计算，并结合海森堡（Heisenberg）模型分析，对FeTe在不同应变条件下的电子结构、磁性序及局域矩行为进行系统性研究。

Result: 发现FeTe在应变驱动下经历从双共线到条纹反铁磁序的转变，中间经由竞争性的交错n聚体（staggered n-mer）反铁磁态。中等应变下量子准粒子（quasiparticle）相干性增强而局域磁矩被抑制；更强应变则使FeTe进入非相干关联区，伴随稳健的局域磁矩及以Fe-3d_z²轨道为主导的低能态。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了FeTe中存在一条穿越不同磁性与关联电子态的应变驱动轨迹，为理解该材料体系中轨道选择性关联、磁性乃至非常规超导电性的交织关系提供了统一的物理图景。

Abstract: FeTe, as a representative parent material among iron-based superconductors, provides an ideal platform for exploring the interplay among orbital-selective correlations, magnetism, and unconventional superconductivity. However, a unified picture of the correlated electronic structure and magnetism of FeTe under strain remains to be fully clarified. Here, combining density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory and Heisenberg model analysis, we uncover an orbital-selective reconstruction of the correlated electronic structure and reveal a strain-driven trajectory from bicollinear to stripe antiferromagnetism (AFM) via an intermediate competing staggered $n$-mer AFM regime in FeTe. Moderate strain gives rise to a regime where more coherent quasiparticles coexist with suppressed local moments. Further strain drives FeTe into an incoherent correlated regime with robust local moments and Fe-$3d_{z^2}$-dominated low-energy states. These results establish a strain-driven trajectory across distinct magnetic and correlated electronic states in FeTe.

</details>


### [164] [Stabilization of Interband Phase Solitons in Two-Band Noncentrosymmetric Superconducting Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26782)
*Yuriy Yerin,Boris Malomed,Stefan-Ludwig Drechsler,F. Sebastian Bergeret*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究两带超导体（two-band superconductors）中带间相位孤子（interband phase soliton）的稳定性，发现当反转对称性被足够强地破缺时，磁电耦合项会产生与绕数（winding number）奇相关的自由能偏置，使有限绕数的相位孤子从亚稳态转变为真正基态，并可通过外加磁场设定其手性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有反转对称性的两带超导环中，带间相位孤子仅是亚稳态（metastable state），与均匀基态被屏蔽电流能量所隔开。作者旨在探究能否通过破缺反转对称性使孤子转变为真正的基态，从而在带间相对相位域中实现磁电模拟的小-帕克斯（Little–Parks）效应。

Method: 在反转对称性（inversion symmetry）破缺的两带超导环中，作者引入磁电耦合（magneto-electric coupling）作为自由能的贡献项。该耦合项随绕数取奇对称形式，在超过某一阈值时成为相关的自由能项。通过比较偏置能与原孤子亚稳态的能量差，分析在平衡态电流-磁通测量中孤子分支的场奇性。

Result: 当反转对称性被足够强地破缺时，磁电耦合产生的偏置项可以超过使孤子原本亚稳态化的能量代价，使具有有限绕数的相位孤子成为真正的基态，其手性（chirality）由外加磁场决定。在平衡态电流-磁通测量中，这一效应表现为场奇（field-odd）孤子分支取代介观环中原本的亚稳态分支。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了通过破缺反转对称性实现相位孤子基态化的机制，在带间相对相位域中构造了一个磁电模拟的Little–Parks通量涡旋分支物理类比，为多组分超导体中介观环中的新奇拓扑态研究提供了理论路线。

Abstract: Two-band superconductors maintain a relative interband phase which can carry winding soliton excitations in a superconducting ring, supported by independent winding numbers in the two bands. In rings of superconductors obeying the inversion symmetry the interband phase solitons are metastable states, separated from the uniform ground state by the energy of screening currents. In this work we find that, breaking the inversion symmetry strongly enough, one can make the soliton a true ground state. In that case, a magneto-electric coupling, absent in centrosymmetric materials, contributes critically above a certain threshold, a relevant, free-energy term, odd with respect to the winding number, which biases the energy balance in favor of a particular winding sign. Once the bias outweighs the energy cost that originally made the soliton metastable, a phase soliton with a finite winding number becomes the ground state, with chirality set by the applied field. In current--flux measurements performed in equilibrium states, the effect is demonstrated by field-odd soliton branches, that replace the metastable ones existing in mesoscopic rings, built by two-component superconductors, realizing a magneto-electric analog of the Little--Parks fluxoid-branch physics in the interband relative-phase sector.

</details>


### [165] [High-Temperature Superconductivity of the Fe-Se-H compound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27349)
*S. I. Bondarenko,A. A. Prokhorov,N. N. Galtsov,V. P. Timofeev,V. P. Koverya,I. S. Bondarenko,A. V. Krevsun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究利用EPR谱仪测量了Fe-Se-H粉末样品在3.6-25 K及295 K温度下、磁场至6000 Oe范围内的微波功率吸收依赖性，测量结果证实该化合物在常压下具有超导电性（superconductivity），包括在室温（常温）条件下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在验证通过热扩散将氢掺入FeSe单晶后得到的Fe-Se-H化合物是否具备超导电性，特别是检验其在常压下、包括室温条件下是否仍能保持超导态。

Method: 研究者采用电子顺磁共振（EPR）谱仪，测量粉末状Fe-Se-H样品在3.6-25 K及295 K温度下、磁场强度最高达6000 Oe范围内的微波功率吸收对磁场的依赖关系。样品由氢热扩散进入临界温度为8 K的FeSe单晶制备获得。

Result: 微波功率吸收的依赖关系曲线形状及测量结果的分析共同证实，Fe-Se-H化合物在常压下于3.6-25 K温度范围及295 K（即室温）下均表现出超导电性。

Conclusion: Fe-Se-H化合物在常压下不仅能在低温段保持超导态，而且在室温下也呈现超导迹象，表明氢掺杂FeSe体系可能具有非常规的高临界温度超导性质。

Abstract: Using an EPR spectrometer, the dependences of microwave power absorption on magnetic field up to 6000 Oe were measured at temperatures of 3.6-25 K and 295 K in a powdered compound Fe-Se-H, obtained by thermal diffusion of hydrogen into a FeSe single crystal with a critical temperature of 8 K. The shape of the dependences and the analysis of the measurement results confirm the superconductivity of the Fe-Se-H compound at normal pressure, both at temperatures of 3.6-25 K and at 295 K, i.e., at room temperature.

</details>
