### [166] [Quantized Transport through a Supermoiré Chern Mosaic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26252)
*Li-Qiao Xia,Aviram Uri,Zachary W. Gomez,Molly P. Andersen,Julian May-Mann,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Trithep Devakul,Yves H. Kwan,Pablo Jarillo-Herrero,Aaron Sharpe*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 魔角螺旋三层石墨烯（magic-angle helical trilayer graphene）在零场下弛豫为携带相反谷分辨陈数（valley-resolved Chern number）的畴域镶嵌结构，其边界承载无间隙导电模式网络。然而实验在电荷中性点附近观测到陈数C=-6的场致陈数间隙（Chern gap），霍尔电阻在4.6 K下以2%精度量子化为-h/6e²。Hofstadter与轨道塞曼（orbital Zeeman）计算表明，中等磁场驱动谷选择性拓扑相变，使各自旋谷味（spin-valley flavor）的占据态总陈数在相邻畴域间一致，畴壁模式因此可被间隙化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角螺旋三层石墨烯的畴域边界网络携带无间隙导电模式，其电荷输运对模式间的连接和散射极为敏感，预期难以实现良好量子化的输运。作者旨在探究该系统中是否存在可观测的量子化输运行为，并揭示其背后的物理机制。

Method: 实验上在电荷中性点附近测量霍尔电阻，观测场致陈数间隙及其量子化平台。理论上分别采用Hofstadter模型和轨道塞曼（orbital Zeeman）计算，分析中等磁场下不同自旋谷味风味中占据态的总陈数如何随磁场演化，并解释畴壁模式在大磁场下如何被间隙化。

Result: 在电荷中性点附近观测到场致陈数间隙，陈数C=-6，霍尔电阻在4.6 K下以2%精度量子化为-h/6e²。Hofstadter与轨道塞曼计算显示，中等磁场驱动谷选择性拓扑相变，使相邻畴域中每个自旋谷味风味的占据态总陈数变得相同，从而畴壁模式可以被间隙化。尽管两种畴域类型间中心价带陈数仍存在差异，但观测到的量子化输运表明系统存在全局间隙（global gap）。

Conclusion: 该研究推翻了螺旋三层石墨烯因畴域网络预期难以实现量子化输运的假设，表明即使在中心价带陈数不同的复杂多畴系统中，磁场诱导的谷选择性拓扑相变仍可使畴壁模式间隙化，从而实现高度量子化的霍尔输运。这一发现为多畴拓扑系统的量子化输运提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Magic-angle helical trilayer graphene---three graphene layers sequentially twisted in the same direction by $\sim1.8^\circ$---relaxes into a mosaic of domains that, at zero field, carry opposite valley-resolved Chern numbers, with boundaries hosting a network of gapless conducting modes. Charge transport through this network depends sensitively on how the modes connect and scatter, making well-quantized transport unlikely. Contrary to this expectation, we observe a field-induced Chern gap with Chern number $C=-6$ emanating from charge neutrality; in this gap, the Hall resistance is quantized to within $2\%$ of the expected value, $-h/6e^2$, at 4.6 K. We explain this behavior using both Hofstadter and orbital Zeeman calculations, which show that a moderate magnetic field drives a valley-selective topological transition. Above the transition, the total Chern number of the occupied states in each spin-valley flavor becomes identical across neighboring domains, and the domain-wall modes can become gapped. Though the central valence-band Chern numbers still differ between the two domain types, the observed quantized transport attests to a global gap.

</details>


### [167] [Hidden Frustration in Collinear Altermagnets: Pairing Vortices and Equilibrium Spin Current Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26260)
*Vincent P. Flynn,Benedetta Flebus*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个磁体在保持完美共线排列的同时，其量子真空（quantum vacuum）可以发生循环。在中心对称的交替磁体中，对称性允许的局域交错Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya耦合在动量空间的高对称点处将规范不可移除的涡旋-反涡旋对（vortex-antivortex pair）印刻到反常磁振子配对关联中。在实空间中，这些隐藏涡旋产生反铁手性排列的平衡自旋流，相邻原胞周围以相反方向循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在经典共线磁序下是否仍可存在规范不可移除的量子涨落结构，即经典共线性与量子纠缠是否可共存。此前复杂的配对、规范不变通量和平衡环流等结构通常被认为仅存在于电子通量相、自旋液体和受挫量子磁体中。

Method: 作者研究了一种中心对称的交替磁体（altermagnet），分析了其中对称性允许的局域交错Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya耦合对磁振子配对相关性的影响。通过考察动量空间高对称点处的反常磁振子配对关联，识别出规范不可移除的涡旋-反涡旋对，并将这些隐藏涡旋映射到实空间中的平衡自旋流模式。

Result: 发现即使在经典自旋保持共线排列的情况下，量子真空仍可携带规范不可移除的拓扑缺陷。在动量空间中形成涡旋-反涡旋对，在实空间中产生反铁手性（antiferrochiral）排列的平衡自旋流，相邻原胞周围的循环方向相反。规范不可移除的受挫（frustration）在经典共线性的表象下仍然存在，完全蕴含于量子关联之中。

Conclusion: 复杂的配对、规范不变通量（gauge-invariant flux）和平衡环流等结构——这些通常出现在电子通量相、自旋液体和受挫量子磁体中的现象——可以被编码在共线磁体的压缩真空（squeezed vacuum）中。该结果建立了经典共线磁序与量子拓扑结构之间的联系。

Abstract: We show that a magnet can remain perfectly collinear while its quantum vacuum circulates. In a centrosymmetric altermagnet, a symmetry-allowed locally staggered Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya coupling imprints a gauge-irremovable vortex-antivortex pair into the anomalous magnon pair correlations at high-symmetry points in momentum space. In real space, these hidden vortices produce an antiferrochiral array of equilibrium spin currents circulating oppositely around neighboring plaquettes. Gauge-irremovable frustration therefore survives despite classical collinearity: it exists entirely in the quantum correlations. Our results show that complex pairing, gauge-invariant fluxes, and equilibrium loop currents - structures encountered across electronic flux phases, spin liquids, and frustrated quantum magnets - can be encoded in the squeezed vacuum of a collinear magnet.

</details>


### [168] [How quantum is quantum geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26269)
*Ady Stern,Felix von Oppen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文通过构造一个经典力学系统——携带经典磁矩并在动量依赖磁场中进动的点粒子——证明了通常被认为源于量子几何张量（quantum geometric tensor）的多种可观测量（如贝里曲率现象和量子度规现象）并非本质上需要量子力学，在纯经典哈密顿力学框架内即可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何张量（由贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与量子度规（quantum metric）组成）被认为是反常霍尔效应、平带超流权重等众多可观测量的基础。本文提出一个关键问题：这些可观测量中究竟哪些真正需要量子力学，从而探索其背后的经典物理根源。

Method: 构建一个纯经典系统：一个携带经典磁矩 \boldsymbol{\ell} 的点粒子，在动量依赖的磁场 \mathbf{B}(\mathbf{p}) 中进动，并在哈密顿经典力学框架内进行分析。该方法将磁矩沿磁场方向的分量与横向进动分量分别与贝里曲率和量子度规现象建立对应。

Result: 磁矩沿磁场方向的分量复现了贝里曲率相关现象，横向进动分量复现了量子度规相关现象。粒子展现出与度规对应的位置展宽（二阶矩），获得轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment），并因位置依赖的力产生惯性质量，在名义上无色散的系统中得到非零的德鲁德权重（Drude weight）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，贝里曲率和量子度规所对应的物理现象（反常霍尔效应、平带超流权重等）可以在纯经典哈密顿力学中实现，无需预设量子力学前提。这一发现对理解量子几何张量的物理本质具有基础性意义。

Abstract: The quantum geometric tensor - the Berry curvature together with the quantum metric - now underlies a long list of observables, from the anomalous Hall effect to the superfluid weight of a flat band. We ask which of these observables actually require quantum mechanics. To answer this question, we study a purely classical system: a point particle carrying a classical magnetic moment $\boldsymbol{\ell}$ that precesses in a momentum-dependent magnetic field $\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{p})$. Within Hamiltonian classical mechanics, the component of $\boldsymbol{\ell}$ along the field reproduces the Berry-curvature phenomena, while its precessing transverse component reproduces the quantum-metric phenomena. The particle acquires a position spread whose second moment is the metric, an orbital magnetic moment, and - most strikingly - an inertial mass generated by a position-dependent force, and with it a nonzero Drude weight in a system that is nominally dispersionless.

</details>


### [169] [Majorana signatures in an asymmetrically coupled quantum dot--topological superconducting nanowire junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26341)
*Levente Máthé,Ioan Grosu,Liviu P. Zârbo,Ionel Ţifrea*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文对中心量子点（quantum dot）非对称耦合到正常引线及两个马约拉纳束缚态（Majorana bound states）的纳米系统进行了理论研究，考察了引线—量子点耦合不对称参数α和偏压不对称参数q对线性电导的影响，分别针对未杂化与杂化马约拉纳束缚态两种情形，揭示了电导对磁通相位φ的依赖规律及其周期性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳束缚态是拓扑超导态研究中实现拓扑量子计算的关键对象，其在输运实验中的识别受实际系统中非对称参数影响显著。作者旨在系统研究引线—量子点耦合不对称性和偏压不对称性如何调制系统的线性电导，从而为实验上正确识别马约拉纳束缚态的输运特征提供理论指导。

Method: 采用理论分析方法研究一个纳米尺度输运系统：中心量子点非对称耦合到正常引线以及位于拓扑超导纳米线两端（受可调磁通控制）的两个马约拉纳束缚态。分别在零温度与有限温度、未杂化与杂化马约拉纳束缚态等情形下，分析线性电导对耦合不对称参数α、偏压不对称参数q及磁通相位φ的依赖关系。

Result: 在零温极限下，对于未杂化的马约拉纳束缚态，线性电导仅在磁通相位φ=(2n+1)π时有限，且满足标度关系G=2qαe²/[h(α+1)]；对于杂化束缚态，电导对系统参数呈现复杂依赖。有限温度下，未杂化情形中电导随磁通相位φ以2π为周期振荡，电导峰位可通过改变偏压不对称参数q从φ=2nπ移至φ=(2n+1)π；杂化情形下，当量子点能级位于引线费米能级（ε_d=ε_F）时行为类似，但当ε_d≠ε_F时振荡周期变为4π，且峰位依赖于ε_d等参数。

Conclusion: 引线—量子点耦合不对称参数和偏压不对称参数在实际实验中普遍存在，能强烈影响马约拉纳束缚态输运特征的识别与观测，本研究揭示了这些非对称因素对线性电导的调控规律，凸显其在马约拉纳束缚态实验探测中的重要性。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of the quantum transport through a nanoscale system in which a central quantum dot (QD) is coupled asymmetrically to normal leads and to two Majorana bound states (MBSs) localized at the ends of a topological superconducting nanowire threaded by a tunable magnetic flux. The effects of the leads--QD coupling asymmetry parameter $α$ and the bias voltage asymmetry parameter $q$ on the system's linear conductance are considered for the case of unhybridized and hybridized MBSs. In the zero-temperature limit, for unhybridized MBSs the system's linear conductance is finite only when the magnetic flux phase $φ= (2n+1)π$ ($n\in\mathbb{Z}$) and it scales as $\mathcal{G}=2qαe^2/[h(α+1)]$, while for hybridized MBSs it presents a complicated dependence on the system's parameters. At finite temperature, for unhybridized MBSs, the system's linear conductance oscillates as a function of the magnetic flux phase $φ$ with a period of $2π$, and the position of the linear conductance maxima can be shifted from $φ=2nπ$ to $φ=(2n+1)π$ by simply varying the value of the bias voltage asymmetry parameter $q$. For hybridized MBSs, the conductance exhibits a similar behavior when the energy level of the central QD, $\varepsilon_d$, is tuned at the leads' Fermi level ($\varepsilon_d=\varepsilon_F$), although when $\varepsilon_d\neq\varepsilon_F$ the oscillation period changes to $4π$, and the position of the linear conductance maxima depends on the actual value of $\varepsilon_d$ and other parameters in the system. Our results highlight the experimental importance of the leads-QD and bias voltage asymmetry parameters, which are often present in realistic experimental setups, and can strongly affect the identification and observation of MBSs transport signatures.

</details>


### [170] [Magnetic Communication with an Acoustically Actuated Magnetoelectric Resonator and a Quantum Diamond Magnetometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26396)
*Christopher T. -K. Lew,Andrew D. M. Charles,Brant C. Gibson,Jean-Philippe Tetienne,David A. Broadway*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型混合磁通信系统，采用声学驱动的磁电谐振器（magnetoelectric resonator）作为发射端，以基于金刚石氮-空位中心（nitrogen-vacancy centers）的量子磁力计作为接收端，在20 kHz交流磁场下实现50 pT/√Hz的灵敏度与1.2 mrad/√Hz的相位灵敏度，为传统环形天线方案提供了可行的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁通信使用环形天线（loop antenna）实现发射与接收，但其灵敏度与频率响应分别受限于环形截面积的大小，限制了在导电介质（如水下、地下）中短至中距离通信的性能提升。本文旨在探索一种不受尺寸约束的混合磁通信系统，以克服现有方案的局限性。

Method: 构建了一个混合磁通信系统：发射端采用紧凑且辐射效率高的声学驱动磁电谐振器，接收端采用基于金刚石氮-空位中心的微米级量子磁力计；二者的核心性能均不受尺寸限制。在20 kHz交流磁场下进行了振幅编码与相位编码的发射和接收演示。

Result: 在20 kHz交流磁场下实现了振幅与相位编码的传输与接收，分别达到50 pT/√Hz的磁场灵敏度与1.2 mrad/√Hz的相位灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了混合磁电谐振器与量子金刚石磁力计组成的通信系统是传统环形天线方案的一种可行且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Wireless communication via propagating magnetic fields is a communication modality that has recently garnered significant interest for short-to-medium range communication in conductive mediums, such as underwater and underground, where existing approaches utilizing electric fields are highly inefficient. Typical implementations of magnetic communication make use of loop antennas as both the transmitter and receiver, with the sensitivity and frequency response scaling with and inversely with the loop cross-sectional area, respectively. Here, we explore an alternative hybrid magnetic communication system consisting of an highly radiation efficient and compact acoustically actuated magnetoelectric resonator as the transmitter, and a highly sensitive micrometer scale quantum magnetometer based on nitrogen-vacancy centers in diamond as the receiver, with their core properties unconstrained by size. We demonstrate amplitude and phase-encoded transmission and reception of AC magnetic fields at $f_{\mathrm{AC}}$ = 20 kHz, achieving a sensitivity of 50 pT/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ and 1.2 mrad/$\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$, respectively. This work establishes the use of hybrid magnetoelectric resonator and quantum diamond magnetometer communication system as a viable alternative to existing loop-based approaches.

</details>


### [171] [Statistical Disorder in MBE-Grown AlGaAs/GaAs Superlattices for Quantum Bragg Mirrors using Synchrotron X-ray Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26406)
*Mateus T. Souza,Germano M. Penello,Guilherme A. Calligaris,Sergio L. Morelhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一套系统性方法，通过高能同步辐射X射线衍射结合递归动力学衍射理论和系综模拟退火精修，对203层AlGaAs/GaAs量子布拉格反射镜（Quantum Bragg Mirror, QBM）器件中的纳米尺度结构无序进行了无损量化，实现了约0.2–0.6 nm（约1–2个原子单层）的统计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子束外延（Molecular Beam Epitaxy, MBE）生长的AlGaAs/GaAs超晶格是先进光电器件（包括QBM红外探测器）的基础，但MBE生长过程中的固有统计涨落会引入纳米尺度结构无序，降低器件效率，因此需要精确量化此类无序。

Method: 对203层QBM器件进行了高能（25 keV）同步辐射X射线衍射结构表征，并将递归动力学衍射形式（recursive dynamical diffraction formalism）与系综模拟退火精修（ensemble simulated annealing refinement）相结合，提取统计稳健的逐层厚度分布。

Result: 分析揭示了系统性的、与材料相关的厚度偏差：所有AlGaAs势垒层均比标称值薄0.3–0.6 nm；最终宏观顶部接触层存在显著的35 nm deficit，首个GaAs层存在40 nm deficit；整体统计精度达到约0.2–0.6 nm。

Conclusion: 该无损诊断方法可为MBE通量校准（flux calibration）协议提供直接可操作的反馈，有助于改善超晶格器件的周期性和界面质量。

Abstract: AlGaAs/GaAs superlattices grown by Molecular Beam Epitaxy (MBE) are foundational for advanced optoelectronic devices, including Quantum Bragg Mirror (QBM) infrared detectors. The performance of these devices critically depends on achieving near-perfect periodicity and abrupt interfaces, however, intrinsic statistical fluctuations during MBE growth introduce nanoscale structural disorder that can degrade device efficiency. In this study, we present a comprehensive methodology for quantifying this disorder in a 203-layer QBM device. High-resolution structural characterization was performed using high-energy (25 keV) synchrotron X-ray diffraction. By coupling a recursive dynamical diffraction formalism with an ensemble simulated annealing refinement, we extracted statistically robust, layer-by-layer thickness profiles. Our analysis reveals highly systematic, material-specific deviations from the nominal design: all AlGaAs barrier layers were consistently thinner than nominal by 0.3-0.6 nm. Furthermore, the sequential thickness profile successfully identified a significant 35 nm deficit in the final macroscopic top contact layer and a 40 nm deficit in the first GaAs layer. Achieving a statistical precision of about 0.2 to 0.6 nm (approximately 1-2 atomic monolayers), this non-destructive diagnostic approach provides directly actionable feedback for MBE flux calibration protocols.

</details>


### [172] [Optical spectroscopy of composite fermion edge states in the fractional quantum Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26521)
*Maria Sebastian,Ashutosh Singh,Alexey Belyanin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究证明可通过亚太赫兹（sub-terahertz）光谱选择性探测和激发分数量子霍尔效应（fractional quantum Hall effect）样品中的边缘态，并利用复合费米子（composite fermion）平均场框架计算Jain填充分数序列的吸光度谱，揭示边缘态吸收峰与复合费米子有效质量的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔体系中的边缘态承载丰富的物理信息，但目前缺乏选择性探测边缘态的光学手段，特别是需要一种能够区分体态与边缘态贡献的光谱方法。

Method: 采用复合费米子平均场框架（composite fermion mean-field framework），将强关联的分数量子霍尔问题映射为有效的整数量子霍尔问题，从而计算涵盖体态和边缘态的Jain序列填充分数下的吸光度谱（absorbance spectrum）。

Result: 复合费米子边缘态吸收峰出现在毫米波至亚太赫兹范围（如在GaAs中B=10 T时为60–500 GHz），相对体复合费米子回旋频率发生蓝移，但远低于同磁场下整数量子霍尔回旋频率尺度。每个系列中分辨峰的数目计数填充的Lambda能级并指纹化填充分数。边缘附近的反演对称性破缺激活了体态中禁戒的光学跃迁，并在电偶极近似（electric-dipole approximation）下产生二阶非线性过程。

Conclusion: 吸收光谱提供了一种直接光学探测复合费米子有效质量的方法，该质量完全由电子-电子相互作用产生，因此该研究建立了边缘态光谱学与相互作用诱导质量之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We show that edge states in fractional quantum Hall effect samples can be selectively probed and excited with sub-terahertz optical spectroscopy. Using the composite fermion (CF) mean-field framework, which maps the strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall problem onto an effective integer quantum Hall problem, we calculate the absorbance spectrum for the Jain sequence of filling fractions including both bulk and edge states. The CF edge-state absorption peaks appear in the millimeter-wave to sub-terahertz range, e.g., 60-500 GHz at B = 10 T in GaAs, i.e. they are blueshifted with respect to the bulk CF cyclotron frequency but are well below the integer quantum Hall cyclotron frequency scale at the same magnetic fields. The number of resolved peaks in each series of the absorption spectrum counts the filled Lambda-levels and fingerprints the fraction. Inversion symmetry breaking near the edge activates optical transitions forbidden in the bulk and enables second-order nonlinear processes in electric-dipole approximation. The absolute frequency scale of the spectrum is set by the CF effective mass, which is generated entirely by electron-electron interactions, so the absorption spectrum provides a direct optical probe of this interaction-induced mass.

</details>


### [173] [Topologically protected perfect crossed Andreev reflection in flux-engineered quantum wire junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26702)
*Abhiram Soori*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种磁通可调的四端正常金属-超导体结，通过精确破坏性阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）和珀尔斯（Peierls）干涉，在φ=π处结构性地禁止电子隧穿（ET）和局域安德烈夫反射（AR），实现确定性的100%效率交叉安德烈夫反射（CAR），并证明该行为由量子化拓扑缠绕数保护，具有正交叉关联散粒噪声的特征和宽带、抗无序的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子信息处理需要高效产生非局域纠缠电子对，库珀对劈裂（Cooper-pair splitting）是实现这一目标的关键途径。然而，交叉安德烈夫反射（CAR）总是与电子隧穿（ET）和局域安德烈夫反射（AR）等竞争输运过程共存，难以在实验中分离和隔离，因此需要新的结构设计来实现确定性、高效的CAR。

Method: 提出一种磁通可调的四端正常金属-超导体结（flux-tunable four-terminal normal metal-superconductor junction）。在半磁通量子φ=π处，利用精确破坏性的阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）干涉和珀尔斯（Peierls）干涉分别结构性地禁止ET和AR；通过调节中央结跳跃（central junction hopping）参数将电子反射抑制为零；运用柯西辐角原理（Cauchy argument principle）证明该抑制对应量子化拓扑缠绕数（topological winding number）。

Result: 证明该方案可实现量子拓扑保护的100%交叉安德烈夫反射概率。该机制对应严格为正的交叉关联散粒噪声（cross-correlation shot noise），可作为库珀对劈裂的明确实验特征；并且该完美CAR在超导能隙内近乎宽带，对结构无序具有显著鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高弹性（highly resilient）的结构架构，可实现确定性的非局域纠缠产生，为固态量子信息处理中的库珀对劈裂研究提供了新的实验可行途径。

Abstract: Generating non-locally entangled electron pairs via Cooper-pair splitting is vital for solid-state quantum information processing. However, isolating the underlying crossed Andreev reflection (CAR) is challenging due to competing transport processes like electron tunneling (ET) and local Andreev reflection (AR). Here, we propose a flux-tunable four-terminal normal metal-superconductor junction that achieves deterministic, 100\% efficient CAR. We demonstrate that at exactly half a magnetic flux quantum ($φ=π$), exact destructive Aharonov-Bohm and Peierls interferences structurally forbid ET and AR respectively. By tuning the central junction hopping, electron reflection is also suppressed to zero. Using the Cauchy argument principle, we prove that this suppression manifests as a quantized topological winding number, guaranteeing a topologically protected unit CAR probability. We establish that this regime is characterized by a strictly positive cross-correlation shot noise, providing an unambiguous experimental signature of Cooper-pair splitting. Furthermore, this perfect CAR is nearly broadband within the superconducting gap and remarkably robust against structural disorder, offering a highly resilient architecture for deterministic nonlocal entanglement generation.

</details>


### [174] [Observation of dodecagonal replica bands in 30$^{\circ}$-twisted bilayer WSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26864)
*Christian Valentiner-Branth Fokdal,Chakradhar Sahoo,Thomas S. Nielsen,Alfred J. H. Jones,Zhihao Jiang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Marcin Mucha-Kruczyński,Søren Ulstrup*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用微米级空间分辨率的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）直接观测了30°扭转双层WSe₂中的复制能带（replica bands），并通过层间Umklapp散射机制解释了其对称性和强度分布。谱函数测量结果与K谷附近的范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularity）一致，表明大扭转角双层体系中存在电子重构的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides）的扭转双层体系是实现可调准晶序（quasicrystalline orders）及其涌现性质的有前景平台，然而具有多轨道十二边形复制能带的二维准晶中底层电子结构和散射过程此前尚未被确定。

Method: 采用具有微米级空间分辨率的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对30°扭转双层WSe₂进行直接测量，观测复制能带，并结合谱函数测量分析其对称性与强度分布。

Result: 直接观测到30°扭转双层WSe₂中的复制能带，其对称性和强度分布可由从底层到顶层WSe₂的层间Umklapp散射（interlayer Umklapp scattering）解释。谱函数测量显示K谷附近存在范霍夫奇点。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在大层间扭转角度的双层体系中，范霍夫奇点的存在为诱导电子重构提供了可能，从而拓展了扭转二维材料体系在量子序调控方面的应用前景。

Abstract: Twisted bilayers of two-dimensional (2D) transition metal dichalcogenides are promising systems for achieving tunable quasicrystalline orders with emergent properties. The underpinning electronic structure and scattering processes in 2D quasicrystals with multiorbital dodecagonal replica bands have so far not been determined. Here, we utilize angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) with micrometer spatial resolution to directly observe replica bands in 30$^{\circ}$-twisted bilayer WSe$_2$. The symmetry and intensity distribution of the observed replicas are explained by interlayer Umklapp scattering from bottom to top WSe$_2$ layers. Our spectral function measurements are consistent with the presence of a van Hove singularity adjacent to the $\mathrm{K}$-valleys, which underlines the possibility of inducing electronic reconstructions in bilayers with a large interlayer twist angle.

</details>


### [175] [Switchable giant room-temperature nonlinear Hall effect in Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26869)
*Margherita Melegari,Ignacio Gutiérrez-Lezama,Justin C. W. Song,Alberto F. Morpurgo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道了双离子栅调控的双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene）器件中的非线性霍尔效应（nonlinear Hall effect, NLHE），其二阶非线性极化率在室温下达到3×10⁻³ μm·S/V，远高于此前报道的室温NLHE材料体系，并可与低温下WTe₂或石墨烯莫尔超晶格中的数值相当，对应的太赫兹电压响应度达4×10⁴ V/W，接近商用肖特基二极管水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际无结器件中利用量子二阶非线性输运需要具有大且可调非线性并能在室温下工作的材料，而当前材料平台尚无法满足这一挑战。本文旨在寻找并演示一种可在室温下工作、非线性可切换且性能优异的无结非线性技术平台。

Method: 作者在双离子栅调控（double-ionic gated）的双层石墨烯器件中实现了异常强的反演对称性破缺（inversion breaking），通过离子栅极提供了有效的调控手段来增强材料的二阶非线性响应。实验中测量了器件的非线性霍尔效应及其二阶非线性极化率χ⁽²⁾_yxx和太赫兹电压响应度。

Result: 观察到的NLHE可方便地切换（开、关及符号反转），其二阶非线性极化率χ⁽²⁾_yxx在室温下达3×10⁻³ μm·S/V，与WTe₂或石墨烯莫尔超晶格在低温下的典型值相当，比此前用于寻找室温NLHE的材料体系高出三到四个数量级。对应的太赫兹电压响应度约4×10⁴ V/W，可比拟商用肖特基二极管（Schottky diodes）。

Conclusion: 双离子栅调控的双层石墨烯器件的室温NLHE性能比此前报道的室温非线性霍尔效应器件高出数个数量级，使其成为无结非线性技术（junction-free nonlinear technology）的优选平台，为室温可工作的量子非线性输运器件提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Utilizing quantum second-order nonlinear transport for practical junction-free devices require materials with large and tunable nonlinearites at room temperature -- a current materials platform challenge. Here, we report the nonlinear Hall effect (NLHE) in double-ionic gated bilayer graphene devices that enable unusually strong inversion breaking. We observe NLHE that are readily switchable (on, off, and sign reversed) with second order nonlinear susceptibilities $χ^{(2)}_{yxx}$ that reaches giant room-temperature values of $3\,10^{-3}\,μ\mathrm{m}\,\mathrm{S/V}$, comparable to values commonly observed at low temperature in WTe$_2$ or in graphene-based moiré superlattices, and three-to-four orders of magnitude larger than values reported in material systems recently employed in search of a room-temperature NLHE. Our devices produce corresponding THz voltage responsivities $\simeq 4\,10^{4}\,\mathrm{V/W}$, comparable to commercially available Schottky diodes. These are orders of magnitude better than for previously reported room-temperature NLHE devices rendering double-ionic gated bilayer graphene a choice platform for junction-free nonlinear technology.

</details>


### [176] [Exceptional point induced by hyperbolicity in an electrostatic spherical shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26905)
*Álvaro Buendía,Marcelo S. Barreiro,Nuno M. R. Peres*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明各向异性核壳纳米粒子的径向静电问题无需增益、损耗或非互易性即可实现奇异点（exceptional point, EP）。通过对数径向坐标将问题映射为阻尼振子及两点Hatano-Nelson哈密顿量，非厄米性在空间中而非时间中被模拟，唯一控制参数是介电各向异性度 ε_t/ε_r。无源、互易、无损耗的壳层仅凭双曲性（hyperbolicity）即可承载EP和非厄米相变，超阈后可产生空间振荡与热区，为无增益损耗的非厄米物理模拟提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异点（exceptional points）传统上依赖平衡增益/损耗或非互易性来构造，这些条件在无源、互易、无损耗的系统中难以实现。作者希望在纯被动谐振系统中寻找EP的非传统实现途径，从而拓宽非厄米物理的适用范围。

Method: 将各向异性核壳纳米粒子的径向静电问题转换到对数径向坐标，识别其为柯西-欧拉方程（Cauchy-Euler equation），并证明其可映射为阻尼谐振子以及两格点Hatano-Nelson哈密顿量。由此将时间域的非厄米模拟转化为空间域的等效，唯一控制参数为介电各向异性比 ε_t/ε_r。

Result: 壳层系统为无源、互易、无损耗，但仍有EP存在，并发生由双曲性主导的非厄米相变。超越双曲性阈值后，壳内电场由单调衰减转变为空间振荡，在中间半径处产生热区（hotspots），可应用于荧光调控、强耦合、传感或隐身。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了双曲介质（hyperbolic media）作为无需增益、损耗或非互易性即可模拟非厄米物理的通用平台，拓展了EP的实验可及性与应用范围。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are non--Hermitian degeneracies at which both eigenvalues and eigenvectors coalesce, usually engineered through balanced gain and loss or non--reciprocity. In this work, we show that the radial electrostatic problem of an anisotropic core--shell nanoparticle realizes an EP without any of the alluded conditons. Written in the logarithmic radial coordinate, the problem is a Cauchy--Euler equation that maps onto a damped oscillator and, equivalently, onto a two-site Hatano--Nelson Hamiltonian, so that the non-Hermiticity is emulated in space rather than in time and the sole control parameter is the degree of dielectric anisotropy, $\varepsilon_t/\varepsilon_r$. The shell is passive, reciprocal, and lossless, yet it hosts an EP and a non--Hermitian phase transition governed by hyperbolicity alone. Beyond a hyperbolicity threshold the electric field inside the shell turns from monotonic decay into spatial oscillation, producing hotspots at intermediate radii which can be exploited to tailor fluorescence, strong coupling, sensing, or cloaking. More broadly, this establishes hyperbolic media as a platform to simulate non--Hermitian physics without gain, loss, or non--reciprocity.

</details>


### [177] [Hall effect in viscous flows of two-dimensional electrons in samples with edges of arbitrary roughness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26979)
*A. V. Gert,P. S. Alekseev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套二维电子系统中流体动力学磁输运（hydrodynamic magnetotransport）理论，描述了低缺陷密度样品中电子黏性流体在任意粗糙度边界条件下的输运行为，发现霍尔电阻（Hall resistance）在高磁场下呈饱和并在特定粗糙度条件下存在极小值，可作为识别电子输运流体动力学区域（hydrodynamic regime）的实验标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超洁净导体中，快速的粒子间碰撞可导致黏性电子流体（viscous electron fluid）的形成，从而进入流体动力学输运区域（hydrodynamic transport regime）。已有研究中边界粗糙度对二维电子流体动力学磁输运的影响尚未得到系统描述，需要建立一套能涵盖任意粗糙度边界条件的理论框架。

Method: 作者在低缺陷密度样品中，以单一具有速度量纲的参数描述边界粗糙度，并将其纳入样品边缘的边界条件中。在此模型基础上，计算了长样品中电子流体流动剖面（flow profiles），以及相应的纵向电阻（longitudinal resistance）和霍尔电阻。通过分析弛豫过程对霍尔电阻的贡献，考察其随磁场和边缘粗糙度参数的变化行为。

Result: 与弛豫过程相关的霍尔电阻贡献在高磁场极限下呈现饱和行为，并在边缘足够粗糙时作为磁场函数出现一个极小值。该极小值在边缘粗糙度减小或样品宽度与体缺陷散射增大时消失。

Conclusion: 霍尔电阻的这些特征可作为在实验中识别电子输运流体动力学区域的标志，并可用于确定该区域的参数，特别是边缘粗糙度的大小。

Abstract: In ultra-clean conductors, fast inter-particle collisions can lead to the formation of a viscous electron fluid and realization of the hydrodynamic transport regime. Here we develop a theory of hydrodynamic magnetotransport of two-dimensional (2D) electrons in samples with low densities of defects and edges of arbitrary roughness. Within our model the roughness is described by a single parameter with the dimension of speed in the boundary conditions on sample edges. The electron-fluid flow profiles in long samples, as well as the corresponding longitudinal and Hall resistances, are calculated. The contribution to the Hall resistance associated with the relaxation processes exhibits a saturation in the limit of high magnetic field and a minimum as a function of the magnetic field for sufficiently rough edges. The minimum disappears as the edge roughness decreases or the sample width and bulk scattering by defects increase. These properties of the Hall resistance can serve as the signs to identify the hydrodynamic regime of electron transport in experiments and can be used to determine its parameters, in particular, the degree of edge roughness.

</details>


### [178] [Spin preservation in screw-symmetric molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27002)
*Jonas Bloch,Fedor Baranov,Maxim Breitkreiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了具有离散螺旋对称性（discrete screw symmetry）的长分子中自旋保持（spin preservation）可远超自旋混合时标的增强机制，并从解析推导和紧束缚模型两方面证实了该效应，同时提出了基于自旋阀结构的大磁阻信号作为实验验证方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长分子的电子输运过程中，自旋通常被认为仅在停留时间远短于自旋混合特征时标时得以保持。作者旨在探索是否可以通过特定的分子对称性超越这一限制，从而为长手性分子中的手性诱导自旋选择性（CISS）效应提供机制解释。

Method: 作者提供了增强自旋保持效应的解析推导，并使用紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）进行数值验证，考察对称性破缺及从离散对称转向连续对称时效应的变化；此外，通过输运模拟展示了在双磁性电极构成的自旋阀结构中，对称性保护的自旋保真度（spin fidelity）会产生显著的磁阻信号。

Result: 具有离散螺旋对称性的分子中，自旋分离在准动量（quasi-momentum）空间中显著增强，使自旋保持时间远超特征自旋混合时标。该效应随螺旋对称性破坏或退化为连续对称而逐渐减弱，且在自旋阀装置中表现为强磁阻信号，可作为实验上可探测的表征。

Conclusion: 对称性保护的自旋保持为长手性分子中的CISS效应提供了理论支持，并表明放大的自旋劈裂机制可能支撑长分子中自旋依赖的输运行为；所提议的磁阻信号为实验验证提供了可操作的路径。

Abstract: In electronic transport through long molecules, spin is expected to be preserved only when the dwell time is much shorter than the characteristic spin-mixing timescale $\hbar/Δ$, where $Δ$ is the magnitude of a spin-dependent potential, such as spin-orbit coupling. We show that, in molecules featuring discrete screw symmetry, spin preservation can be enhanced far beyond this timescale owing to strong spin separation in quasi-momentum. This spin fidelity in long molecules is consistent with chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS), suggesting spin-dependent transport in long, chiral molecules with amplified spin-splitting mechanisms. We provide analytical derivation of the enhanced spin preservation and test it on tight-binding models, which confirm that the effect gradually weakens when the screw symmetry is broken or changes from discrete to continuous. Furthermore, we perform transport simulations to show that a strong magnetoresistance trace of symmetry-protected spin fidelity emerges in a spin-valve setup with two magnetic leads, which we propose as an experimentally accessible signature.

</details>


### [179] [Magnon-induced phononic Chern insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27011)
*Rui-Chang Shen,Yihao Yang,Haoran Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由磁子诱导的声子陈绝缘体（magnon-induced phononic Chern insulator），通过将铁磁岛置于蜂窝声子晶体（honeycomb phononic crystal）六边形中心，利用圆偏振基特尔模（Kittel mode）与声子耦合打破时间反演对称性并打开完整的陈能隙（Chern gap），实现可调陈数|C|=1和|C|=2的拓扑声子相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频人造声子晶体为片上集成提供了低损耗平台，但在GHz频率实现陈声子相（Chern phononic phase）仍具挑战，需要探索可行的实现机制。

Method: 在蜂窝声子晶体六边形中心放置铁磁岛，使圆偏振基特尔模与周围声子产生带相位缠绕的耦合，从而打破时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）。在大失谐（large-detuning）区域，该机制等效于一个具有磁子诱导复数跃迁的Haldane型声子模型；通过调节磁子-声子相互作用可以调控系统的陈相。

Result: 该系统可进入陈数|C|=1和|C|=2的可调陈相（Chern phases），且预测的能隙超过实际声子和磁子线宽，可在GHz声学器件中观测。

Conclusion: 手性磁子-声子杂化（chiral magnon-phonon hybridization）为磁性可重构拓扑声子学（magnetic reconfigurable topological phononics）提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: High-frequency artificial phononic crystals offer a low-loss platform compatible with on-chip integration, yet realizing Chern phononic phases at GHz frequencies remains challenging. Here, we propose a magnon-induced phononic Chern insulator in a honeycomb phononic crystal hybridized with ferromagnetic islands at the hexagon centers. A circularly polarized Kittel mode couples to the surrounding phonons with a phase winding, which breaks time-reversal symmetry and opens a full Chern gap. In the large-detuning regime, this mechanism leads to an effective Haldane-type phononic model with magnon-induced complex hopping. By tuning the magnon-phonon interaction, the full hybrid system accesses Chern phases with tunable Chern numbers |C|=1 and |C|=2. The predicted gaps can exceed realistic phonon and magnon linewidths, enabling their observation in GHz acoustic devices. Our work establishes chiral magnon--phonon hybridization as a route to magnetically reconfigurable topological phononics.

</details>


### [180] [Giant bulk photovoltaic effect driven by interfacial symmetry breaking in MoS2/Ta2NiSe5 heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27064)
*Jianwen Ma,Pengliang Leng,Lei Peng,Congming Hao,Xianghao Meng,Jiaqi Liu,Yang Gan,Min Luo,Zifan Zhang,Jiaming Gu,Qinghang Liu,Lidan Duan,Du Xiang,Wu Shi,Peng Wang,Weibin Chu,Xiang Yuan,Weida Hu,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究在交叉条状MoS2/Ta2NiSe5范德瓦尔斯异质结（vdW heterostructure）的重叠区域观察到显著的体光伏效应（BPV effect），利用正交器件几何结构独立探测层内和界面光电响应路径，并通过栅极电压和垂直电场调控层间势垒，实现了247 A/cm²的零偏置光电流密度和0.99 V⁻¹的BPV系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 范德瓦尔斯异质结虽可通过界面对称性破缺调控非常规体光伏效应，但结构复杂性、自发电荷转移和强层间耦合导致多种光物理机制共存，掩盖了BPV响应的微观起源，阻碍了其理性优化。现有难题在于如何在复杂体系中解耦并识别主导机制。

Method: 采用交叉条状MoS2/Ta2NiSe5 vdW异质结构，利用垂直堆叠引起的对称性破缺移除MoS2的反演中心；正交器件几何结构实现层内与界面光电响应路径的独立探测，以分离竞争机制；通过栅极电压和垂直电场调控层间势垒景观；辅以理论建模支持实验结果的解释。

Result: 在异质结重叠区域观察到局域化的显著BPV效应；实现了优化的零偏置光电流密度247 A/cm²和BPV系数0.99 V⁻¹；自发界面电荷转移建立了强层间电子耦合，理论建模验证了实验发现。

Conclusion: 极简器件几何结构能够将复杂异质结构转化为实验上可操控的平台，为分析和优化界面驱动的BPV效应提供了新策略，对自供电光电器件、宽带光电探测和能量采集纳米器件具有应用前景。

Abstract: Van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures offer a versatile platform for engineering unconventional bulk photovoltaic (BPV) effect through interfacial symmetry breaking. However, the coexistence of multiple photophysical mechanisms, driven by structural complexity, spontaneous charge transfer, and strong interlayer coupling, often obscures the microscopic origin of the BPV response and hinders its rational optimization. Here, we demonstrate a pronounced BPV effect localized at the overlap region of a cross-bar MoS2/Ta2NiSe5 vdW heterostructure, where symmetry breaking induced by vertical stacking lifts the inversion center of MoS2. The orthogonal device geometry enables the independent probing of intralayer and interfacial photoresponse pathways, facilitating clear separation of competing mechanisms. Spontaneous interfacial charge transfer between MoS2 and Ta2NiSe5 further establishes a strong interlayer electronic coupling. By modulating the interlayer potential landscape through gate voltage and vertical electric fields, we achieve an optimized zero-bias photocurrent density of 247 A/cm2 and a BPV coefficient of 0.99 V-1. Supported by theoretical modelling, our results illustrate how minimalist device geometry can transform complex heterostructures into experimentally tractable platforms. This strategy paves the way for analyzing and optimizing interface-driven BPV effect, with implications for self-powered optoelectronics, broadband photodetection, and energy-harvesting nanodevices.

</details>


### [181] [Terahertz anomalous Hall effect in magnetic Weyl semimetal Co$_3$Sn$_2$S$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27195)
*Ashutosh Singh,Hongjing Xu,Xielin Wang,Kohei Fujiwara,Atsushi Tsukazaki,Shengxi Huang,Andrey Baydin,Junichiro Kono,Alexey Belyanin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用太赫兹光谱学结合半解析计算，系统研究了Co₃Sn₂S₂韦尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）的低能旋光（gyrotropic）光学响应，从动量空间节点分离的内在旋光性角度给出了所观测磁光现象的可靠解释，并通过实验与理论的定量对比对材料参数施加了严格约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性破缺的韦尔半金属至少含两个电子能带结构中的节点，作为贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的源和汇，产生反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）。尽管Co₃Sn₂S₂中已观测到巨大的反常霍尔效应，但直接探测韦尔节点附近准粒子激发（quasiparticle excitations）的低能区实验研究仍然有限，因此需要系统探索其内在低能光学响应。

Method: 采用太赫兹光谱学（terahertz spectroscopy）实验手段，结合基于物理直观的有效模型（effective model）进行的半解析（semianalytical）计算，对Co₃Sn₂S₂的内在低能旋光光学响应进行系统研究。

Result: 实验结果提供了一种稳健且透明的解释，将所观测的磁光现象归因于韦尔节点在动量空间中分离所产生内在旋光性（intrinsic gyrotropy）；实验与理论之间的定量对比进一步对材料参数施加了严格约束。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了Co₃Sn₂S₂低能区实验研究的空白，给出了韦尔半金属磁光现象的内在旋光性解释，并为材料参数的确定提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Time-reversal-symmetry-broken Weyl semimetals are known to have at least two nodes in their electronic band structure, separated in momentum space and acting as sources and sinks of Berry curvature. This gives rise to a transverse Hall conductivity, known as the anomalous Hall effect (AHE), which, in the simplest two-node picture, is proportional to the momentum-space separation between the nodes in the zero frequency limit. In the recently discovered Weyl semimetal $\mathrm{Co_3Sn_2S_2}$, a giant AHE has been observed. However, experimental investigations in the low-energy regime, which directly probe quasiparticle excitations near the Weyl nodes, remain limited. Here, we present a systematic study of the intrinsic low-energy gyrotropic optical response of $\mathrm{Co_3Sn_2S_2}$ using terahertz spectroscopy combined with semianalytical calculations based on a physically intuitive effective model. Our results provide a robust and transparent explanation of the observed magnetooptical phenomena in terms of intrinsic gyrotropy arising from momentum-space separation of the Weyl nodes. Furthermore, quantitative comparison between experiment and theory places stringent constraints on the material parameters.

</details>


### [182] [Occupation-Driven Josephson Diode in a Symmetric Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27213)
*Jianxiong Zhai,Zelei Zhang,Jiawei Yan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约瑟夫森二极管（Josephson diode）新机制：非互易性并非源于常规的非对称安德烈夫谱（asymmetric Andreev spectrum），而是由所连接储库工程化（reservoir engineering）产生的非平衡占据态。通过在双量子点（double-quantum-dot）结中实现该机制，位相纹理非局域储库作为量子芝诺选择器（quantum Zeno selector），快速耗散冻结亮态（bright state），保留暗安德烈夫通道（dark Andreev channel），从而产生约瑟夫森二极管效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约瑟夫森二极管通常依赖非对称安德烈夫谱来实现非互易输运，本文旨在探索一种全新的物理机制：通过储库工程化调控非平衡占据态，而非直接设计安德烈夫能谱，来实现约瑟夫森二极管效应，从而为器件设计提供新自由度。

Method: 提出在双量子点结中利用位相纹理的非局域储库作为量子芝诺选择器：快速耗散冻结与跳跃算符L直接耦合的亮态，同时保留与之正交的暗安德烈夫通道。在无穷能隙极限下，稳态电流可分解为I_ss=I_A P_γ，其中P_γ的位相不对称性可独立产生二极管效应。还利用凯尔迪什-林德布拉德（Keldysh-Lindblad）计算验证有限超导能隙和耗散条件下的鲁棒性，并引入局部库仑排斥（Coulomb repulsion）通过暗对共振（dark-pair resonance）驱动系统趋向理想二极管。

Result: 在无穷能隙极限下，即使安德烈夫电流I_A严格互易，仅凭P_γ的位相不对称性即可产生约瑟夫森二极管效应；局部库仑排斥可驱动系统进入接近理想的二极管工作区；Keldysh-Lindblad计算表明该效应在有限超导能隙和耗散存在时依然稳健。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过储库工程化而非能谱对称性破缺来实现约瑟夫森二极管的可行途径，暗态与亮态的选择性耗散调控为超导非互易器件提供了新的设计原理。

Abstract: We propose a Josephson diode mechanism in which nonreciprocity arises not from a conventional asymmetric Andreev spectrum but from nonequilibrium occupation of the current-carrying states engineered by attached reservoirs. We realize this mechanism in a double-quantum-dot junction, where a phase-textured nonlocal reservoir acts as a quantum Zeno selector: rapid dissipation freezes out the bright state directly coupled to the jump operator $L$, while preserving an orthogonal dark Andreev channel whose supercurrent remains comparable to that of the lossless junction. In the infinite-gap limit, the steady-state current factorizes as $I_{\rm ss}=I_A P_γ$, so that even when the Andreev current $I_A$ is strictly reciprocal, the phase asymmetry of $P_γ$ alone can produce a Josephson diode effect through reservoir engineering. We further show that local Coulomb repulsion can drive the system toward a nearly ideal diode regime via a dark-pair resonance. Using Keldysh-Lindblad calculations, we demonstrate that our results remain robust for realistic junctions with a finite superconducting gap and dissipation.

</details>


### [183] [Mobility Enhancement in Si/SiGe Quantum Well Enabled by a Buried Si Layer Trapping Oxygen Impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27377)
*Felix Reichmann,Alberto Mistroni,Fabian Fidorra,Giovanni Capellini,Yuji Yamamoto,Marco Lisker,Marvin H. Zoellner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过在Si/SiGe异质结构的下层SiGe势垒中引入一层薄的电学惰性埋藏Si层，实现了氧背景杂质浓度约五倍的降低和电子迁移率的提升，且不改变量子阱有源区结构及低密度无序景观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未掺杂Si/SiGe场效应异质结构中的无序（disorder）问题仍是可扩展量子器件（尤其电子自旋量子比特）的重要材料挑战。虽然背景杂质（如氧）已被确认会限制迁移率，但抑制其掺入的实用异质结构设计策略仍缺乏探索，其对不同输运机制的影响也尚未完全明确。

Method: 在200 mm Si(100)衬底上通过减压化学气相沉积（RP-CVD）生长Si/SiGe量子阱异质结构，并在下层SiGe势垒中引入薄的电学惰性埋藏Si层。采用二次离子质谱（SIMS）表征氧背景浓度，并通过密度和温度依赖的磁输运测量评估电子迁移率、渗流密度（percolation density）及密度依赖的迁移率标度行为。

Result: 埋藏Si层可重复地将后续生长的SiGe中氧背景降低约五倍，且不改变活性量子阱区域。氧的减少提升了电子迁移率，但渗流密度和密度依赖迁移率标度基本不变。在0.3 K冷却后，高氧与低氧器件表现出相似的密度依赖分数迁移率增强，表明氧背景减少主要改善动量弛豫（momentum relaxation），而非显著改变主导低密度无序景观。

Conclusion: 埋藏Si层是一种简单且与工艺兼容的异质结构设计元素，可用于降低氧掺入并改善200 mm CVD生长的Si/SiGe量子器件材料的输运性能。

Abstract: Reducing disorder in undoped Si/SiGe field-effect heterostructures remains an important materials challenge for scalable quantum devices, particularly electron spin qubits. Background impurities such as oxygen have been identified as mobility-limiting, yet practical heterostructure-design strategies for suppressing their incorporation remain underexplored, and their influence across different transport regimes is not fully established. Here, we demonstrate a simple route to oxygen reduction and mobility enhancement in Si/SiGe quantum-well heterostructures grown by reduced-pressure chemical vapor deposition (RP-CVD) on 200 mm Si(100) substrates through the introduction of a thin, electrically passive buried Si layer within the lower SiGe barrier. Secondary-ion mass spectrometry shows that the buried Si layer reproducibly reduces the oxygen background in the subsequently grown SiGe by approximately a factor of five, without modifying the active quantum-well region. Density- and temperature-dependent magnetotransport measurements further show that this reduction increases the electron mobility, while leaving the percolation density and density-dependent mobility scaling largely unchanged. Upon cooling to 0.3 K, both high- and low-oxygen devices exhibit similar density-dependent fractional mobility enhancements, indicating that the reduced oxygen background improves momentum relaxation without substantially altering the dominant low-density disorder landscape. These results establish the buried Si layer as a straightforward and process-compatible heterostructure-design element for reducing oxygen incorporation and improving transport in 200 mm CVD-grown Si/SiGe quantum-device materials.

</details>


### [184] [Scalable, Simple, and Versatile Encapsulation of 2D Materials and Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27383)
*Gabriel Natale,Uma Chirkova,Flávio Henriques Feres,Ran Jing,Michael Geiwitz,Wenyao Liu,Emma Low,Josh Leeman,Kyung-Mo Kim,Leslie M. Schoop,Mohamed Shehabeldin,Qiong Ma,Michael A. Susner,Pijush Bhattacharya,Genda Gu,Katherine Lee,James Hone,Mengkun Liu,Kenneth S. Burch*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文演示了电子束（e-beam）蒸发的氧化铝（AlO_x）薄膜可同时用作空气敏感二维材料的封装层和直接器件制造的平台，克服了封装与简便器件制造之间的长期权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气敏感二维材料在器件集成中面临根本挑战，传统封装方法对较厚薄层常失效且使制造过程复杂化。

Method: 采用电子束蒸发沉积氧化铝（AlO_x）作为封装层，无需堆叠程序且支持免后封装刻蚀的直接接触；系统研究不同基底和薄层厚度下的封装优化。

Result: 在稀土三碲化物（RTe_3）、半金属WTe_2和超导FeTe_xSe_{1-x}等多种材料上验证AlO_x有效抑制氧化并保持本征光电性能；超薄AlO_x保留WTe_2等离激元响应并维持FeTe_xSe_{1-x}的超导性能。

Conclusion: 该方法可扩展至更厚薄层及全器件或晶圆尺度覆盖，与转移法相比无需堆叠，突破了空气敏感量子材料中封装与直接器件制造之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Air-sensitive 2D materials present a fundamental challenge for device integration. Encapsulation is often required to preserve intrinsic properties, yet conventional protection strategies often fail for thicker layers and complicate fabrication. Here, we demonstrate that electron-beam (e-beam) evaporated aluminum oxide ($\mathrm{AlO}_x$) serves as both an effective encapsulation layer and a platform for direct device fabrication. Unlike transfer-based approaches, this scalable method is compatible with thicker flakes and full device or wafer coverage. It requires no stacking procedures and enables contacts without post-encapsulation etching. Using rare-earth tritellurides ($\mathrm{RTe}_3$, R = La, Er), semimetallic $\mathrm{WTe}_2$, and superconducting $\mathrm{FeTe}_x\mathrm{Se}_{1-x}$, we show that $\mathrm{AlO}_x$ suppresses oxidation and preserves intrinsic optical and electronic properties. We establish substrate-dependent optimization of encapsulation across a range of flake thicknesses, demonstrate that ultrathin $\mathrm{AlO}_x$ preserves $\mathrm{WTe}_2$'s plasmonic response and maintains superconducting performance in $\mathrm{FeTe}_x\mathrm{Se}_{1-x}$. Thus we overcome the longstanding tradeoff between encapsulation and straightforward device fabrication in fragile quantum materials.

</details>


### [185] [Trapping $e/4$ quasiparticles in bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27444)
*Mario Di Luca,Emily Hajigeorgiou,Ning Ma,Alexandra Waldherr,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Mitali Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本研究利用双层石墨烯（bilayer graphene）中栅极定义的量子点势垒（antidot），测量了偶分母分数量子霍尔（even-denominator fractional quantum Hall, FQH）态中准粒子的电荷。在$ν=-5/2$、$-1/2$和$3/2$态观测到局域电荷$e/4$，在空穴共轭态$ν=2/3$观测到$e/3$，并揭示了量子点势垒束缚态与扩展边缘态耦合增强时从最小激发周期到约两倍周期两个输运区间的交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量偶分母分数量子霍尔态所承载的准粒子电荷，是识别其基态拓扑性质的关键步骤，对区分可能的非阿贝尔（non-Abelian）基态候选理论至关重要。

Method: 在双层石墨烯中制备栅极定义的量子点势垒，并额外添加一个仅控制势垒电势的栅极，用以测量环绕势垒的偶分母FQH态内捕获准粒子的电荷。通过调节势垒束缚态与扩展边缘态之间的耦合强度，观察输运行为的变化。

Result: 在$ν=-5/2$、$-1/2$和$3/2$处观测到局域电荷$e/4$，与领先偶分母基态候选理论所预期的最小激发一致；在空穴共轭态$ν=2/3$处观测到$e/3$。增强耦合可驱动最小激发栅压周期与约两倍周期两个输运区间之间的交叉。

Conclusion: 结合此前对子态（daughter states）的观测结果，表明双层石墨烯中的偶分母FQH态与非阿贝尔基态兼容，且其准粒子可局域在量子霍尔势垒周围，这为拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computation）提供了必要条件。

Abstract: Measuring the charge of the quasiparticles hosted by even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states is essential to identify the topology of their ground state. Here, we use a gate-defined antidot in bilayer graphene, with an additional gate to control only the antidot potential, to measure the charge of the quasiparticles trapped around it in even-denominator FQH states. We observe a localized charge of $e/4$ at $ν=-5/2$, $-1/2$, and $3/2$, consistent with the minimal excitation expected for leading candidate even-denominator ground states, and $e/3$ at the hole-conjugate state $ν=2/3$. We further show that increasing the coupling between the antidot-bound states and extended edge states drives a crossover between two regimes, characterized by the minimal-excitation gate-voltage period and approximately twice that period, respectively. We discuss two possible explanations for this crossover: quasiparticle bunching and a crossover between distinct antidot transport regimes. Our results, together with previous observations of the daughter states, show that the even-denominator FQH states in bilayer graphene are compatible with a non-Abelian ground state, and that their quasiparticles can be localized around a quantum Hall antidot, a necessary ingredient for topological quantum computation.

</details>


### [186] [Band's Geometry Origin of Quantum Spin Transport Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27445)
*Elena Derunova,Mazhar N. Ali*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种基于电子能带与费米面（Fermi surface）局部几何结构的自旋依赖输运（spin-dependent transport）的几何学描述，揭示了双曲区域对自旋电流的几何贡献，并通过时间反演对称性将自旋算符代数与能带切空间的外代数联系起来，最终确立了费米面几何与内禀自旋输运之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋输运理论通常不直接利用能带结构的几何信息，而作者希望从能带与费米面的局部几何结构出发，发展一种统一的几何框架来描述自旋相关的输运现象。

Method: 对于准二维系统，作者分析了等能面（constant-energy surface）中的双曲区域对费米速度（Fermi velocity）的几何贡献，并证明该贡献与电子自旋自然耦合，从而产生自旋电流响应。进一步，在时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）条件下，将自旋算符代数与能带切空间（tangent space）的外代数（exterior algebra）建立联系；随后引入辛几何（symplectic description）描述费米面上自旋分离的输运，并通过接触几何（contact geometry）将其推广至三维能带流形。

Result: 作者展示了自旋算符代数与能带切空间外代数之间的几何对应关系，并利用辛几何与接触几何推广了自旋分离输运的描述框架，从而建立了费米面几何结构与内禀自旋输运（intrinsic spin transport）之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 该研究为自旋输运提供了一种全新的几何视角，表明费米面的几何属性（尤其是双曲区域与接触结构）可以直接决定内禀自旋电流行为，为未来在更高维度或更复杂能带结构下的自旋输运研究提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We develop a geometric description of spin-dependent transport based on the local geometric structure of electronic bands and the Fermi surfaces. For quasi-two-dimensional systems, we show that hyperbolic regions of constant-energy surfaces generate a geometrical contribution to the Fermi velocity that couples naturally to electron spin and produces a spin-current response. We further show that, in the presence of time-reversal symmetry, the algebra of spin operators can be related to the exterior algebra of the band's tangent space, providing an additional geometric interpretation of spin in momentum space. This framework motivates a symplectic description of spin-separated transport on Fermi surfaces and its extension to three-dimensional band manifolds through contact geometry. Our results establish a direct connection between Fermi-surface geometry and intrinsic spin transport.

</details>


### [187] [Distinguishing Quantum Capacitance Signatures of a Topological Majorana Wire from a Normal Wire Segment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27452)
*Binayyak Bhusan Roy,Jay Deep Sau,Sumanta Tewari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过量子点干涉仪（quantum-dot interferometer）探测的量子电容（quantum capacitance）响应的实验判据，用以区分拓扑超导（topological superconducting, TS）线中的马约拉纳零模（Majorana zero modes, MZMs）与无隙（gapless, Δ=0）普通线中由阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆（Aharonov-Bohm）干涉产生的类马约拉纳信号。关键区分在于类马约拉纳信号在参数空间中缺乏持久性，而真实MZM应答则在量子点势和线化学势的有限范围内稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来研究表明，无隙普通线可通过阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆干涉再现类马约拉纳（Majorana-like）的磁通依赖量子电容振荡，这给MZM的实验确认带来了歧义。本文的动机在于提出一种能够实验区分真实MZM信号与无隙线类MZM赝象的判据。

Method: 作者基于对两种体系中宇称依赖（parity-dependent）信号的能量共振结构分析进行区分：在无隙普通线中，宇称相关信号来源于分立能量共振（缺乏通用的零能态）；而在拓扑线中，偶宇称与奇宇称的一对低能级在能量上近似简并（nearly degenerate），因而两条宇称分支落在量子点势的同一较宽共振区域内。作者进一步考察了信号在量子点势和线化学势（chemical potential）独立变化下的持续性。

Result: 结果显示，实验观测到的量子电容响应不应通过单点参数空间中的特定磁通迹线来判定，而应通过其在有限范围的独立可控参数（包括量子点势）上的持续性来区分。这一参数空间稳定性可直接排除无隙普通线作为类马约拉纳响应来源的可能性。

Conclusion: 实验结果可通过参数空间中的持久性判定来区分真实的MZM响应与无隙线的类MZM赝象，为拓扑超导线的MZM实验确认提供了更稳健的判据。

Abstract: Majorana zero modes (MZMs) are spatially separated, near-zero-energy excitations expected at the ends of a topological superconducting (TS) wire. A quantum-dot interferometer can be used to probe the quantum capacitance of the TS wire, and the location and magnitude of the capacitance resonances provide information about the MZMs, while their magnetic flux dependence probes coherent coupling to the two Majorana modes. It has been recently shown that, a gapless (i.e., $Δ=0$) wire segment can also exhibit flux-dependent quantum capacitance oscillations through Aharonov-Bohm interference and, with suitable tuning, can reproduce a Majorana-like response. Here we show that the two mechanisms can be distinguished experimentally. In the gapless normal wire segment, the two parity-dependent signals originate from separate energy resonances corresponding to the lack of generic zero-energy states. By contrast, in the topological wire, the pair of low energy levels with even and odd parity are nearly degenerate in energy, and therefore, the two parity branches remain within the same broader resonance region in the quantum-dot potential, and persist under independent variations of the dot potential and wire chemical potential. Our results show that the experimentally observed quantum capacitance response is distinguished not by a particular flux trace at one optimized point in parameter space, but by its persistence over a finite range of independently controlled parameters, including the quantum-dot potential. This parameter space stability provides a direct means of ruling out the gapless normal wire segment as the origin of the observed Majorana-like response.

</details>

# nlin.CD [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Exploring continuous beta-ensembles: A Python implementation for random matrix spectral statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27224)
*Dorin Weissman*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的Python包，用于采样随机矩阵理论中的高斯型、圆形和拉盖尔型β系综（β-ensembles），并提供了从最近邻间距到谱型因子（spectral form factor）在内的系列谱统计工具，可应用于通用的谱数据并与β系综预测进行比较和拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵理论中的β系综在量子混沌等领域具有广泛应用，但缺乏一个通用、高效且开源的软件工具来生成一般β>0的随机谱并进行谱统计分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 该包实现了Dumitriu-Edelman和Killip-Nenciu构造方法，支持高效生成一般β>0值下的随机谱；同时提供从标准最近邻间距和间距比到非相邻k间距以及谱型因子的谱统计工具，这些工具可适用于通用的谱数据。

Result: 数值实验包括三方面：将β作为间距比统计中的连续拟合参数进行分析；检验k间距比分布猜想（k-spacing ratio distributions）的数值证据；以及对一般β值的谱型因子进行研究。

Conclusion: 该包为随机矩阵β系综的谱生成与分析提供了完整的开源解决方案，其工具链可帮助用户将通用谱数据与β系综预测进行对比或拟合，在量子混沌等应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We present an open-source Python package for sampling the Gaussian, Circular, and Laguerre $β$-ensembles of random matrix theory. The package implements the Dumitriu-Edelman and Killip-Nenciu constructions, allowing efficient generation of random spectra for general $β> 0$. In addition to spectrum generation, it includes tools for the analysis of spectral statistics, from standard nearest-neighbor spacings and spacing ratios to non-adjacent $k$-spacings and the spectral form factor. These tools can be applied to generic spectral data, allowing users to compare them with and fit them to $β$-ensemble predictions. In this note, we review the $β$-ensembles, describe the package interface, and illustrate its use through several numerical experiments motivated by applications to quantum chaos. Our numerical results include an analysis of $β$ as a continuous fitting parameter in spacing ratio statistics, an examination of the numerical evidence for the conjectured $k$-spacing ratio distributions, and a study of the spectral form factor for general values of $β$.

</details>
